中文摘要翻译: 手语翻译（SLT）将连续的手语视频转换为 spoken language text。无词汇表方法利用预训练的视觉编码器和语言模型，但仅依赖翻译监督信号进行隐式的跨模态对齐。本文提出 VTaMo 框架，引入三个层次的显式多粒度对齐：（1）局部对齐，通过基于熵正则化最优传输和可学习空标记，实现细粒度的帧到标记对应；（2）全局对齐，通过可学习的正交变换，利用推土机距离校准嵌入空间的几何结构；（3）位置对齐对比学习，用于判别性的标记级表示。在 Phoenix-2014T、CSL-Daily、How2Sign 和 OpenASL 上的实验表明，该方法取得一致的最先进性能，消融实验确认了每个组件的互补贡献。代码已开源：https://github.com/junyi2005/vtamo。

</details>


### [30] [IB-Flow: Information Bottleneck-Guided CFG Distillation for Few-Step Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09133)
*Yiting Wang,Jingyi Zhang,Wenhu Zhang,Ke Chao,Yves Liang,Kun Cheng,Kang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对少步扩散蒸馏中CFG轨迹的粗粒度盲注入问题，提出基于信息瓶颈理论的自适应双轨框架，实现实例感知的注入目标选择和熵感知的注入强度调度，在2步生成设置下达到SOTA保真度并根除过条件伪影。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有少步CFG蒸馏方法采用全局静态的引导强度和盲目采样教师时间步，忽略了图像生成是熵逐步降低的动态演化过程，导致性能受限并产生严重的过条件伪影。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈理论将蒸馏过程建模为动态互信息博弈，提出双轨自适应框架：(1) 实例感知选择机制，将KL散度约束转化为基于局部向量场范数的零开销闭式解以确定注入目标；(2) 熵感知调度策略，随信噪比动态衰减，初始阶段施加最大推力进行结构锚定，然后平滑回归自然流形细化微观细节。

Result: 实验证明该方法从根本上消除了过条件伪影，突破了性能上限，在极严格的2步生成配置下达到了SOTA生成保真度。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应双轨框架通过信息论视角重新审视蒸馏过程，克服了传统盲注入范式的根本缺陷，为少步扩散模型的高效压缩提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: While large-scale text-to-image generative models have achieved unprecedented visual performance, their inherent reliance on multi-step iterative solvers incurs severe inference latency. Few-step distillation targeting the Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) trajectory has emerged as the prevalent dual-dimensional compression paradigm. However, existing frameworks remain subjugated by a coarse-grained blind injection paradigm that perpetually enforces a globally static guidance strength while indiscriminately sampling the supervisor timestep. This state-agnostic design completely disregards the intrinsic nature of image generation as a dynamic evolutionary process characterized by progressive entropy reduction, which not only restricts the performance boundary of few-step compression but also precipitates severe CFG over-conditioning artifacts. To transcend these limitations, we re-examine the distillation procedure through the theoretical lens of Information Theory, formally modeling it as a dynamic mutual information game constrained by the Information Bottleneck (IB) principle. Specifically, we dismantle traditional blind assumptions via a dual-track adaptive framework. To determine the injection target, we propose an instance-aware selection mechanism that transmutes the intractable KL divergence constraint into a zero-overhead closed-form solution predicated on the local vector field norm. To regulate the injection strength, we introduce an entropy-aware schedule that dynamically decays alongside the SNR, applying maximal thrust for initial structural anchoring before smoothly reverting to the natural manifold to refine micro-details. Extensive empirical evaluations corroborate that our framework fundamentally eradicates over-conditioning artifacts, shattering the performance ceiling to achieve SOTA generative fidelity under extremely stringent 2-step configurations.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管大规模文本到图像生成模型在视觉表现上取得了前所未有的成就，但其本质上依赖多步迭代求解器导致严重的推理延迟。针对分类器无引导（CFG）轨迹的少步蒸馏已成为主流的双维压缩范式。然而，现有框架仍受困于粗粒度的盲注入范式——持续施加全局静态的引导强度，同时不加区分地采样教师时间步。这种状态无关的设计完全忽视了图像生成作为渐进熵减动态演化过程的内在本质，不仅限制了少步压缩的性能边界，还引发了严重的CFG过条件伪影。为突破这些限制，我们从信息论的理论视角重新审视蒸馏过程，将其形式化为受信息瓶颈（IB）约束的动态互信息博弈。具体而言，我们通过双轨自适应框架拆解了传统的盲假设。在注入目标确定方面，提出实例感知选择机制，将难以处理的KL散度约束转化为基于局部向量场范数的零开销闭式解。在注入强度调节方面，引入熵感知调度策略，该策略随信噪比动态衰减，在初始阶段施加最大推力进行结构锚定，随后平滑回归自然流形以细化微观细节。大量实证评估证实，我们的框架从根本上消除了过条件伪影，突破了性能上限，在极为严苛的2步配置下达到了最先进的生成保真度。

</details>


### [31] [Super-Generalist: Towards Comprehensive and Accurate Medical Image Understanding via Generalist-Specialist Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09135)
*Shaoteng Zhang,Weiwei Cao,Wanxing Chang,Yutong Xie,Kai Cao,Zaiyi Liu,Yu Shi,Tingbo Liang,Qi Zhang,Ling Zhang,Yong Xia,Jianpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SuG提出了一种“超级通才”框架，通过将通才视觉-语言学习与专科目标（如多专家分割先验和文本条件视觉注意力校准）相结合，在医学影像诊断中同时实现了广泛泛化能力和专科级的病灶感知与空间可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学影像模型存在两难：视觉-语言通才模型任务覆盖广、零样本能力强，但缺乏细粒度解剖和病灶感知，诊断可靠性和空间可解释性不足；而监督专科模型在特定任务上性能强，但跨疾病和跨解剖结构的泛化能力差。为此，作者希望构建一个兼具通才泛化能力和专科诊断精度的统一框架。

Method: SuG框架的核心包括：(1) 专科增强的视觉-语言对齐，融合来自多个分割专家（解剖分割器、类别特异性病灶分割器、类别无关病灶分割器）的空间先验；(2) 利用病灶掩模作为空间先验来校准文本条件视觉注意力，使疾病相关语义聚焦于临床相关区域。

Result: 在胸部与腹部CT基准（CT-RATE、Merlin、MedVL-CT69K及多个内部肿瘤数据集）上，SuG在广泛疾病诊断任务中达到SOTA，在多个关键肿瘤诊断基准上超越专科模型，并展现出强大的病灶定位泛化能力（包括对缺乏类别特异性监督的病灶类型）。

Conclusion: SuG框架成功统一了通才视觉-语言学习与专科目标，在医学影像诊断中实现了广度与精度的兼得，尤其在大范围疾病诊断和病灶定位方面均表现出色。

Original Abstract: Medical images require comprehensive and accurate interpretation to support the diagnosis of diverse clincial conditions. Recent vision-language generalist models offer broad task coverage and promising zero-shot capabilities, yet often lack fine-grained anatomical and lesion awareness for reliable diagnosis and spatial interpretability. In contrast, supervised specialist models achieve strong performance on specific tasks but typically lack generalization across diseases and anatomies. In this work, we present SuG, a Super-Generalist framework that unifies generalist vision-language learning with specialist objectives, enabling both broad generalization and specialist-level diagnostic capability. We perform specialist-enhanced vision-language alignment in SuG by incorporating spatial priors from multiple segmentation experts, including anatomy, class-specific lesion and class-agnostic lesion segmentors that captures lesions beyond anatomies annotated during training. To improve lesion grounding capability, we leverage lesion masks as spatial priors to calibrate text-conditioned visual attention, encouraging disease-related semantics to focus on clinically relevant regions. We evaluate SuG on extensive chest and abdominal CT benchmarks, including CT-RATE, Merlin, MedVL-CT69K, and several in-house tumor datasets. SuG achieves state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of disease diagnosis tasks and surpasses specialist models on several critical tumor diagnosis benchmarks. Furthermore, SuG demonstrates strong lesion grounding capability, including robust generalization to lesion types lacking class-specific supervision.

中文摘要翻译: 医学影像需要全面且准确的解读，以支持多种临床状况的诊断。最近的视觉-语言通才模型提供了广泛的任务覆盖和令人期待的零样本能力，但往往缺乏用于可靠诊断和空间可解释性的细粒度解剖与病灶感知能力。相比之下，监督式专科模型在特定任务上表现强劲，但通常缺乏跨疾病和跨解剖结构的泛化能力。本文提出了SuG，一种“超级通才”框架，它将通才视觉-语言学习与专科目标相统一，从而同时实现广泛泛化和专科级的诊断能力。我们在SuG中通过融合来自多个分割专家的空间先验（包括解剖分割器、类别特异性病灶分割器和类别无关病灶分割器——后者能捕获训练中未标注解剖结构的病灶）来实现专科增强的视觉-语言对齐。为了提升病灶定位能力，我们利用病灶掩模作为空间先验来校准文本条件视觉注意力，促使疾病相关语义聚焦于临床相关区域。我们在广泛的胸部与腹部CT基准（包括CT-RATE、Merlin、MedVL-CT69K及多个内部肿瘤数据集）上对SuG进行评估。SuG在广泛疾病诊断任务上取得了最先进性能，并在多个关键肿瘤诊断基准上超越了专科模型。此外，SuG展示了强大的病灶定位能力，包括对缺乏类别特异性监督的病灶类型的鲁棒泛化。

</details>


### [32] [Weaving Light and Time: Unified Harmonic-Geometric Representation Learning for Dense RGB-Event Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09143)
*Chenxu Peng,Chongtian zhou,Dicheng Liu,Bo-Wen Yin,Yimian Dai,Xialei Liu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evita是首个专为密集RGB-事件（RGB-Event）联合解析设计的统一骨干网络，通过嵌入几何视差矫正、谐波谱共振和瞬态全局路由等内在共学习模块，以及构建N-ImageNetV2数据集和随机事件表示混合预训练协议，在多个基准上取得了最先进性能，实现了精度与延迟的优越权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RGB-Event融合方法要么采用解耦双编码器导致计算开销翻倍，要么采用通用统一设计但无法解决密集强度网格与稀疏运动尖峰之间的几何视差和跨频谱混叠问题，亟需一个专门针对密集RGB-Event解析的统一骨干网络。

Method: 提出Evita统一骨干网络，在每个编码器层嵌入三个内在共学习模块：几何视差矫正模块实现自适应空间对齐、谐波谱共振模块在复数频域进行纹理迁移、瞬态全局路由模块实现事件驱动的非对称注意力；同时构建N-ImageNetV2数据集和随机事件表示混合预训练协议，使网络能无缝兼容任意事件格式。

Result: 在DELIVER、DDD17和DSEC基准上的大量评估表明，Evita取得了新的最先进指标，同时在实时多模态感知中实现了优越的精度-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: Evita通过专门设计的统一骨干网络和创新的共学习模块，有效解决了RGB-Event融合中的几何视差和跨频谱混叠问题，为多模态感知提供了高效且精准的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Fusing standard RGB frames with asynchronous event streams has emerged as a definitive paradigm for robust perception in degraded environments. Although unified backbones have recently gained traction in multi-modal vision, adapting them to the RGB-Event domain remains fundamentally challenging. Existing architectures either resort to decoupled dual encoders that double computational overhead, or adopt generic unified designs that fail to resolve implicit geometric parallax and cross-spectral aliasing under the extreme representational divide between dense intensity grids and sparse kinematic spikes. To transcend these bottlenecks, we present Evita, the first unified backbone specifically engineered for dedicated dense RGB-Event parsing. To achieve profound modal synergy, Evita explicitly embeds a suite of intrinsic co-learning modules directly into every encoder layer. Specifically, it features Geometric Parallax Rectification for adaptive spatial alignment, Harmonic Spectral Resonance for texture transfer exclusively in the complex frequency domain, and Transient Global Routing for event-driven asymmetric attention. To guarantee robust feature extraction against spatial misalignments and decouple representations from specific event encodings, we construct N-ImageNetV2 alongside a stochastic event representation mixing pretraining protocol, empowering the network to seamlessly accommodate arbitrary event formats in downstream tasks. Extensive evaluations across the DELIVER, DDD17, and DSEC benchmarks confirm that Evita establishes new state-of-the-art metrics while delivering a superior accuracy-latency trade-off for real-time multimodal perception.The code are publicly available at: https://github.com/chaineypung/Evita.

中文摘要翻译: 将标准RGB帧与异步事件流融合已成为在退化环境中进行鲁棒感知的明确范式。尽管统一骨干网络最近在多模态视觉中获得了关注，但将其适配到RGB-事件领域仍然面临根本性挑战。现有架构要么采用解耦双编码器导致计算开销翻倍，要么采用通用统一设计，但无法解决密集强度网格与稀疏运动尖峰之间极端表征差异下的隐含几何视差和跨频谱混叠问题。为突破这些瓶颈，我们提出了Evita，这是首个专门为密集RGB-事件联合解析设计的统一骨干网络。为实现深度的模态协同，Evita在每个编码器层中显式嵌入了一套内在共学习模块。具体而言，它包含用于自适应空间对齐的几何视差矫正模块、仅在复数频域进行纹理迁移的谐波谱共振模块，以及用于事件驱动非对称注意力的瞬态全局路由模块。为确保在空间未对齐情况下的鲁棒特征提取，并将表征与特定事件编码解耦，我们构建了N-ImageNetV2数据集以及一种随机事件表示混合预训练协议，使网络能够无缝兼容下游任务中的任意事件格式。在DELIVER、DDD17和DSEC基准上的大量评估证实，Evita在取得新的最先进指标的同时，为实时多模态感知提供了优越的精度-延迟权衡。代码已公开于：https://github.com/chaineypung/Evita。

</details>


### [33] [What Pixels Are Enough? SEAMS: Sufficiency Saliency via MSE-Preservation Soft-Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09164)
*Magdalena Trędowicz,Łukasz Struski,Arkadiusz Lewicki,Karolina Pachota,Andrzej Grudzień,Mateusz Jagła,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SEAMS，一种基于充分性的显著性方法，通过软掩码优化和可学习预算直接寻找能保持模型行为的紧凑掩码，无需辅助数据集或架构特定机制，可生成不同粒度的解释。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有显著性方法常需辅助数据集或架构特定机制，且缺乏对"哪些区域足以保持模型行为"的直接优化，希望提出一种通用、简洁且无需额外数据集的充分性显著性方法。

Method: 基于软掩码和可学习预算的优化框架，构建由查询图像生成的三种图像复合，通过保持目标输出（如类别概率、CLS嵌入或token表示）来优化紧凑掩码，无需可微top-k松弛或辅助干扰数据集。

Result: 在冻结ViT-S/16和ConvNeXt模型上，同一优化管线通过改变保持目标即可生成目标级、类别条件化和token级解释；生成的掩码紧凑、可解释、对随机初始化稳定，在插入和删除基准上具有竞争力；不同架构往往依赖不同的充分证据但保持相似保真度。

Conclusion: SEAMS是一种简洁、通用且有效的充分性显著性方法，揭示了视觉解释的架构依赖性。

Original Abstract: Saliency maps are most useful when they identify the image regions that are sufficient to preserve a model's behaviour. We introduce SEAMS, a sufficiency-based saliency method that directly optimises a soft mask using a preservation objective. Given a frozen differentiable model output, such as a class probability, CLS embedding, or token representation, SEAMS searches for a compact mask that preserves the selected output. The approach relies on a simple optimisation framework based on soft masks, a learnable budget, and a three-way image composite generated entirely from the query image. As a result, it requires no auxiliary distractor dataset, architecture-specific attribution mechanism, or differentiable top-k relaxation. Experiments with frozen ViT-S/16 and ConvNeXt models show that the same optimisation pipeline can generate object-level, class-conditioned, and token-level explanations by changing only the preserved target. The resulting masks are compact, interpretable, stable across random initialisations, and competitive on insertion and deletion benchmarks. Our results also indicate that different architectures often rely on different sufficient evidence while achieving similar preservation fidelity, highlighting the architecture-dependent nature of visual explanations.

中文摘要翻译: 当显著性图能够识别出足以维持模型行为的图像区域时，它们最为有用。本文提出SEAMS，一种基于充分性的显著性方法，通过保持目标直接优化软掩码。给定冻结的可微分模型输出（如类别概率、CLS嵌入或token表示），SEAMS搜索一个紧凑掩码以保持所选输出。该方法依赖于基于软掩码、可学习预算以及完全由查询图像生成的三路图像复合的简单优化框架。因此，它不需要辅助干扰数据集、架构特定的归因机制或可微的top-k松弛。在冻结的ViT-S/16和ConvNeXt模型上的实验表明，仅通过改变保持目标，同一优化管线即可生成目标级、类别条件化和token级的解释。生成的掩码紧凑、可解释、对随机初始化稳定，并在插入和删除基准测试中具有竞争力。我们的结果还表明，不同架构在实现相似保持保真度的同时，往往依赖于不同的充分证据，这凸显了视觉解释的架构依赖性。

</details>


### [34] [TSR-Ego: Temporally Guided Stereo Refinement Framework for Egocentric 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09169)
*Md Mushfiqur Azam,John Quarles,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TSR-Ego提出了一种基于时间引导的立体框架，用于从头戴式立体相机进行自我中心3D人体姿态估计。该方法通过因果深度可分离时间卷积增强密集立体特征图，并采用单阶段因果立体解码器联合优化时间自注意力、关节自注意力和鱼眼可变形立体交叉注意力，有效解决了鱼眼畸变、自遮挡和关节截断等挑战，在UnrealEgo2和UnrealEgo-RW上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自我中心立体3D人体姿态估计方法严重依赖当前帧的局部立体线索，或仅将时序信息作为辅助的姿态层级上下文使用，导致当当前帧立体线索微弱、被遮挡或模糊时鲁棒性不足。作者希望更深度地将短时运动证据融入特征采样和关节表示构建的全过程。

Method: 1) 采用因果深度可分离时间卷积对密集立体特征图进行时序增强，使过去的视觉证据在可变形交叉注意力之前影响特征空间；2) 设计单阶段因果立体解码器，通过时间自注意力、关节自注意力和鱼眼可变形立体交叉注意力联合优化3D关节查询，利用不断演化的姿态估计生成2D采样参考点；3) 整个框架支持在线推理，无需未来帧。

Result: 在UnrealEgo2和UnrealEgo-RW数据集上达到最先进性能，尤其是在真实世界序列上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: TSR-Ego通过将短时运动证据与投影引导的特征采样相结合，在时序上深度融合了运动上下文到立体特征和关节表示中，有效提升了在困难场景下的鲁棒性，验证了时序引导在自我中心立体姿态估计中的关键作用。

Original Abstract: Egocentric 3D human pose estimation from head-mounted stereo cameras is challenging due to fisheye distortion, severe self-occlusion, and frequent truncation of body joints outside the camera field of view. Recent stereo egocentric methods have improved performance through heatmap lifting, stereo correspondence, and transformer-based refinement, but they often rely heavily on frame-local evidence or use temporal information only as auxiliary pose-level context. This limits robustness when current-frame stereo cues are weak, occluded, or ambiguous. We propose TSR-Ego, a temporally guided stereo framework that couples short-term motion evidence with projection-guided feature sampling. The model first enriches dense stereo feature maps using a causal depthwise-separable temporal convolution, allowing past visual evidence to influence the feature space before deformable cross-attention. A single-stage causal stereo decoder then refines learned 3D joint queries through temporal self-attention, joint self-attention, and fisheye deformable stereo cross-attention, using the evolving pose estimate to generate 2D sampling references. Unlike methods that apply temporal reasoning mainly after pose prediction, TSR-Ego uses motion context to shape both the sampled stereo features and the joint representations while preserving online inference without future frames. Experiments on UnrealEgo2 and UnrealEgo-RW show state-of-the-art performance, with especially strong gains on real-world sequences.

中文摘要翻译: 从头戴式立体相机进行自我中心3D人体姿态估计因鱼眼畸变、严重自遮挡以及身体关节频繁截断出视野而具有挑战性。最近的立体自我中心方法通过热力图提升、立体对应和基于Transformer的改进提高了性能，但它们通常严重依赖帧局部证据，或仅将时序信息作为辅助的姿态层级上下文使用。这导致当前帧立体线索微弱、被遮挡或模糊时鲁棒性受限。我们提出TSR-Ego，一种时间引导的立体框架，它将短时运动证据与投影引导的特征采样相结合。该模型首先使用因果深度可分离时间卷积增强密集立体特征图，使过去的视觉证据在可变形交叉注意力之前影响特征空间。一个单阶段因果立体解码器随后通过时间自注意力、关节自注意力和鱼眼可变形立体交叉注意力来优化学习到的3D关节查询，利用不断演化的姿态估计生成2D采样参考点。与主要在姿态预测后应用时序推理的方法不同，TSR-Ego在保留无未来帧在线推理的同时，使用运动上下文来塑造采样的立体特征和关节表示。在UnrealEgo2和UnrealEgo-RW上的实验展示了最先进的性能，在真实世界序列上尤其取得了显著提升。

</details>


### [35] [Causally Debiased Latent Action Model for Embodied Action Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09185)
*Yufan Wei,Kun Zhou,Lingjun Mao,Zijun Zhang,Ziming Xu,Ziqiao Xi,Shuang Liang,Ruobing Han,Yuchen Yan,Xinyue Wang,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CD-LAM框架，通过因果去偏方法解决潜在动作模型中动作无关偏差问题，仅需少量微调即可显著提升基于世界模型的动作可控性和下游任务表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型（LAM）仅使用重建目标训练，导致动作相关动态与动作无关视觉因素（如背景、未触碰物体）纠缠不清，这种动作无关偏差阻碍了可控动作条件世界模型（ACWM）的实现。

Method: 提出CD-LAM框架，包含三个高效微调目标：以具身为中心的重建、以动作为中心的对比学习、以及潜在空间校准，共同鼓励具身聚焦、动作感知且校准的非坍塌潜在动作表征。

Result: 在2B和14B规模的ACWM主干上，CD-LAM显著改善潜在动作可控性、下游机器人动作跟随能力、视觉保真度和适应效率，仅需6k步微调，且机器人动作适应更新次数比基线少12倍以上。

Conclusion: 动作无关偏差是可控ACWM的关键障碍，CD-LAM通过因果去偏框架有效解决了该问题，为大规模无标签视频中学习可控世界模型提供了高效路径。

Original Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) aim to simulate future observations conditioned on embodied actions, offering a promising foundation for robot planning, policy evaluation, and data augmentation. However, learning controllable ACWMs requires large-scale action-labeled data, which remains costly to collect in the real world. Latent action models (LAMs) mitigate this bottleneck by inferring latent actions from unlabeled videos, but existing LAMs are typically trained with reconstruction-only objectives and therefore entangle action-relevant dynamics with action-irrelevant visual factors such as backgrounds and untouched objects. In this work, we identify this action-irrelevant bias as a key obstacle to controllable ACWMs and introduce evaluation metrics to measure latent-action bias, action following, and robustness. We propose CD-LAM, a causally debiased framework for LAM-based ACWMs. CD-LAM introduces three efficient fine-tuning objectives: embodiment-centric reconstruction, action-centric contrastive learning, and latent space calibration, which together encourage embodiment-focused, action-aware, and calibrated non-collapsed latent action representations. Experiments on 2B and 14B ACWM backbones show that CD-LAM substantially improves latent-action controllability, downstream robot-action following, visual fidelity, and adaptation efficiency, requiring only 6k fine-tuning steps and more than 12$\times$ fewer robot-action adaptation updates than the baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 动作条件世界模型（ACWM）旨在模拟基于具身动作的未来观测，为机器人规划、策略评估和数据增强提供了有前景的基础。然而，学习可控的ACWM需要大规模带动作标签的数据，这在现实世界中收集成本高昂。潜在动作模型（LAM）通过从未标注视频中推断潜在动作来缓解这一瓶颈，但现有的LAM通常仅使用重建目标进行训练，因此将动作相关的动态信息与动作无关的视觉因素（如背景和未触碰物体）纠缠在一起。在本工作中，我们将这种动作无关偏差识别为可控ACWM的关键障碍，并引入了评估指标来衡量潜在动作偏差、动作跟随能力和鲁棒性。我们提出了CD-LAM，一种用于基于LAM的ACWM的因果去偏框架。CD-LAM引入了三个高效的微调目标：以具身为中心的重建、以动作为中心的对比学习以及潜在空间校准，这些目标共同鼓励形成具身聚焦、动作感知且校准的非坍塌潜在动作表征。在2B和14B规模的ACWM主干上的实验表明，CD-LAM显著提升了潜在动作可控性、下游机器人动作跟随能力、视觉保真度和适应效率，仅需6k步微调，且机器人动作适应更新次数比基线少12倍以上。

</details>


### [36] [HiHR: Hierarchical Hyperbolic Representation for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09186)
*Qiwei Yang,Pingping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次化双曲表示（HiHR）框架，通过文本引导的多粒度融合和双曲空间中的层次化学习，同时捕获视角不变和视角特有的判别特征，有效解决了跨视角航拍-地面行人再识别中的特征对齐问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AG-ReID方法在异质航拍与地面相机平台间进行直接特征对齐，忽略了视角特有的判别线索，导致性能次优。

Method: 首先基于预训练的视觉-文本编码器提取多粒度特征；然后提出文本引导的多粒度融合（TMF）增强身份特征表示能力；最后引入层次化双曲学习（HHL），在双曲空间中构建包含粗粒度（确保身份可分性与跨视角一致性）和细粒度（保留视角特有判别线索）的层次化特征结构。

Result: 在四个AG-ReID基准数据集上的大量实验证明了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的HiHR框架能够有效聚合视角不变和视角特有的判别特征，显著提升了航拍-地面行人再识别的性能。

Original Abstract: Aerial-Ground Person Re-IDentification (AG-ReID) aims to retrieve the same person across heterogeneous aerial and ground camera platforms. Although great progress has been made, existing methods remain suboptimal due to the direct feature alignment across views, overlooking view-specific cues. To address this issue, we propose a novel Hierarchical Hyperbolic Representation (HiHR) framework for AG-ReID. More specifically, we first extract multi-granularity features based on pre-trained visual-text encoders. Then, we propose a Text-guided Multi-granularity Fusion (TMF) to fuse multi-granularity features and enhance the representation ability of identity features. Furthermore, we introduce the Hierarchical Hyperbolic Learning (HHL) to construct a hierarchical feature structure in a hyperbolic space. This hierarchy includes a coarse level that ensures identity separability and cross-view consistency, and a fine level that preserves view-specific discriminative cues. As a result, our proposed framework can effectively aggregate view-invariant and view-specific discriminative features for AG-ReID. Extensive experiments on four AG-ReID benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. The source code is available at https://github.com/YangQiWei3/HiHR.

中文摘要翻译: 航拍-地面行人再识别（AG-ReID）旨在跨异质航拍与地面相机平台检索同一行人。尽管现有方法已取得较大进展，但由于直接进行跨视角特征对齐而忽略了视角特有的线索，其性能仍非最优。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种新颖的层次化双曲表示（HiHR）框架用于AG-ReID。具体而言，我们首先基于预训练的视觉-文本编码器提取多粒度特征；然后，提出文本引导的多粒度融合（TMF）方法融合多粒度特征，增强身份特征的表示能力；此外，我们引入层次化双曲学习（HHL），在双曲空间中构建层次化特征结构。该层次结构包含一个保证身份可分性和跨视角一致性的粗粒度层级，以及一个保留视角特有判别线索的细粒度层级。由此，我们提出的框架能够有效聚合视角不变和视角特有的判别特征用于AG-ReID。在四个AG-ReID基准数据集上的大量实验证明了我们框架的有效性。源代码已发布在 https://github.com/YangQiWei3/HiHR。

</details>


### [37] [YeTI: You Only Need Two Noisy Images for Real-World sRGB Noise Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09193)
*Jaekyun Ko,Byung Wan Lim,Soomin Lee,Dongjin Kim,Tae Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YeTI是一种仅需两幅同一场景的含噪观测图像即可学习真实sRGB噪声生成框架，通过重构自编码器解耦场景与噪声，并利用一致性训练的条件扩散Transformer建模噪声分布，实现了无需干净图像和相机元数据的噪声合成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实sRGB图像去噪面临传感器非线性噪声和难以获取对齐的干净-含噪图像对的挑战。有监督方法过拟合有限配对数据，自监督方法依赖足够多样的噪声观测。现有方法受限于对干净图像或相机元数据的依赖，亟需一种无需干净图像和元数据即可建模真实噪声的可扩展噪声合成方法。

Method: YeTI包含两个核心组件：(1) 重构自编码器（Reconstruction Autoencoder），将场景结构信息与噪声特征进行解耦分离；(2) 基于一致性目标训练的、单步采样的条件扩散Transformer（Conditional Diffusion Transformer），对解耦后的潜在噪声分布进行建模。推理时，仅需单张含噪输入图像即可生成真实且信号相关的噪声。

Result: 在SIDD数据集上评估噪声生成质量，并在SIDD+、MAI2021、SID等数据集上验证泛化能力。下游去噪实验在DND数据集上表明，使用YeTI合成图像训练的去噪器达到了强竞争的真实世界性能。

Conclusion: YeTI证明了无需干净图像和相机元数据即可生成高质量真实噪声的可行性，为真实场景去噪提供了一种实用且可扩展的噪声合成方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Real-world sRGB image denoising remains challenging due to the nonlinear characteristics of sensor noise and the difficulty of acquiring aligned clean-noisy image pairs. Supervised denoisers often overfit to limited paired datasets, while self-supervised methods still depend on sufficiently diverse noisy observations. These limitations motivate scalable noise synthesis methods that can model real-world noise without clean ground truth or camera metadata. We propose YeTI, a real-world sRGB noise generation framework that learns from only two noisy observations of the same scene. YeTI uses a Reconstruction Autoencoder to disentangle scene structure and noise characteristics, and models the latent noise distribution with a one-step Conditional Diffusion Transformer trained using consistency objectives. Given a single noisy input at inference time, YeTI generates realistic, signal-dependent noise while preserving the underlying scene content. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of YeTI across real-world benchmarks. We evaluate noise generation on SIDD and further assess generalization on SIDD+, MAI2021, and SID, covering smartphone and diverse consumer-camera sensors. Downstream denoising results on DND further show that denoisers trained with YeTI-synthesized images achieve strong real-world performance, highlighting the practical value of clean-image-free and metadata-free noise generation.

中文摘要翻译: 由于传感器噪声的非线性特性以及难以获取对齐的干净-含噪图像对，真实世界sRGB图像去噪仍然面临挑战。有监督去噪器往往过拟合于有限的配对数据集，而自监督方法仍然依赖于足够多样化的噪声观测。这些局限性推动了可扩展噪声合成方法的发展，使其能够在无需干净真值或相机元数据的情况下建模真实噪声。我们提出YeTI，一个仅需同一场景的两幅含噪观测即可学习的真实sRGB噪声生成框架。YeTI使用重构自编码器解耦场景结构与噪声特征，并通过一致性目标训练的单步条件扩散Transformer对潜在噪声分布进行建模。在推理时，给定单张含噪输入，YeTI能够生成真实且信号相关的噪声，同时保留底层场景内容。大量实验证明了YeTI在真实世界基准上的有效性。我们在SIDD上评估噪声生成，并在SIDD+、MAI2021和SID上进一步评估泛化能力，涵盖智能手机和多种消费级相机传感器。DND上的下游去噪结果表明，使用YeTI合成图像训练的去噪器实现了强竞争的真实世界性能，突显了无需干净图像和无需元数据的噪声生成方法的实用价值。

</details>


### [38] [Glob3R: Global Structure-from-Motion with 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09225)
*Junyuan Deng,Heng Li,Kejie Qiu,Lingteng Qiu,Rui Peng,Weichao Shen,Weihao Yuan,Siyu Zhu,Zilong Dong,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Glob3R 提出在冻结的3D基础模型（Pi3X）上附加轻量级密集匹配头，通过预测图像扭曲生成多视图特征轨迹，并结合关键帧滑动窗口关联策略进行全局运动平均与光束法平差优化，实现了比现有前馈模型和经典SfM更鲁棒、更精确的全局三维重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D几何基础模型（如VGGT）虽能前馈式预测相机姿态和3D点云，但结果仍不精确；处理长序列或大规模无序图像集时需要分块处理，导致漂移和不一致性问题。

Method: 1) 在冻结的Pi3X骨干网络上附加一个轻量级密集匹配头，预测参考帧与邻近视图间的图像扭曲；2) 将密集扭曲转换为稀疏但可靠的多视图特征轨迹，提供对应约束；3) 提出基于关键帧的滑动窗口关联策略，跨重叠窗口传播轨迹和相对位姿；4) 执行全局运动平均和光束法平差，优化相机位姿、减小尺度不一致并恢复稠密场景几何。

Result: 在室内、室外、大规模驾驶场景以及无序SfM基准上，Glob3R实现了鲁棒且精确的重建，持续优于前馈基础模型基线和最新的可扩展重建方法，且比经典SfM流程更鲁棒；优化的位姿还带来了更高质量的神经渲染效果。

Conclusion: 将基础模型的前馈先验与全局几何优化相结合，能够显著提升3D重建的精度和鲁棒性，验证了该范式的有效性。

Original Abstract: Recent 3D geometric foundation models, such as VGGT, provide robust feed-forward 3D reconstruction by directly predicting camera poses and 3D scene points from input images. However, their results remain inaccurate, and scaling them to long sequences or large unordered image sets typically requires chunk-wise processing, which can introduce drift and inconsistency. We present Glob3R, a global SfM-style reconstruction built on 3D foundation models. Our key idea is to explicitly optimize feed-forward geometric predictions. To this end, we augment a frozen Pi3X backbone with a lightweight dense matching head that predicts image warps between selected reference frames and neighboring views. These dense warps are converted into sparse but reliable multi-view feature tracks, which provide correspondence constraints for global optimization. We further introduce a keyframe-based sliding-window association strategy that propagates tracks and relative poses across overlapping windows, enabling scalable reconstruction. Finally, we perform global motion averaging and bundle adjustment to refine camera poses, reduce scale inconsistencies, and recover dense scene geometry. Extensive experiments on indoor, outdoor, large-scale driving, and unordered SfM benchmarks demonstrate that Glob3R achieves robust and accurate reconstruction. It consistently improves over feed-forward foundation-model baselines and recent scalable reconstruction methods, while being more robust than classical SfM pipelines. The refined poses also lead to higher-quality neural rendering, validating the benefit of combining foundation-model priors with global geometric optimization. Project page: https://junyuandeng.github.io/Glob3r

中文摘要翻译: 最近的3D几何基础模型，例如VGGT，通过从输入图像直接预测相机姿态和3D场景点，提供了鲁棒的前馈式三维重建。然而，它们的结果仍然不够精确，并且将其扩展到长序列或大规模无序图像集时通常需要分块处理，这会引入漂移和不一致性。我们提出了Glob3R，一种基于3D基础模型构建的全局SfM风格重建方法。我们的核心思想是显式地优化前馈几何预测。为此，我们在冻结的Pi3X骨干网络上附加了一个轻量级的密集匹配头，用于预测选定参考帧与邻近视图之间的图像扭曲。这些密集扭曲被转换为稀疏但可靠的多视图特征轨迹，从而为全局优化提供对应约束。我们进一步引入了一种基于关键帧的滑动窗口关联策略，该策略跨重叠窗口传播轨迹和相对位姿，从而实现可扩展的重建。最后，我们执行全局运动平均和光束法平差，以优化相机位姿、减小尺度不一致性并恢复稠密的场景几何。在室内、室外、大规模驾驶场景以及无序SfM基准上的大量实验表明，Glob3R实现了鲁棒且精确的重建。它持续优于前馈基础模型基线和最新的可扩展重建方法，同时比经典SfM流程更加鲁棒。优化后的位姿还带来了更高质量的神经渲染，验证了将基础模型先验与全局几何优化相结合的优势。项目页面：https://junyuandeng.github.io/Glob3r

</details>


### [39] [AnythingReality: Robust Online Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Open-Vocabulary VR Scene Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09260)
*Timofei Kozlov,Dmitrii Maliukov,Andrey Marchenko,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种集ORB-SLAM3位姿估计、在线3D高斯溅射重建、VR沉浸式探索和语音驱动VLM交互于一体的鲁棒系统，在噪声真实世界数据上显著提升了图像质量和物体识别率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线高斯溅射方法通常假设干净的深度数据或外部位姿输入，无法应对真实世界中噪声数据的挑战，且缺乏沉浸式交互能力。

Method: 将ORB-SLAM3位姿估计与在线高斯重建相结合，构建VR管道实现增量重建的沉浸式探索，并集成语义模块进行语音命令转录、场景描述生成和兴趣点记录。

Result: 在自有数据集上PSNR提升14.5%、SSIM提升8.6%、LPIPS降低14.3%；在TUM-RGBD上PSNR提升11.7%、SSIM提升7.8%、LPIPS降低21.6%；VLM物体识别率达88%。

Conclusion: 该集成架构能够在噪声真实数据中实现鲁棒的在线3D重建、实时VR探索和语音驱动的VLM交互，性能优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: We present a novel integrated architecture for robust online 3D Gaussian splatting, real-time VR exploration, and speech-driven Vision-Language-Model interaction. Unlike methods assuming clean depth or external poses, our system combines ORB-SLAM3-based pose estimation with online Gaussian reconstruction for noisy real-world data. A VR pipeline enables immersive exploration of incremental reconstructions; a semantic module transcribes voice commands, generates scene descriptions, and records points of interest. Against state-of-the-art online Gaussian splatting methods, we improve image quality on our dataset (+14.5% PSNR, +8.6% SSIM, -14.3% LPIPS) and TUM-RGBD (+11.7% PSNR, +7.8% SSIM, -21.6% LPIPS), with comparable or superior frame rates via quality-speed configurations. We achieve an 88% VLM object-recognition rate.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种新颖的集成架构，用于鲁棒的在线三维高斯溅射、实时VR探索和语音驱动的视觉-语言模型交互。与假设存在干净深度或外部位姿的方法不同，我们的系统将基于ORB-SLAM3的位姿估计与在线高斯重建相结合，以应对噪声真实世界数据。一个VR管道支持对增量重建进行沉浸式探索；一个语义模块用于转录语音指令、生成场景描述并记录兴趣点。与最先进的在线高斯溅射方法相比，我们在自有数据集上提升了图像质量（PSNR +14.5%，SSIM +8.6%，LPIPS -14.3%），在TUM-RGBD数据集上也有提升（PSNR +11.7%，SSIM +7.8%，LPIPS -21.6%），并通过质量-速度配置实现了相当或更优的帧率。我们的VLM物体识别率达到88%。

</details>


### [40] [Semantic Hardness Is Not Visual Hardness: Sign-Aware Hard Negative Mining for Sign Language Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09263)
*Junmyeong Lee,Chan Hur,ChangSu Choi,Sukmin Cho,Fitsum Gaim,Eui Jun Hwang,Hoyun Song,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出手语检索在细粒度场景下的性能瓶颈并非源于模型容量，而是由于无效的困难负样本监督。作者提出了一种基于视觉混淆度的符号感知困难负样本挖掘方法（SAN），通过在符号嵌入空间中构建视觉上易混淆的负样本，显著提升了细粒度检索性能，同时保持了粗粒度精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有手语检索模型在视觉相似但语义不同的手语词（细粒度场景）上表现脆弱，作者发现原因并非模型容量不足，而是负样本分布不匹配：现有基于文本的困难负样本挖掘策略无法有效捕捉视觉混淆性导致的歧义，使得语义不同但视觉易混淆的样本很少被作为困难负样本处理。

Method: 提出符号感知困难负样本挖掘（Sign-Aware Hard Negative Mining, SAN），该方法在符号嵌入空间中基于视觉混淆度而非语言相似性来构建困难负样本，从而更有效地进行负样本监督。

Result: 在PHOENIX-2014T数据集上的实验表明，SAN显著提升了细粒度检索性能，同时保持了粗粒度精度。

Conclusion: 本文强调在手语检索中，将负样本监督与视觉混淆性对齐至关重要，SAN方法有效解决了负样本分布不匹配问题。

Original Abstract: Sign Language Retrieval (SLRet) enables efficient access to sign language content but remains fragile in fine-grained scenarios where visually similar signs must be distinguished. We show that this limitation does not stem from model capacity, but from ineffective hard negative supervision. Specifically, we formulate fine-grained retrieval failures as a negative distribution mismatch: semantically distinct yet visually confusable signs are rarely treated as hard negatives, while existing text-based mining strategies fail to capture such visual ambiguity. To address this issue, we propose Sign-Aware Hard Negative Mining (SAN), which constructs hard negatives based on visual confusability in the sign embedding space rather than linguistic similarity. Experiments on PHOENIX-2014T demonstrate that SAN substantially improves fine-grained retrieval performance while preserving coarse-grained accuracy, highlighting the importance of aligning negative supervision with visual ambiguity in sign language retrieval.

中文摘要翻译: 手语检索（SLRet）能够高效访问手语内容，但在需要区分视觉相似手语的细粒度场景中仍然表现脆弱。我们发现这一限制并非源于模型容量，而是由于无效的困难负样本监督。具体而言，我们将细粒度检索失败归因于负样本分布不匹配：语义不同但视觉易混淆的手语很少被作为困难负样本处理，而现有的基于文本的挖掘策略无法捕捉这种视觉歧义。为解决该问题，我们提出符号感知困难负样本挖掘（SAN），该方法在符号嵌入空间中基于视觉混淆度而非语言相似性来构建困难负样本。在PHOENIX-2014T数据集上的实验表明，SAN显著提升了细粒度检索性能，同时保持了粗粒度精度，突出了在手语检索中将负样本监督与视觉混淆性对齐的重要性。

</details>


### [41] [REMIND: RE-Identification with Memory for INDoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09267)
*Pablo Diaz-Pereda,Alejandro Rodriguez-Ramos,David Perez-Saura,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REMIND是一个面向室内通用物体长期多目标重识别的在线跟踪器，结合冻结的DINOv3特征、双库多原型外观记忆、部件/背景级描述符、邻域上下文推理模块和联合匈牙利分配，在室内数据集上达到90.35% IDF1，远超现有基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法存在不足：多目标跟踪优化于行人/车辆的短期关联；行人/车辆重识别缺乏持久记忆机制；视频目标分割依赖被动干扰过滤而非全局身份一致性。室内机器人需要长时间跨度、大视角变化和严重光照变化下对通用物体进行重识别。

Method: REMIND结合：(1) 冻结的DINOv3特征；(2) 双库多原型外观记忆；(3) 部件级和背景级描述符；(4) 利用空间共现的邻域上下文推理模块；(5) 联合匈牙利分配及模糊性感知安全机制。无需相机位姿或深度信息，仅依赖单目RGB图像。

Result: 在室内数据集上达到90.35% IDF1，比视频目标分割基线高近20个点，比强跟踪-检测基线高36个点以上。在ScanNet++上几乎所有设置中取得最高IDF1，且完整处理所有场景（视频目标分割基线在66.9%场景中耗尽GPU显存）。

Conclusion: REMIND证明了结合视觉认知启发的外观熟悉度与空间上下文机制，能够在无需自定位或深度信息的情况下，显著提升室内通用物体的长期多目标重识别性能，且系统已开源。

Original Abstract: Mobile robots operating indoors must re-identify previously observed objects after long temporal gaps, significant viewpoint changes, and severe illumination variations. This remains a challenging problem: multi-object tracking methods are optimized for short-term association of pedestrians and vehicles at video rates, person and vehicle re-identification approaches lack persistent memory mechanisms, and state-of-the-art video object segmentation techniques rely on reactive distractor filtering rather than enforcing global identity consistency.
  To address these limitations, we present REMIND, an online tracker designed for long-term multi-object re-identification of generic indoor objects from monocular RGB imagery, requiring neither camera pose nor depth. Motivated by evidence from visual cognition that humans rely on accumulated appearance familiarity and spatial context rather than explicit self-localization, REMIND combines frozen DINOv3 features with a dual-bank multi-prototype appearance memory, part- and background-level descriptors, a neighbour-context reasoning module exploiting spatial co-occurrence, and joint Hungarian assignment with ambiguity-aware safeguards. On a purpose-built indoor dataset featuring controlled revisits and dense same-class clutter, REMIND reaches 90.35% IDF1, nearly 20 points above a state-of-the-art video object segmentation baseline and more than 36 above a strong tracking-by-detection baseline. On ScanNet++, it attains the highest IDF1 in every setting but one, end-to-end detection over all scenes, where the tracking-by-detection baseline is marginally ahead while REMIND still associates and recovers identities more accurately; it also completes every scene, whereas the video object segmentation baseline exhausts GPU memory on 66.9% under YOLO detections. The complete system, evaluation framework, and dataset are publicly released.

中文摘要翻译: 在室内环境中运行的移动机器人必须在长时间间隔、显著视角变化和严重光照变化下重新识别先前观察到的物体。这仍然是一个具有挑战性的问题：多目标跟踪方法针对行人和车辆在视频速率下的短期关联进行了优化，行人/车辆重识别方法缺乏持久记忆机制，而最先进的视频目标分割技术依赖被动式干扰过滤而非强制执行全局身份一致性。
  为解决这些局限性，我们提出了REMIND，一种在线跟踪器，专用于从单目RGB图像中对室内通用物体进行长期多目标重识别，无需相机位姿或深度信息。受视觉认知证据的启发——人类依赖累积的外观熟悉度和空间上下文而非明确的自定位——REMIND结合了冻结的DINOv3特征、双库多原型外观记忆、部件级和背景级描述符、利用空间共现的邻域上下文推理模块，以及带有模糊性感知安全机制的联合匈牙利分配。在一个专门构建的、具有受控重访和密集同类杂物的室内数据集上，REMIND达到了90.35%的IDF1，比最先进的视频目标分割基线高出近20个百分点，比强跟踪-检测基线高出36个百分点以上。在ScanNet++上，REMIND在除一种设置外的所有设置中均取得了最高的IDF1——即所有场景的端到端检测设置，在该设置下跟踪-检测基线略占优势，而REMIND仍能更准确地关联和恢复身份；此外，REMIND完整处理了每一个场景，而视频目标分割基线在使用YOLO检测时，在66.9%的场景中耗尽了GPU显存。完整的系统、评估框架和数据集均已公开发布。

</details>


### [42] [Rethinking Monocular Depth Embedding for Generalized Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09284)
*Libo Lin,Shuangli Du,Minghua Zhao,Zhenzhen You,Shun Lv,Yiguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的单目深度嵌入方法用于立体匹配，通过减少分支耦合、构建软约束、融合单目深度与RGB图像、引入单目深度梯度引导视差更新，以及边缘感知损失函数，在多个标准基准上达到SOTA性能，兼具高精度与强泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单目方法缺乏几何精度，立体方法在无纹理和遮挡区域表现不佳。现有结合两者优势的方法面临对齐不稳定、单目线索不可靠导致性能退化的问题。本文旨在重新设计单目深度嵌入方式，避免捷径学习并提高对单目深度误差的容忍度。

Method: (1) 减少分支耦合而非扩展网络宽度，防止捷径学习；(2) 从单目深度构建软约束而非硬约束，提高对单目深度误差的鲁棒性；(3) 将单目深度图与RGB图像融合，增强深度边界感知并抑制匹配歧义；(4) 利用融合图像进行特征提取，使上下文特征编码全局几何信息；(5) 引入单目深度梯度特征指导GRU迭代中的视差更新，帮助逃离局部震荡；(6) 提出边缘置信度估计方法和边缘感知损失函数，解决数据增强导致的监督视差边界模糊问题。

Result: 在多个标准基准上达到SOTA性能，在提升精度的同时展现出优秀的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文重新思考了单目深度嵌入方式，通过减少分支耦合、构建软约束、融合单目深度信息以及边缘感知损失，有效结合了单目与立体方法的优势，在立体匹配任务上实现了精度与泛化性的双重提升。

Original Abstract: Generally, monocular methods capture rich contextual priors but lack geometric precision, whereas stereo methods are geometrically accurate yet struggle in textureless and occluded regions. Several approaches attempt to combine their strengths to enhance the generalization of stereo matching (SM) by aligning monocular depth with stereo information. However, establishing a stable and generalizable alignment is challenging, and unreliable monocular cues can substantially degrade performance. This paper rethinks monocular depth embedding. First, to prevent shortcut learning, we reduce branch coupling instead of expanding network width. Second, we construct soft constraints instead of hard ones from monocular depth to improve tolerance to monocular depth errors. Based on the principles, we integrate monocular information into both feature extraction and GRU iterations. Specifically, the monocular depth map is fused with the RGB image to sharpen depth boundary perception and suppress matching ambiguities. The fused image is then used for feature extraction, allowing the contextual features to encode global geometric information. Furthermore, the monocular depth gradient feature is employed to guide disparity updates, helping to escape local oscillations. Finally, to address the boundary blurring of supervised disparity caused by data augmentation, we propose an edge confidence estimation method and an edge-aware loss function. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on multiple standard benchmarks, demonstrating excellent generalization while improving accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/linliboabc-maker/stereo-matching-digital.

中文摘要翻译: 通常，单目方法能够捕获丰富的上下文先验但缺乏几何精度，而立体方法几何精度高却在无纹理和遮挡区域表现不佳。一些方法尝试通过将单目深度与立体信息对齐来结合两者的优势，以增强立体匹配的泛化能力。然而，建立稳定且可泛化的对齐具有挑战性，且不可靠的单目线索会显著降低性能。本文重新思考了单目深度嵌入方式。首先，为防止捷径学习，我们减少分支耦合而非扩展网络宽度。其次，我们从单目深度构建软约束而非硬约束，以提高对单目深度误差的容忍度。基于这些原则，我们将单目信息整合到特征提取和GRU迭代两个阶段。具体而言，将单目深度图与RGB图像融合，以增强深度边界感知并抑制匹配歧义。融合后的图像用于特征提取，使上下文特征能够编码全局几何信息。此外，利用单目深度梯度特征引导视差更新，帮助逃离局部振荡。最后，为解决数据增强导致的监督视差边界模糊问题，我们提出了一种边缘置信度估计方法和边缘感知损失函数。我们的方法在多个标准基准上达到了最先进的性能，在提升精度的同时展现出优异的泛化能力。代码已开源至 https://github.com/linliboabc-maker/stereo-matching-digital。

</details>


### [43] [TextileNet: Towards Zero-shot Text-style Segmentation of Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09299)
*Anguelos Nicolaou,Antonella Ambrosio,Desiree Di Donato,Georg Vogeler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextileNet是一个全卷积多任务网络，仅用合成数据训练即可零样本迁移到历史手稿分析，用于笔迹和性别识别，并首次建立了中世纪晚期文本风格辨别的人类基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自动笔迹识别系统虽已取得显著进展，但由于标注数据稀缺、书写者集合开放以及图像质量退化，其在档案古文字学中的部署仍然受限。本文旨在解决这些实际挑战。

Method: 提出TextileNet，一个全卷积多任务网络，仅使用合成数据训练，生成密集的像素级纹理嵌入，并零样本迁移到历史手稿分析。同时设计了80对/三元组问题的古文字视觉测验，在严格匿名条件下对从普通参与者到资深古文字学者进行测试，建立了手写风格辨别的人类基线。

Result: TextileNet零样本检索在子词粒度上对手写和性别识别有效；实验表明性别与笔迹的关系需谨慎对待。

Conclusion: TextileNet在古文字学领域建立了可信度，但更重要的是实验性证明了手写中的性别问题需要审慎处理。

Original Abstract: Automatic writer identification systems have progressed remarkably in recent years, yet their deployment in archival paleography remains limited by the scarcity of labeled training data, open scribe sets, and degraded image quality. We present TextileNet, a fully convolutional multi-task network trained exclusively on synthetic data to produce dense pixel-level texture embeddings, which we transfer zeroshot to historical manuscript analysis. As an original contribution to evaluation methodology, we designed a paleographic visual quiz of 80 pair and triplet questions and administered it to a range from lay participants to senior paleographers under strict anonymity, establishing to our knowledge for the first time a human baseline for script-style discrimination on late medieval text. We employ TextileNet embeddings to perform zero-shot retrieval on sub-word granularity for hand and gender identification. Our experimental results help in building the credibility of TextileNet in the paleographic domain, but more than that demonstrate in experimental terms that the question of gender in handwriting needs to be treated with caution.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，自动笔迹识别系统取得了显著进展，但由于标注训练数据稀缺、书写者集合开放以及图像质量退化，其在档案古文字学中的部署仍然受限。我们提出了TextileNet，这是一个全卷积多任务网络，仅使用合成数据训练，生成密集的像素级纹理嵌入，并通过零样本方式迁移到历史手稿分析中。作为评估方法论的一项原创贡献，我们设计了一个包含80道成对和三选一问题的古文字视觉测验，并在严格匿名条件下对从普通参与者到资深古文字学者进行了测试，据我们所知，这是首次为中世纪晚期文本的手写风格辨别建立人类基线。我们利用TextileNet嵌入在子词粒度上执行零样本检索，用于手写和性别识别。我们的实验结果有助于建立TextileNet在古文字学领域的可信度，但更重要的是，实验性证明了手写中的性别问题需要谨慎对待。

</details>


### [44] [From Classification to Localization and Clinical Validation: Large-Scale Development of a Deep Learning System for Thoracic Disease Detection on Chest Radiographs in Thailand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09305)
*Isarun Chamveha,Tretap Promwiset,Napat Wanchaitanawong,Trongtum Tongdee,Pairash Saiviroonporn,Warasinee Chaisangmongkon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Inspectra CXR version 5，一个基于DenseNet-121、Attend-and-Compare Modules和Probabilistic Class Activation Map的深度学习系统，用于胸部X光片的胸部疾病多标签分类和弱监督病灶定位。模型在泰国874,858张胸片数据上训练，在院内测试集上达到0.994的平均AUROC，在跨13家医院的泛化集上达到0.970，且获得了放射科医生94.7%的定位一致性和89分的系统可用性评分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 泰国及东南亚地区放射科医生严重短缺，而将深度学习模型本地化适配到泰国人群数据可以显著提高诊断准确性，因此需要开发一个针对泰国人群的、高性能且具备病灶定位能力的胸部X光片分析系统。

Method: 模型采用DenseNet-121作为骨干网络，结合Attend-and-Compare Modules (ACM)和Probabilistic Class Activation Map (PCAM)聚合层，实现在单一模型内同时进行多标签胸部疾病分类和弱监督病灶定位，输出每个病种的分类分数和热力图。

Result: 在19,871例院内测试集上，平均AUROC为0.994（平均敏感度92.4%，特异度98.6%）；在来自泰国13家医院的5,992例独立泛化集上，平均AUROC为0.970；病灶定位方面，在4,549例标注数据上，平均病灶定位分数为77.9%，每张图像非病灶定位数为0.59；在与5位放射科医生的可用性评估中，分类一致率93.6%，定位一致率94.7%，系统可用性量表平均分89分。

Conclusion: 本地开发的、具备定位能力的胸部X光片分析系统能够在泰国实现高准确率、跨医院泛化能力强，并获得了执业放射科医生的信任。

Original Abstract: Chest radiography (CXR) remains the most widely used thoracic imaging modality, yet expert interpretation is constrained by a severe shortage of radiologists in Thailand and across Southeast Asia. Local adaptation of deep learning models to Thai data has been shown to substantially improve accuracy on Thai populations. Here we present the development and comprehensive validation of the chest radiograph analysis model in Inspectra CXR version 5, a deep learning system that performs multi-label thoracic disease classification and weakly supervised lesion localization within a single model. The architecture couples a DenseNet-121 backbone with Attend-and-Compare Modules (ACM) and a Probabilistic Class Activation Map (PCAM) aggregation layer, producing a per-condition classification score and heatmap simultaneously. The model was developed on 874,858 frontal chest radiographs with paired radiologist reports from Siriraj Hospital, Bangkok. On a held-out, radiologist-verified in-domain test set of 19,871 cases, it achieved a mean AUROC of 0.994 (mean sensitivity 92.4%, specificity 98.6%) across nine clinically important conditions. On an independent generalization set of 5,992 cases from 13 hospitals across Thailand, the mean AUROC was 0.970, indicating robust transfer across sites. For localization, evaluated on 4,549 radiologist-annotated cases, the model attained a mean lesion-localization fraction (LLF) of 77.9% at 0.59 non-lesion localizations per image. In a usability evaluation with five thoracic radiologists, the system reached a classification concordance of 93.6%, a localization concordance of 94.7%, and a mean System Usability Scale (SUS) score of 89. These results indicate that a locally developed, localization-capable CXR system can deliver high accuracy, generalize across heterogeneous Thai hospitals, and earn the trust of practicing radiologists.

中文摘要翻译: 胸部X光片（CXR）仍然是最广泛使用的胸部影像学检查方式，然而专家的判读受到泰国及整个东南亚地区放射科医生严重短缺的限制。将深度学习模型本地化适配到泰国数据已被证明能够显著提高其在泰国人群上的准确性。本文介绍了Inspectra CXR version 5胸部X光片分析模型的开发和全面验证，该深度学习系统能够在单一模型内实现多标签胸部疾病分类和弱监督病灶定位。模型架构将DenseNet-121骨干网络与Attend-and-Compare Modules (ACM)和Probabilistic Class Activation Map (PCAM)聚合层相结合，同时输出每个病种的分类分数和热力图。该模型使用来自曼谷Siriraj医院的874,858张正面胸部X光片及其配套放射科报告进行开发。在由放射科医生验证的19,871例保留院内测试集上，模型在九种临床重要病种上达到了平均AUROC为0.994（平均敏感度92.4%，特异度98.6%）。在来自泰国13家医院的5,992例独立泛化测试集上，平均AUROC为0.970，表明模型在各站点间具有稳健的迁移能力。在病灶定位方面，基于4,549例经放射科医生标注的病例，模型的平均病灶定位分数（LLF）为77.9%，每张图像的非病灶定位数为0.59。在与五位胸部放射科医生的可用性评估中，系统达到了93.6%的分类一致率、94.7%的定位一致率，以及平均89分的系统可用性量表（SUS）评分。这些结果表明，一个本地开发的、具备定位能力的胸部X光片分析系统能够提供高准确率，在泰国各类型医院间具有良好的泛化能力，并赢得执业放射科医生的信任。

</details>


### [45] [Dynamic Inverse Rendering for Enhanced Material-Lighting Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09329)
*Raza Yunus,Benjamin Ummenhofer,Jan Eric Lenssen,Eddy Ilg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可重光照方法，通过结合刚性运动物体的跟踪、重建与逆渲染，利用运动带来的多样化光照-表面交互来解耦材质与光照，从而在逆渲染中解决材质-光照歧义性问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 逆渲染中将表面辐射分解为材质和光照是严重病态问题，因为多种组合可以产生相同观测颜色。虽然多光照条件捕获有助于解决歧义，但本文探索利用刚性运动物体提供的多样化光-表面交互观测来替代多光照条件，以解决材质-光照歧义。

Method: 提出一种可重光照方法，将物体跟踪与重建同逆渲染相结合，适用于一般刚性运动物体。通过观测物体刚性运动下多样化的光-表面交互来约束优化，实现材质与光照的解耦。

Result: 在合成数据上的实验表明，运动有助于解耦材质和光照：物体在刚性运动下重建的材质显著比静态时更准确。在真实手持物体的RGB视频上，该方法即使在有噪声的真实条件下仍能保持这一优势。

Conclusion: 刚性运动可以作为逆渲染中解耦材质与光照的有效先验，运动信息能够替代多光照条件，提升材质重建的准确性，并在真实世界条件下具有鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Decomposing outgoing surface radiance into material and illumination during inverse rendering is essential for applications such as relighting and augmented reality, yet it is severely ill-posed since multiple combinations can result in the same observed colour. Capturing an object under multiple lighting conditions usually helps resolve this ambiguity as it constrains the optimization towards correct solutions. In this work, we explore the potential of reconstructing rigidly moving objects -- which provides observations of diverse light-surface interactions -- to resolve the material-lighting ambiguity in inverse rendering. For this purpose, we introduce a relightable approach that marries object tracking and reconstruction with inverse rendering for general rigidly moving objects. Our experimental analysis on synthetic data demonstrates that motion can be an advantage for disentangling material and lighting: the reconstructed material is significantly more accurate when the object is observed under rigid motion than when it is static. Moreover, results on RGB videos of real hand-held objects show that our pipeline preserves this advantage even under noisy real-world conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 在逆渲染过程中，将出射表面辐射分解为材质和光照对于重光照和增强现实等应用至关重要，然而这是一个严重病态的问题，因为多种组合可能产生相同的观测颜色。通常在多种光照条件下捕获物体有助于解决这种歧义，因为它能约束优化朝着正确的解方向进行。本文探索了利用刚性运动物体——提供多样化的光-表面交互观测——来解诀逆渲染中的材质-光照歧义问题的潜力。为此，我们提出了一种可重光照方法，将物体跟踪与重建同逆渲染相结合，适用于一般刚性运动物体。在合成数据上的实验分析表明，运动有助于解耦材质与光照：当物体在刚体运动下被观测时，重建的材质显著比静态时更准确。此外，在真实手持物体的RGB视频上的结果显示，我们的方法即使在有噪声的真实世界条件下仍能保持这一优势。

</details>


### [46] [Simon-SR: Spatially Adaptive Modulation and Visual Prompt Adaptation for Text-Reinforced Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09351)
*Haotong Cheng,Yuxuan Li,Zijie Cui,Rongling Tan,Chenyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Simon-SR 提出了一种基于可学习提示的多模态超分辨率框架，通过对比提示学习和提示引导的空间自适应细化，在不依赖昂贵标注的情况下实现了更优的感知质量和重建精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于多模态的超分辨率方法对错误先验敏感且需要昂贵的标注，缺乏高效且鲁棒的多模态融合机制。

Method: 提出 Simon-SR 框架，包含对比提示学习（Contrastive Prompt Learning）和提示引导的空间自适应细化（Prompt-Guided Spatially Adaptive Refinement），利用可学习提示进行高效的语义挖掘和鲁棒的文本-图像融合。

Result: 在PSNR上最高提升0.50 dB，SSIM上提升0.0133，LPIPS上提升0.0695，超过了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: Simon-SR 通过可学习提示有效解决了多模态超分辨率中对错误先验敏感和依赖昂贵标注的问题，在多个指标上达到了最优性能。

Original Abstract: Single Image Super-Resolution (SISR) reconstructs high-quality images from low-resolution inputs. While recent multi-modal methods improve perceptual quality, they remain sensitive to erroneous priors and require expensive annotations. To address these issues, we propose Simon-SR, a multi-modal SISR framework leveraging learnable prompts for efficient semantic mining and robust text-image fusion. Our approach combines Contrastive Prompt Learning with Prompt-Guided Spatially Adaptive Refinement to enhance multi-modal alignment. Experiments demonstrate that Simon-SR surpasses state-of-the-art methods, achieving maximum improvements of 0.50 dB in PSNR, 0.0133 in SSIM, and 0.0695 in LPIPS. Code will be released.

中文摘要翻译: 单图像超分辨率（SISR）旨在从低分辨率输入重建高质量图像。尽管近年来多模态方法提升了感知质量，但它们对错误先验仍然敏感，并且需要昂贵的标注。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了 Simon-SR，一种基于可学习提示的多模态 SISR 框架，用于高效的语义挖掘和鲁棒的文本-图像融合。我们的方法结合了对比提示学习（Contrastive Prompt Learning）与提示引导的空间自适应细化（Prompt-Guided Spatially Adaptive Refinement），以增强多模态对齐。实验结果表明，Simon-SR 超越了当前最先进的方法，在 PSNR 上最高提升 0.50 dB，在 SSIM 上提升 0.0133，在 LPIPS 上提升 0.0695。代码将开源。

</details>


### [47] [CtrlVTON: Controllable Virtual Try-On via Visual-Instance-Prompt Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09362)
*Seungyong Lee,Hyun Jun Jang,Sangoh Kim,Sungjoon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIP-SAM解决服装实例分割，以及CtrlVTON框架通过像素级掩码控制实现可控虚拟试穿，两者均达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有虚拟试穿系统对服装的穿着方式（松紧、风格如塞入/敞开、空间位置）缺乏用户控制能力。

Method: 1) VIP-SAM：定义并解决视觉实例提示分割任务，给定平铺服装图像，在穿着该服装的人体照片中分割出特定实例；2) CtrlVTON：将试穿重构为图像编辑问题，引入分割掩码作为像素级控制，实现对服装风格、尺寸和空间位置的控制。

Result: VIP-SAM和CtrlVTON各自在对应任务上达到SOTA；CtrlVTON在遵循用户提供布局方面远超最强专有编辑系统，同时在服装保真度上与之相当。

Conclusion: 通过实例分割和可控试穿框架的结合，有效弥补了现有VTO系统在服装穿着方式控制上的空白。

Original Abstract: Virtual try-on (VTO) has made significant progress in realistically transferring garments onto a target person. Yet most systems give the user little control over how a garment should be worn -- its size (loose or fitted), style (e.g., tucked in or untucked, open or closed), and spatial placement on the body. We address this gap with two complementary contributions. First, we define and solve Visual-Instance-Prompt Segmentation via VIP-SAM: given a flatlay image of a garment, segment that specific instance in a photograph of a person wearing it. This is an instance-level task, distinct from the typically studied category-level segmentation. Second, we introduce CtrlVTON, a controllable VTO framework that recasts try-on as an image editing problem and adds segmentation masks as pixel-level control over garment layout, including style, size, and spatial placement on the body. VIP-SAM and CtrlVTON each achieve state-of-the-art results on their respective tasks. In particular, CtrlVTON generates images that follow user-provided layouts far more faithfully than the strongest proprietary editing systems while matching them on garment fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 虚拟试穿（VTO）在将服装真实地迁移到目标人物身上取得了显著进展。然而，大多数系统给予用户对服装穿着方式的控制非常有限——包括其尺寸（宽松或修身）、风格（例如塞入或外露、敞开或闭合）以及身体上的空间位置。我们通过两项互补贡献来弥补这一空白。首先，我们通过VIP-SAM定义并解决了视觉实例提示分割任务：给定一张服装的平铺图像，在穿着该服装的人物照片中分割出该特定实例。这是一个实例级任务，不同于通常研究的类别级分割。其次，我们引入了CtrlVTON，一个可控的VTO框架，它将试穿重定义为图像编辑问题，并添加分割掩码作为对服装布局的像素级控制，包括风格、尺寸和身体上的空间位置。VIP-SAM和CtrlVTON各自在对应任务上达到了最先进的结果。特别是，CtrlVTON在遵循用户提供布局方面远超最强的专有编辑系统，同时在服装保真度上与之相当。

</details>


### [48] [SVF-CR: Synchronized Visual-Facial Cross-Refinement for Multimodal Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09417)
*Hyein Park,Namho Kim,Junhwa Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种同步视觉-面部交叉精炼框架（SVF-CR），通过多模态对齐与证据融合，用于识别矛盾心理与犹豫行为状态，在BAH公共测试集上达到0.7156的macro-F1。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 矛盾心理与犹豫是细微的行为状态，涉及言语、面部、视觉语境和声学线索的复杂组合。现有方法未能有效建模跨模态间时间对齐的行为证据交互，因此需要一种能够同步对齐视觉与面部信息、并实现双向交叉精炼的多模态融合方法。

Method: 首先对视频进行相同时间划分，提取全局视频段令牌和裁剪面部段令牌；通过模态内自注意力和双向视觉-面部交叉注意力进行令牌精炼；构建段级一致性/不一致性视觉-面部证据；文本和声学特征经上下文自注意力轻量精炼后，利用成对证据融合在决策阶段与视觉-面部证据融合。

Result: 在BAH公共评估集上，所提SVF-CR方法优于全局视觉-面部令牌融合和同步证据基线，公共macro-F1达到0.7156。

Conclusion: 同步视觉-面部交叉精炼框架能有效利用跨模态时间对齐信息提升矛盾心理与犹豫识别性能，代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy are subtle behavioral states that are expressed through a combination of verbal content, facial behavior, visual context, and acoustic cues. Effective recognition therefore requires not only extracting informative unimodal representations, but also modeling how temporally aligned behavioral evidence interacts across modalities. In this paper, we propose a synchronized visual-facial cross-refinement framework (SVF-CR) with pairwise multimodal evidence fusion for ambivalence and hesitancy recognition. The proposed method first extracts whole-video segment tokens and cropped-face segment tokens using the same temporal partition. The synchronized visual and facial tokens are refined through intra-modal self-attention and bidirectional visual-facial cross-attention, allowing whole-video context and local facial behavior to mutually refine each other before evidence construction. We then construct segment-level visual-facial evidence using consistency and discrepancy modeling, followed by temporal self-attention and attention pooling. Textual and acoustic features are lightly refined through context self-attention and are fused with the enhanced visual-facial evidence at the final decision stage using pairwise evidence fusion. Experiments on the BAH (Behavioral Ambivalence/Hesitancy) public evaluation split show that the proposed synchronized visual-facial cross-refinement improves public macro-F1 over both global visual-face token fusion and synchronized evidence baselines, achieving a public macro-F1 of 0.7156. Code is available at : https://github.com/hiinnnii/BAH-Challenge-ECCV2026\_SVF-CR.

中文摘要翻译: 矛盾心理与犹豫是微妙的行为状态，通过言语内容、面部行为、视觉语境和声学线索的组合来表达。因此，有效的识别不仅需要提取信息丰富的单模态表征，还需要对时间对齐的行为证据在模态间的交互方式进行建模。本文提出了一种同步视觉-面部交叉精炼框架（SVF-CR），结合成对多模态证据融合进行矛盾心理与犹豫识别。该方法首先使用相同的时间划分提取视频段令牌（whole-video segment tokens）和裁剪面部段令牌（cropped-face segment tokens）。同步的视觉和面部令牌通过模态内自注意力（intra-modal self-attention）和双向视觉-面部交叉注意力（bidirectional visual-facial cross-attention）进行精炼，使得全局视频上下文与局部面部行为在证据构建前能够相互精炼。随后，我们通过一致性（consistency）和差异性（discrepancy）建模构建段级视觉-面部证据，并应用时间自注意力（temporal self-attention）和注意力池化（attention pooling）。文本和声学特征通过上下文自注意力（context self-attention）进行轻量精炼，并在最终决策阶段通过成对证据融合（pairwise evidence fusion）与增强后的视觉-面部证据进行融合。在BAH（Behavioral Ambivalence/Hesitancy）公共评估集上的实验表明，所提出的同步视觉-面部交叉精炼在公共macro-F1指标上优于全局视觉-面部令牌融合和同步证据基线，取得了0.7156的公共macro-F1。代码开源地址：https://github.com/hiinnnii/BAH-Challenge-ECCV2026_SVF-CR。

</details>


### [49] [Multimodal Scenario Similarity Search for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09428)
*Tamás Matuszka,András Tamásy,Balázs Szolár*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种多模态框架，结合视觉和轨迹表示用于自动驾驶场景检索，发现轨迹表示擅长运动中心事件检索，视觉嵌入在外观信息丰富时表现更优，二者融合能持续提升检索质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶场景检索方法要么依赖视觉表示，要么依赖运动描述，难以理解两种方法的相对优势和局限，因此需要统一框架进行系统性比较与融合。

Method: 提出多模态检索框架，集成两种轨迹方法：Exo-Trajectory（基于周围智能体运动的显式匹配）和ScenarioFormer（基于对比学习的Transformer轨迹表示），并与强视觉基线对比，在多样化驾驶场景中分析行为。

Result: 轨迹表示在切入、转弯、排队等运动中心事件中检索性能强；视觉嵌入在外观信息丰富时表现好；二者融合持续提升检索质量，达到最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 外观与运动捕获是场景相似性的互补概念，激励了面向自动驾驶数据挖掘、数据集策划和场景验证的多模态检索系统。

Original Abstract: Large-scale autonomous-driving datasets contain vast numbers of recorded scenarios, creating a need for efficient retrieval methods that can identify situations similar to a given query. Existing approaches typically rely on either visual representations or motion-based descriptions, making it difficult to understand their relative strengths and limitations for scenario retrieval. In this work, we present a multimodal framework for autonomous-driving scenario retrieval that combines visual and trajectory-based representations within a unified retrieval pipeline. We investigate two trajectory-based approaches: Exo-Trajectory, an explicit matching method based on surrounding-agent motion, and ScenarioFormer, a transformer-based representation learned from object trajectories using contrastive learning. We compare these approaches against strong vision-based baselines and analyze their behavior across a diverse set of driving scenarios. Experimental results show that trajectory representations provide strong retrieval performance for motion-centric events such as cut-ins, turning maneuvers, and traffic queueing, while visual embeddings excel when appearance cues are informative. Most importantly, combining visual and trajectory information consistently improves retrieval quality, yielding the best overall performance. These findings demonstrate that appearance and motion capture are complementary notions of scenario similarity and motivate multimodal retrieval systems for autonomous-driving data mining, dataset curation, and scenario-based validation.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模自动驾驶数据集包含大量记录场景，因此需要高效的检索方法来识别与给定查询相似的场景。现有方法通常依赖视觉表示或基于运动的描述，这使得难以理解它们在场景检索中的相对优势和局限性。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种用于自动驾驶场景检索的多模态框架，该框架在统一检索流程中结合了视觉和基于轨迹的表示。我们研究了两种基于轨迹的方法：Exo-Trajectory，一种基于周围智能体运动的显式匹配方法；以及ScenarioFormer，一种通过对比学习从对象轨迹中学习得到的基于Transformer的表示。我们将这些方法与强视觉基线进行比较，并分析它们在多样化驾驶场景中的行为。实验结果表明，轨迹表示为切入、转弯和排队等以运动为中心的事件提供了强大的检索性能，而视觉嵌入在外观信息丰富时表现优异。最重要的是，结合视觉和轨迹信息能够持续提升检索质量，取得最佳的整体性能。这些发现表明，外观和运动捕获是场景相似性的互补概念，并激励了面向自动驾驶数据挖掘、数据集策划和场景验证的多模态检索系统。

</details>


### [50] [Parameter-Efficient Vision-Language Adaptation with Continuous Metadata Conditioning for Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09443)
*Anil Osman Tur,Tonje Knutsen Sordalen,Kim Tallaksen Halvorsen,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CLIP的参数高效微调框架，通过连续元数据条件化机制（将数值型元数据直接融入提示表示）提升动物长期再识别在形态演变和季节外观变化下的鲁棒性，实验在七年纵向鱼类数据集和多个野生动物基准上验证了优越性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长期动物再识别需要应对渐进的形态演变和季节性外观变化，现有的视觉-语言模型虽提供强预训练表示，但难以适应纵向生态场景中的身份和时序分布偏移。

Method: 提出参数高效的CLIP适应框架：1) 低秩视觉适应（LoRA-style）；2) 基于提示的监督；3) 跨模态对齐；4) 核心贡献是连续元数据条件化机制——在训练时将数值型元数据（如时间、长度等）直接编码为连续提示表示，而非离散化为文本类别，实现嵌入空间的平滑调制，测试时仅需纯视觉推理。

Result: 在七年纵向鱼类数据集和多个野生动物基准上，该方法在闭集、开集和时间感知评估协议下均取得改进。连续元数据条件化提升了对长期外观变化和时序分布偏移的鲁棒性，参数高效微调使测试时无需元数据即可进行纯视觉推理。

Conclusion: 连续元数据条件化策略有效提升了动物长期再识别对时序分布偏移的鲁棒性，同时参数高效微调保持了测试时的纯视觉推理效率，为纵向生态监测提供了实用方案。

Original Abstract: Long-term animal re-identification (ReID) must remain robust to gradual morphological evolution and seasonal appearance shifts. Although recent vision-language models provide strong pretrained visual representations, adapting them to longitudinal ecological settings remains challenging, particularly under identity and temporal distribution shifts. We present a parameter-efficient CLIP adaptation framework for animal ReID and introduce a continuous metadata-conditioning mechanism that incorporates numerical attributes directly into the prompt representation during training. While low-rank visual adaptation, prompt-based supervision, and cross-modal alignment provide the adaptation framework, the proposed metadata-conditioning strategy constitutes the primary methodological contribution. By preserving the continuous structure of numerical metadata rather than discretizing it into textual categories, the proposed approach enables smooth modulation of the embedding space during training while maintaining a purely visual inference pipeline. Experiments on a seven-year longitudinal fish dataset and multiple wildlife benchmarks demonstrate improved performance under closed-set, open-set, and time-aware evaluation protocols. The results demonstrate that continuous metadata conditioning improves robustness to longitudinal appearance variation and temporal distribution shifts, while parameter-efficient adaptation enables a purely visual inference pipeline without requiring metadata at test time. Code and evaluation splits can be found at: https://github.com/AnilOsmanTur/MetaPrompt-ReID.

中文摘要翻译: 长期动物再识别必须对渐进的形态演变和季节性的外观变化保持鲁棒。尽管最近的视觉-语言模型提供了强大的预训练视觉表示，但将它们适应到纵向生态场景仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在身份分布偏移和时序分布偏移的情况下。我们提出了一种参数高效的CLIP适应框架用于动物再识别，并引入了一种连续元数据条件化机制，该机制在训练期间将数值属性直接融入提示表示中。虽然低秩视觉适应、基于提示的监督和跨模态对齐构成了适应框架的基础，但所提出的元数据条件化策略构成了主要的方法贡献。通过保留数值元数据的连续结构而非将其离散化为文本类别，所提方法能够在训练期间实现嵌入空间的平滑调制，同时保持纯视觉的推理流程。在七年纵向鱼类数据集和多个野生动物基准上的实验表明，该方法在闭集、开集和时间感知评估协议下均取得了改进的性能。结果表明，连续元数据条件化提升了对纵向外观变化和时序分布偏移的鲁棒性，而参数高效适应使得测试时无需元数据即可进行纯视觉推理。代码和评估划分可在 https://github.com/AnilOsmanTur/MetaPrompt-ReID 获取。

</details>


### [51] [Robustifying Vision-Language Models via Test-Time Prompt Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09450)
*Xingyu Zhu,Huanshen Wu,Shuo Wang,Beier Zhu,Jiannan Ge,Jiaheng Zhang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RITA框架，通过分布级对齐（最优传输）和动态缓存实现鲁棒的测试时提示自适应，显著提升对抗鲁棒性而不影响干净准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法依赖样本级置信度启发式，忽略数据内在分布结构，无法区分对抗性错误预测与真实语义一致性。

Method: RITA框架：1) 使用最优传输将增强视觉特征的分布与文本原型对齐，缓解对抗异常值并纠正跨模态语义错位；2) 引入动态缓存，从测试流中逐步累积可靠线索进行在线优化。

Result: 大量实验表明RITA显著提升对抗鲁棒性，同时不损害干净样本的准确率。

Conclusion: 从样本级估计转向分布级对齐能有效应对对抗扰动，实现鲁棒的测试时自适应。

Original Abstract: Pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP achieve strong zero-shot generalization, but their performance degrades sharply under adversarial perturbations. Existing test-time adaptation methods typically rely on sample-level confidence heuristics, overlooking the intrinsic distributional structure of the data. This sample-centric approach limits robustness, as it fails to distinguish confident adversarial mispredictions from true semantic consistency. In this work, we observe that adversarial distortion is structurally brittle: while holistic representations are corrupted, semantic integrity is often preserved in the distribution of augmented views. Motivated by this insight, we propose RITA, a Robust test-tIme prompt-TAdaptation framework that shifts from sample-level estimates to distribution-level alignment. Specifically, RITA employs optimal transport to align the distribution of augmented visual features with textual prototypes, mitigating adversarial outliers and rectifying cross-modal semantic misalignment. Furthermore, we introduce a dynamic cache to progressively accumulate reliable cues from the test stream for online refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RITA significantly improves adversarial robustness without compromising clean accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 预训练的视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）在零样本泛化上表现出色，但在对抗扰动下性能急剧下降。现有的测试时自适应方法通常依赖样本级置信度启发式，忽略了数据内在的分布结构。这种以样本为中心的方法限制了鲁棒性，因为它无法区分高置信度的对抗性错误预测与真正的语义一致性。在这项工作中，我们观察到对抗性失真在结构上是脆弱的：虽然整体表示被破坏，但语义完整性往往保留在增广视图的分布中。受此启发，我们提出RITA（鲁棒的测试时提示自适应框架），将关注点从样本级估计转向分布级对齐。具体而言，RITA采用最优传输将增广视觉特征的分布与文本原型对齐，缓解对抗性异常值并纠正跨模态语义错位。此外，我们引入动态缓存，从测试流中逐步累积可靠线索以进行在线优化。大量实验表明，RITA显著提升了对抗鲁棒性，同时不损害干净样本的准确率。

</details>


### [52] [Hydra++: Real-Time Hierarchical 3D Scene Graph Construction With Object-Level Shape Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09455)
*Hyungtae Lim,Nathan Hughes,Xihang Yu,Ruihan Xu,Yun Chang,Jingnan Shi,Rajat Talak,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hydra++ 是一个将学习型形状估计器集成到层次化3D场景图管道的系统，通过类别无关形状估计和重投影一致性检查提升物体和场景重建质量，并支持混合LiDAR-相机配置以应对室外环境挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D场景图系统对物体几何的建模较为粗糙，依赖部分点云或类别级CAD模板，缺乏实例级别的形状细节。本文旨在通过集成学习型形状估计器来增强场景图中的几何细节。

Method: Hydra++ 集成了类别无关形状估计方法，并引入重投影掩码一致性检查来拒绝从部分观测或不精确分割产生的退化预测。默认基于CRISP配置实现在线场景图构建，同时将SAM3D等更慢的估计器作为模块化替代进行泛化-延迟权衡评估。为应对室外场景中稀疏噪声的深度测量，支持混合LiDAR-相机配置以提升大规模场景重建质量。

Result: 在仿真和真实室外校园场景中的实验表明，Hydra++ 显著提升了物体级和场景级的重建质量。

Conclusion: Hydra++ 通过系统级集成学习型形状估计器，有效提升了层次化3D场景图中物体和场景的几何重建质量，并在室内外场景中验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: 3D scene graphs provide a hierarchical abstraction of environments by encoding spatial entities, such as objects and places, and their relationships. However, existing scene graph systems model object geometry coarsely, relying on partial point clouds or class-level CAD templates, which limits instance-specific shape detail. This paper presents Hydra++, a system-level investigation into how learning-based object shape estimators can be integrated into a hierarchical 3D scene graph pipeline. Hydra++ incorporates category-agnostic shape estimation and a reprojection-mask consistency check to reject degenerate predictions from partial observations or imprecise segmentation. In its default CRISP-based configuration, Hydra++ performs online scene graph construction; slower estimators such as SAM3D are evaluated as modular alternatives to demonstrate generalization-latency trade-offs. Furthermore, to address the challenges of sparse and noisy depth measurements in outdoor environments, Hydra++ supports a hybrid LiDAR-camera configuration for large-scale operation, improving scene-level reconstruction quality. Experiments in both simulation and real-world outdoor campus scenarios demonstrate that Hydra++ improves object- and scene-level reconstruction quality. Project page is available at https://hydra-plusplus.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 3D场景图通过编码空间实体（如物体和地点）及其关系，提供了环境的分层抽象。然而，现有的场景图系统对物体几何的建模较为粗糙，依赖于部分点云或类别级CAD模板，这限制了实例级别的形状细节。本文介绍了Hydra++，一项系统级的研究，探讨如何将基于学习的物体形状估计器集成到分层3D场景图管道中。Hydra++融合了类别无关的形状估计方法，并引入重投影掩码一致性检查机制，以拒绝因部分观测或分割不精确而产生的退化预测。在其默认的CRISP配置下，Hydra++能够实现在线场景图构建；同时，诸如SAM3D等较慢的估计器作为模块化替代方案被评估，以展示泛化-延迟之间的权衡。此外，为应对室外环境中稀疏且带噪声的深度测量挑战，Hydra++支持混合LiDAR-相机配置以实现大规模操作，从而提升场景级重建质量。在仿真环境和真实室外校园场景中的实验表明，Hydra++改善了物体级和场景级的重建质量。项目页面可访问 https://hydra-plusplus.github.io/。

</details>


### [53] [Foveation-Guided Dynamic Token Selection for Robust and Efficient Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09480)
*Ibrahim Batuhan Akkaya,Kishaan Jeeveswaran,Bahram Zonooz,Elahe Arani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Foveated Dynamic Transformer (FDT)，一种受人类视觉系统启发的视觉Transformer架构，通过注视点引导的动态令牌选择机制，在保持高鲁棒性的同时显著提升计算效率，在50%注视预算下凭借81.9%的准确率超越DeiT-S（80.9%），并减少34.57%的计算量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 受人类视觉系统中注视采样与眼动机制的高效性与鲁棒性启发，旨在将生物视觉的适应性和抗噪能力引入视觉Transformer，解决现有模型在应对噪声和对抗攻击时脆弱性高、计算资源消耗大等问题。

Method: 提出注视（fixation）模块和中央凹（foveation）模块：注视模块用于定位注视点并过滤无关信息，中央凹模块生成包含多尺度信息的中央凹嵌入（foveated embeddings），两者协同实现动态令牌选择，构建FDT架构。

Result: 在50%注视预算设置下，FDT准确率达81.9%，超越DeiT-S的80.9%，同时计算量（乘加操作）减少34.57%，展现出精度-效率的优越权衡；且对多种噪声和对抗攻击表现出固有鲁棒性，无需专门针对此类挑战进行训练。

Conclusion: FDT作为一种受人类视觉系统启发的视觉Transformer架构，成功结合了自适应计算与更强的抗干扰能力，为设计兼具鲁棒性与高效性的人工神经网络提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: The human visual system (HVS) employs foveated sampling and eye movements to achieve efficient perception, conserving both metabolic energy and computational resources. Drawing inspiration from this robustness and adaptability, we introduce the Foveated Dynamic Transformer (FDT), a foveation-guided dynamic token-selection architecture that integrates these mechanisms into a vision transformer framework. The FDT exhibits strong resilience to various types of noise and adversarial attacks, despite not being explicitly trained for such challenges. This inherent robustness is achieved through the use of fixation and foveation modules: the fixation module identifies fixation points to filter out irrelevant information, while the foveation module generates foveated embeddings with multi-scale information. At the 50% fixation-budget setting, FDT achieves higher accuracy than DeiT-S (81.9% vs. 80.9%) while reducing multiply-accumulate operations by 34.57%, highlighting one operating point on its accuracy-efficiency trade-off. These attributes position FDT as an HVS-inspired step toward artificial neural networks that combine adaptive computation with improved resilience.

中文摘要翻译: 人类视觉系统通过注视采样和眼动实现高效感知，节约了代谢能量和计算资源。受这种鲁棒性和适应性的启发，我们提出了注视引导的动态令牌选择架构——Foveated Dynamic Transformer (FDT)，将这些机制集成到视觉Transformer框架中。尽管未针对这些挑战进行显式训练，FDT仍对多种类型的噪声和对抗攻击表现出强鲁棒性。这种固有鲁棒性通过注视模块和中央凹模块实现：注视模块识别注视点以过滤无关信息，中央凹模块生成具有多尺度信息的中央凹嵌入。在50%注视预算设置下，FDT的准确率（81.9%）高于DeiT-S（80.9%），同时乘加操作减少34.57%，凸显了其在精度-效率权衡上的优越工作点。这些特性使FDT成为迈向结合自适应计算与更强抗干扰能力的人工神经网络的一次受人类视觉系统启发的探索。

</details>


### [54] [Decoupling Language Guidance from Backbones for Text-Guided Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09481)
*Yungeng Liu,Xuanzi Fang,Haijin Zeng,Qi Dai,Yongyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BTHA提出了一种骨干网络可迁移的分层适配器框架，用于文本引导的医学图像分割，通过特征级接口和粗到细的分层监督策略，在不同视觉和文本骨干网络上保持有效，且计算开销小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本引导医学图像分割模型将跨模态融合、监督信号和解码器设计紧密耦合在特定任务架构中，导致当编码器对更换时语言引导模块难以复用，需要重新设计网络。

Method: BTHA框架包括三个核心组件：(1) 基于形状保持适配器的稳定特征级接口，在多尺度视觉特征和文本表示间注入语义引导；(2) 分层粗到细监督策略，包含全局图像-文本对齐、多尺度辅助定位和边界感知的最终掩码精炼；(3) 尺度自适应门控语义引导适配器，通过分辨率特定门控控制文本注入，通道重校准抑制冗余跨模态响应。

Result: 在多种视觉（卷积和Transformer）和文本骨干网络上验证了相同的适配器和监督设计均保持有效；在四个公开数据集上，BTHA以适度的计算开销提升了强文本引导基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: BTHA通过解耦跨模态融合与特定架构的绑定，实现了语言引导模块在不同骨干网络间的可迁移复用，为文本引导医学图像分割提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Text-guided medical image segmentation leverages clinical semantics to improve lesion delineation, yet many existing models bind cross-modal fusion, supervision, and decoder design into a task-specific architecture. Such tight coupling makes it difficult to reuse language guidance modules across heterogeneous vision and text backbones, and often requires redesigning the network when the encoder pair changes. This paper presents BTHA, a backbone-transferable hierarchical adapter framework for text-guided medical image segmentation. BTHA is built around a stable feature-level interface: given multi-scale visual features and a text representation, it injects semantic guidance through shape-preserving adapters while maintaining the decoder-side tensor contract. To make this interface effective, we introduce a Hierarchical Coarse-to-Fine Supervision Strategy that decomposes learning into global image-text alignment, multi-scale auxiliary localization, and boundary-aware final mask refinement. We further design a Scale-Adaptive Gated Semantic Guidance (SAGSG) adapter, where resolution-specific gates adaptively control textual injection and channel recalibration suppresses redundant cross-modal responses. Evaluations across diverse vision and text backbones show that the same adapter and supervision design remains effective across convolutional and transformer-based visual encoders as well as different language encoders. Experiments on four public datasets further demonstrate that BTHA improves strong text-guided baselines with modest computational overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 文本引导的医学图像分割利用临床语义改善病灶勾画，然而许多现有模型将跨模态融合、监督信号和解码器设计绑定在特定任务架构中。这种紧密耦合使得语言引导模块难以在异构的视觉和文本骨干网络之间复用，且当编码器对更换时常需重新设计网络。本文提出BTHA，一种骨干网络可迁移的分层适配器框架，用于文本引导的医学图像分割。BTHA围绕一个稳定的特征级接口构建：给定多尺度视觉特征和文本表示，它通过形状保持适配器注入语义引导，同时保持解码器端的张量约束。为使该接口有效，我们引入分层粗到细监督策略，将学习过程分解为全局图像-文本对齐、多尺度辅助定位和边界感知的最终掩码精炼。我们进一步设计了尺度自适应门控语义引导适配器，其中分辨率特定的门控自适应控制文本注入，通道重校准抑制冗余的跨模态响应。在多种视觉和文本骨干网络上的评估表明，相同的适配器和监督设计在卷积和基于Transformer的视觉编码器以及不同语言编码器上均保持有效。在四个公开数据集上的实验进一步证明，BTHA以适度的计算开销提升了强文本引导基线方法的性能。

</details>


### [55] [SigLIP-HD by Fine-to-Coarse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09488)
*Lihe Yang,Zhen Zhao,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SigLIP-HD，通过一种简单的由粗到细的监督设计，在不增加推理预算的前提下，让中分辨率图像的粗粒度特征模仿高分辨率图像的细粒度特征，从而在标准分辨率下获得更优的视觉表征，显著提升多模态大模型在OCR等任务上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs直接使用更高分辨率图像虽能产生更细粒度的视觉token，但会引入额外的计算和设计复杂度（多次前向传播和大量后处理）。作者质疑：在简单采用更高分辨率之前，是否真正挖掘了模型在标准分辨率下的全部感知能力？因此，研究如何在无需更大图像、更低成本下实现精细视觉感知。

Method: 核心是一种简单有效的由细到粗的监督设计（fine-to-coarse supervision）：强制中分辨率图像的粗粒度特征去模仿同一图像高分辨率版本的细粒度特征。该框架构建在先进的SigLIP 2模型之上。

Result: 在完全相同的推理预算下，SigLIP-HD能生成更优的视觉token。在大量多模态大模型基准测试上，该模型始终优于基线模型，尤其在OCR相关任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的特征模仿监督，无需提高输入分辨率或增加推理成本，即可显著提升视觉表征质量和MLLMs的下游任务性能，为高效视觉感知提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: High-quality visual representation is a long-standing pursuit in computer vision. In the context of multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), feeding higher-resolution images can produce more fine-grained visual tokens. However, it introduces additional computational and design complexity, due to multiple forward passes and post-processing of increased tokens. Before simply adopting a higher resolution, have we truly unlocked the model's full perception capability at a standard resolution? Therefore, we study an interesting problem: how to achieve fine visual perception under lower cost without larger images. We present SigLIP-HD in this work. The core is a highly simple fine-to-coarse supervision design. We enforce the coarse feature of a mid-resolution image to mimic the fine-grained feature of its high-resolution version. We build this framework on the advanced SigLIP 2 model. Our final model produces better visual tokens at exactly the same inference budget. It is validated on extensive MLLM benchmarks and consistently delivers stronger results than our baseline model, especially on OCR-related tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 高质量的视觉表征是计算机视觉领域的长期追求。在多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的背景下，输入更高分辨率的图像可以产生更细粒度的视觉token。然而，由于需要多次前向传播以及对增多token的后处理，这会引入额外的计算和设计复杂度。在简单采用更高分辨率之前，我们是否真正挖掘了模型在标准分辨率下的全部感知能力？因此，我们研究了一个有趣的问题：如何在不使用更大图像的情况下，以更低的成本实现精细的视觉感知。本文提出了SigLIP-HD，其核心是一种极其简单的由细到粗的监督设计。我们强制中分辨率图像的粗粒度特征去模仿其高分辨率版本的细粒度特征。我们将该框架构建在先进的SigLIP 2模型之上。最终模型在完全相同的推理预算下生成了更优的视觉token。该方法在大量多模态大模型基准测试上得到了验证，并且始终比基线模型表现出更强的性能，尤其是在OCR相关任务上。

</details>


### [56] [What VGGT Knows About Overlap: Probing Geometric Foundation Models for Co-Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09503)
*Filippo Ziliotto,Luciano Serafini,Lamberto Ballan,Tommaso Campari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现VGGT模型隐式编码了共视性信息，其内部表示呈现类似于大语言模型的层级结构：早期层构建3D感知场景表示，后期层则充当专门的共视性推理器。基于此，提出Co-VGGT，冻结VGGT并训练一个轻量级的层感知混合专家头（<7.5M参数），仅从RGB图像分类共视性，在Co-VisiON基准上超越人类标注基线，比先前工作提升超过25%（成对）和10%（多视图）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3D重建和机器人定位中的核心挑战是共视性判断——确定哪些图像对共享重叠的可见表面，尤其是在重叠区域极小的情况下。现有方法要么依赖人工标注，要么需要复杂的后处理，效率低下。

Method: 首先通过分析VGGT各层表示，发现第L17层作为负锚点，始终将非共视图像对路由到该层。基于此发现，提出Co-VGGT：冻结VGGT全部权重，仅训练一个轻量级层感知混合专家头（Layer-wise Mixture-of-Experts），将每一层视为一个专门化专家，根据输入图像对自适应加权各层的几何抽象特征，从而从纯RGB图像中分类共视性。

Result: 在Co-VisiON基准上，Co-VGGT超越人类标注基线，成对共视性分类提升超过25%，多视图任务提升超过10%；成对预测校准良好（ECE=0.030），可直接作为可视化图中的边权重用于下游SfM和SLAM流程，无需后处理校正。

Conclusion: VGGT模型隐式编码了共视性作为涌现行为，其层级结构与LLM类似。Co-VGGT以极小的参数量实现了SOTA共视性分类性能，且预测结果可直接用于下游3D重建和SLAM任务。

Original Abstract: A fundamental challenge in 3D reconstruction and robotic localization is co-visibility: determining which image pairs share overlapping visible surfaces, particularly in scenarios with minimal overlap. We demonstrate that VGGT implicitly encodes co-visibility as an emergent behavior: without any supervision for this task, its internal representations exhibit a clear hierarchical structure mirroring that of large language models, i.e. early layers build a 3D-aware scene representation, while late layers act as dedicated co-visibility reasoners. In particular, we identify layer L17 as a negative anchor that consistently routes non-co-visible pairs for this backbone, regardless of the evaluation setting, providing task-grounded evidence of layer specialization in a geometry-grounded foundation model. Building on this, we introduce Co-VGGT, which freezes VGGT and trains only a lightweight layer-wise mixture-of-experts head (less than 7.5M parameters) to classify co-visibility from RGB alone, treating each layer as a specialized expert whose geometric abstraction is adaptively weighted per input pair. On the Co-VisiON benchmark, Co-VGGT surpasses the human annotation baseline and improves over prior work by more than 25% pairwise and 10% multiview. Pairwise predictions are well-calibrated (ECE=0.030), enabling direct use as edge weights in visibility graphs for downstream SfM and SLAM pipelines without post-hoc correction. Code and data are available.

中文摘要翻译: 3D重建和机器人定位中的一个基本挑战是共视性：确定哪些图像对共享重叠的可见表面，特别是在重叠区域极小的情况下。我们证明VGGT隐式地将共视性编码为一种涌现行为：在没有对此任务进行任何监督的情况下，其内部表示呈现出类似于大语言模型的清晰层级结构，即早期层构建3D感知场景表示，而后期层则充当专门的共视性推理器。特别地，我们识别出第L17层作为一个负锚点，无论评估设置如何，该层始终将非共视图像对路由到该骨干网络，为几何基础模型中的层专门化提供了基于任务的经验证据。基于此，我们提出Co-VGGT，它冻结VGGT并仅训练一个轻量级的层感知混合专家头（少于7.5M参数），从纯RGB图像中分类共视性，将每一层视为一个专门化专家，其几何抽象根据输入图像对进行自适应加权。在Co-VisiON基准上，Co-VGGT超越了人类标注基线，比先前工作在成对任务上提升超过25%，在多视图任务上提升超过10%。成对预测校准良好（ECE=0.030），使其能够直接用作下游SfM和SLAM流程中可视化图的边权重，无需后验校正。代码和数据已公开。

</details>


### [57] [DGSfM: Depth-Guided Scale-Aware Global Structure-from-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09507)
*Sithu Aung,Viktor Kocur,Yaqing Ding,Torsten Sattler,Zuzana Kukelova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DGSfM提出了一种利用单目深度图作为可扩展先验的深度感知全局运动恢复结构（SfM）管线，通过深度感知的相对位姿求解器、视图图过滤和深度一致性引导的对应点剪枝，以及全局尺度平均和深度引导的位姿-点初始化，有效解决了传统全局SfM对噪声基线和弱视图图约束敏感的问题，在ETH3D和IMC2021上显著提升了位姿精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统全局SfM依赖尺度模糊的对极几何，导致全局定位对噪声基线估计和弱视图图约束非常敏感，同时视觉模糊的图像对产生的虚假边会进一步降低重建质量。作者希望引入单目深度图作为可扩展的先验信息，将尺度模糊的对极约束转化为尺度感知的相对位姿约束，从而提升全局SfM的鲁棒性和精度。

Method: 1) 深度感知相对位姿求解器：将尺度模糊的对极约束转化为尺度感知的相对位姿约束；2) 视图图过滤与深度一致性对应点剪枝：抑制仅在对极几何下看似合理的虚假边和错误匹配；3) 全局尺度平均与深度引导的位姿-点初始化：将单目深度图对齐到公共重建尺度，为全局定位和光束法平差提供稳定的初始化。

Result: 在ETH3D和IMC2021数据集上，DGSfM在稀疏和稠密匹配前端下均能持续优于强全局SfM基线方法，在位姿精度上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: DGSfM通过引入单目深度先验，有效克服了传统全局SfM中尺度模糊和弱约束带来的问题，在保持显式多视图优化的同时显著提升了重建的鲁棒性和精度，代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Global Structure-from-Motion (SfM) is an efficient paradigm for recovering camera poses and sparse 3D structure from unordered images. However, its reliance on scale-ambiguous epipolar geometry makes global positioning sensitive to noisy baseline estimates and weak view-graph constraints, while false edges from visually ambiguous pairs can further degrade reconstruction. We propose DGSfM, a depth-aware global SfM pipeline that uses monocular depth maps as a scalable prior while preserving explicit multi-view optimization. For each image pair, we use a depth-aware relative pose solver to convert scale-ambiguous epipolar constraints into scale-aware relative pose constraints. We further improve robustness through view-graph filtering and depth-consistency-based correspondence pruning, which suppress false edges and matches that remain plausible under epipolar geometry alone. Finally, global scale averaging and depth-guided pose-point initialization align monocular depth maps into a common reconstruction scale and provide stable initialization for global positioning and bundle adjustment. Experiments on ETH3D and IMC2021 show that DGSfM consistently improves over strong global SfM baselines across sparse and dense matching front-ends, achieving substantial gains in pose accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/sithu31296/DGSfM.

中文摘要翻译: 全局运动恢复结构（Global Structure-from-Motion, SfM）是一种从无序图像中恢复相机位姿和稀疏三维结构的高效范式。然而，其对尺度模糊的对极几何的依赖使得全局定位对噪声基线估计和弱视图图约束十分敏感，而视觉模糊图像对产生的虚假边会进一步降低重建质量。我们提出了DGSfM，一种深度感知的全局SfM管线，该方法在保持显式多视图优化的同时，利用单目深度图作为可扩展的先验。对于每对图像，我们使用深度感知的相对位姿求解器，将尺度模糊的对极约束转化为尺度感知的相对位姿约束。我们进一步通过视图图过滤和基于深度一致性的对应点剪枝来提升鲁棒性，从而抑制那些仅在对极几何下看似合理的虚假边和错误匹配。最后，全局尺度平均和深度引导的位姿-点初始化将单目深度图对齐到共同的重建尺度，并为全局定位和光束法平差提供稳定的初始化。在ETH3D和IMC2021上的实验表明，DGSfM在稀疏和稠密匹配前端下均能持续优于强全局SfM基线方法，在位姿精度上取得了显著提升。代码开源在 https://github.com/sithu31296/DGSfM。

</details>


### [58] [Seeing is Free, Speaking is Not: Uncovering the True Energy Bottleneck in Edge VLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09520)
*Junfei Zhan,Haoxun Shen,Mingang Guo,Zixuan Huang,Tengjiao He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地测量了边缘设备上VLM推理的能耗分布，推翻了“视觉处理是主要能耗来源”的常见假设，发现模型功耗是固有常量，输出token数量才是能耗和延迟的主导因素，且解码阶段能耗远高于预填充阶段。结论是：VLM在边缘设备上的真正能耗瓶颈不在于模型看到了什么，而在于它说了多少。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有关于VLM效率的研究主要集中于减少视觉token，隐含假设视觉处理是主要能耗成本，但该假设缺乏实证依据。本文旨在通过系统性能耗分析检验这一假设的正确性。

Method: 对5个VLM模型（涵盖3种架构族、4种输入分辨率、2种硬件平台）进行系统性的on-device能耗分析，测量推理功率、延迟和能耗，并分析输入分辨率、图像复杂度、提示类型等因素的影响。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 平均推理功率是模型固有常数，在不同条件下变化小于5%；(2) 每个输出token的耗时是输入token的11-39倍，输出token数量是延迟和能耗的主导因素；(3) 图像复杂度（物体数量）可导致高达4.1倍的能耗差异，但源于输出长度而非视觉处理成本。即使移除所有视觉token最多节省10%能耗，而控制输出长度最多可节省97%能耗。

Conclusion: 边缘VLM推理的真正能耗瓶颈不在于模型看到了什么，而在于它说了多少。解码阶段的能耗主导地位随模型规模增大而增强，输出长度控制比视觉token剪枝更有效。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are the perceptual backbone of embodied AI, but their energy footprint on edge hardware remains poorly understood. Existing efficiency efforts focus predominantly on reducing visual tokens, implicitly treating visual processing as the dominant energy cost. We overturn this implicit assumption through the first systematic energy profiling of on-device VLM inference, spanning five models across three architecture families, four input resolutions, and two hardware platforms (NVIDIA RTX 3070 and Jetson Orin NX). Our analysis yields three findings. First, average inference power is a model-intrinsic constant, invariant to input resolution, image complexity, and prompt type, with less than 5% variation across all conditions. This means that all energy variation across inputs must arise from variation in inference time, not from variation in power draw. Second, each output token costs 11 to 39x more wall-clock time than each input token due to the compute-bound and memory-bound asymmetry between prefill and decode, making output token count the dominant driver of both latency and energy. Third, image complexity, measured by the number of objects in an image, induces up to 4.1x energy differences at identical resolution. This variation arises not from increased visual processing cost, but from differences in output length. These findings expose a fundamental limitation of visual token pruning: even removing all visual tokens saves at most 10% of total energy for fixed-token models. Across models spanning 1 billion to 8 billion parameters, controlling output length saves up to 97% of total energy, with the energy dominance of decoding growing stronger at larger model scale. In short, the true energy bottleneck in edge VLM inference is not what the model sees, but how much it says.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）是具身智能的感知主干，但它们在边缘硬件上的能耗足迹仍鲜为人知。现有的效率研究主要集中于减少视觉token，隐含地将视觉处理视为主导能耗成本。本文通过首次对设备端VLM推理进行系统性能耗分析，推翻了这一隐含假设。研究涵盖5个模型、3种架构族、4种输入分辨率和2种硬件平台（NVIDIA RTX 3070和Jetson Orin NX）。我们的分析得出三个发现。第一，平均推理功率是模型固有常数，不受输入分辨率、图像复杂度和提示类型的影响，在所有条件下变化小于5%。这意味着所有跨输入的能耗差异必然来自推理时间的变化，而非功率变化。第二，由于预填充与解码阶段在计算和内存上的不对称性，每个输出token的墙上时间成本是每个输入token的11-39倍，使得输出token数量成为延迟和能耗的主导因素。第三，图像复杂度（以图像中的物体数量衡量）在相同分辨率下可导致高达4.1倍的能耗差异。这种差异并非来自视觉处理成本的增加，而是源于输出长度的差异。这些发现揭示了视觉token剪枝的根本局限性：对于固定token模型，即使移除所有视觉token，最多也只能节省10%的总能耗。在参数规模从10亿到80亿的模型中，控制输出长度最多可节省97%的总能耗，且解码阶段的能耗主导地位随模型规模增大而增强。简言之，边缘VLM推理的真正能耗瓶颈不在于模型看到了什么，而在于它说了多少。

</details>


### [59] [ALICE: Learning a General-Purpose Pathology Foundation Model from Vision, Vision-Language, and Slide-Level Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09526)
*Jiawen Li,Tian Guan,Huijuan Shi,Xitong Ling,Mingxi Fu,Anjia Han,Chao He,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALICE是一个通过多阶段凝聚蒸馏统一多种病理学基础模型（视觉、视觉-语言、全切片）的单一骨干模型，在21个任务场景、96个下游任务上取得最优平均排名。

<details>
