  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有病理学基础模型因预训练目标、数据来源和空间尺度的不同而彼此割裂，互补的专业能力分散在不同的骨干网络上，缺乏一个统一的基础模型来整合这些能力。

Method: 提出多阶段凝聚蒸馏（Multi-stage Agglomerative Distillation），将八个视觉、视觉-语言和全切片级别的教师模型依次蒸馏到一个单一骨干模型的专用模块中。模型在24,985,184张图块级病理图像和155,604张高分辨率图像上预训练。

Result: 在21个任务场景、96个下游任务和48个数据源上评估，涵盖感兴趣区域组织分析、视觉-语言多模态评估和全切片临床评估三个场景，ALICE在所有三个评估设置中均取得任务匹配病理基础模型的最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 凝聚蒸馏能够将来自多个专业模型的互补能力整合到一个统一的骨干网络中，用于广泛的计算病理学应用。

Original Abstract: Foundation models are reshaping computational pathology, yet their capabilities remain shaped by pretraining objectives, data sources, and spatial scales, fragmenting complementary expertise across separate backbones. Here we present ALICE, a unified foundation model trained through multi-stage agglomerative distillation that sequentially distills eight vision-only, vision-language, and slide-level teacher models into dedicated modules of a single backbone. ALICE is pretrained on 24,985,184 tile-level pathology images and 155,604 high-resolution images, and evaluated across 21 task scenarios, 96 downstream tasks, and 48 data sources, spanning region-of-interest tissue analysis, vision-language multimodal evaluation, and whole-slide clinical assessment. In all three evaluation settings, ALICE achieved the best average rank among task-matched pathology foundation models. These results demonstrate that agglomerative distillation can consolidate complementary capabilities from specialized models into a unified backbone for broad computational pathology applications. The model is available at https://github.com/WonderLandxD/ALICE.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型正在重塑计算病理学，但其能力仍然受到预训练目标、数据来源和空间尺度的制约，使得互补的专业知识分散在独立的骨干网络中。本文提出ALICE，一个统一的基础模型，通过多阶段凝聚蒸馏训练，将八个视觉、视觉-语言和全切片级别的教师模型依次蒸馏到一个单一骨干网络的专用模块中。ALICE在24,985,184张图块级病理图像和155,604张高分辨率图像上进行预训练，并在21个任务场景、96个下游任务和48个数据源上进行评估，涵盖感兴趣区域组织分析、视觉-语言多模态评估和全切片临床评估。在所有三个评估设置中，ALICE在与任务匹配的病理基础模型中取得了最佳平均排名。这些结果表明，凝聚蒸馏能够将来自专业模型的互补能力整合到一个统一的骨干网络中，用于广泛的计算机病理学应用。该模型已开源在https://github.com/WonderLandxD/ALICE。

</details>


### [60] [The Count Is There, but Misaligned: Understanding and Correcting Counting Failures in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09544)
*Ahmed Oumar El-Shangiti,Abzal Nurgazy,Hilal AlQuabeh,Nikolai Rozanov,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）在物体计数任务中，即使输出错误答案，其内部激活往往已编码了正确计数。通过训练非线性探针检测计数错误，并利用因果干预和自校正方法，无需更新参数即可将计数准确率提升最多15.6个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究VLM在基础物体计数任务上表现不佳的根本原因：是模型内部缺乏计数知识，还是内部表征与最终输出之间存在差距。

Method: 1) 在四种VLM的激活上训练简单探针（线性/非线性），检测计数错误；2) 使用SVCCA分析探针的激活子空间对齐性；3) 进行因果 steering 干预验证探针方向的有效性；4) 提出检测器引导的自校正方法：仅当内部错误检测器预测失败时，重新提示模型。

Result: 非线性探针能可靠检测计数错误；SVCCA分析表明基于真实计数的探针与基于模型输出的探针共享部分激活子空间但沿未对齐方向读取；因果干预证实增强计数识别探针方向可提升计数性能；所提自校正方法无需参数更新即可将计数准确率提升最多15.6个绝对百分点。

Conclusion: 激活层面的错误探针既可作为改进VLM计数的实用工具，也为理解内部知识与模型输出之间的差距提供了机制视角。

Original Abstract: Despite strong performance on many multimodal tasks, vision-language models (VLMs) still struggle with basic object counting. We investigate whether this reflects missing internal knowledge or a gap between internal representations and verbalized outputs. Training simple probes on activations from four VLMs across five counting datasets reveals that nonlinear probes can reliably detect counting errors, suggesting that VLMs often encode the correct count even when they output the wrong answer. SVCCA analysis shows that probes trained on ground-truth counts and probes trained on model outputs occupy a partially shared activation subspace but read out along misaligned directions. We further validate our findings using a causal steering intervention, proving that strengthening the direction of count-identified probes does improve model counting performance. Motivated by this result, we propose a detector-guided self-correction method that selectively re-prompts the model only when an internal error detector predicts failure. This simple inference-time intervention improves counting accuracy by up to 15.6 absolute percentage points, without any parameter updates. Our results establish activation-based error probing as both a practical tool for improving VLM counting and a mechanistic lens on the gap between internal knowledge and model outputs.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视觉语言模型（VLM）在众多多模态任务上表现优异，但在基本的物体计数任务上仍然存在困难。本文探究了这一问题究竟反映了模型内部知识的缺失，还是内部表征与言语化输出之间存在差距。通过在四种VLM的激活层上训练简单探针，并在五个计数数据集上进行测试，我们发现非线性探针能够可靠地检测到计数错误，这表明VLM即便输出了错误答案，其内部往往已经编码了正确的计数。SVCCA分析显示，基于真实计数训练的探针和基于模型输出训练的探针占据部分共享的激活子空间，但读取方向未对齐。我们进一步利用因果引导干预验证了上述发现，证明增强计数识别探针的方向确实能够提升模型的计数性能。受此结果启发，我们提出了一种检测器引导的自校正方法：仅在内部错误检测器预测失败时，有选择性地重新提示模型。这种简单的推理时干预方法无需任何参数更新，即可将计数准确率提升多达15.6个绝对百分点。我们的实验结果确立了基于激活的错误探针既可作为改进VLM计数的实用工具，也可作为理解内部知识与模型输出之间差距的机制视角。

</details>


### [61] [TCLA: Training-Free Class-wise Logit Adaptation for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09562)
*Tianyou Jiang,Ziyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TCLA是一种无需训练的少量样本适应方法，通过校正推理logits来提升医学视觉语言模型在分布外数据上的性能，在九个医学影像数据集上优于现有的基于训练的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的医学视觉语言模型在零样本场景下表现良好，但在分布外数据上性能下降，原因是域偏移和类别偏差。已有的少量样本适应方法需要引入额外可训练组件，在极低数据场景（如单样本）下不稳定且缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: TCLA是一种纯无训练的少量样本适应方法，通过少量支持样本校正推理logits，具体通过改善类间去混淆和减少域偏移来提升预训练视觉语言模型的性能。

Result: 在X光、超声、MRI、CT、组织病理学等九种数据集上的实验表明，TCLA能够持续提升医学视觉语言模型在分布外数据上的性能，并且在大多数情况下优于现有的基于训练的适应方法。

Conclusion: TCLA作为一种快速、模型无关且无需训练的少量样本适应方法，能有效提升医学视觉语言模型在分布外数据上的鲁棒性和性能。

Original Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot performance, yet their effectiveness still declines on out-of-distribution (OOD) data due to domain shifts and class bias inherited from large-scale pretraining. Existing few-shot adaptation methods typically introduce additional trainable components, which can be unstable in extremely low-data regimes (e.g., 1-shot), and lack robustness on different medical data. We present TCLA, a purely training-free few-shot adaptation method for Medical VLMs, which is fast and model-agnostic. TCLA corrects inference logits based on a small set of support samples, boosting pretrained VLMs performance by improving inter-class deconfusion and reducing domain shift. Extensive experiments on nine datasets across multiple medical imaging modalities including X-ray, Ultrasound, MRI, CT, Histopathology, demonstrate that TCLA consistently improves OOD performance of Medical VLMs and, in most of cases, outperforms existing training-based adaptation methods.

中文摘要翻译: 医学视觉语言模型（VLM）展现出强大的零样本性能，但由于大规模预训练带来的域偏移和类别偏差，其在分布外（OOD）数据上的有效性仍然下降。现有的少量样本适应方法通常引入额外的可训练组件，这在极低数据场景（如单样本）下可能不稳定，且在不同医学数据上缺乏鲁棒性。我们提出TCLA，一种纯无训练的医学VLM少量样本适应方法，该方法快速且模型无关。TCLA基于少量支持样本校正推理logits，通过改善类间去混淆和减少域偏移来提升预训练VLM的性能。在涵盖X光、超声、MRI、CT、组织病理学等多种医学影像模态的九个数据集上的大量实验表明，TCLA持续提升了医学VLM在OOD数据上的性能，并且在大多数情况下优于现有的基于训练的适应方法。

</details>


### [62] [Wan-Dancer: A Hierarchical Framework for Minute-scale Coherent Music-to-Dance Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09581)
*Mingyang Huang,Peng Zhang,Li Hu,Guangyuan Wang,Bang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种分层框架，通过全局关键帧规划与局部时序细化，实现从音乐到分钟级连贯舞蹈视频（720p/30fps，超过60秒）的生成，解决了现有扩散模型在长时长下的时序漂移、身份不一致和动作重复问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型在音乐到舞蹈生成任务中受限于20秒以内的时序约束，基于3D骨架或端到端合成的方法在长时长场景下存在时序漂移、身份不一致和动作模式重复等问题，难以生成长时长、高清且节奏同步的舞蹈视频。

Method: 提出分层框架：1）全局关键帧规划，利用全曲音乐上下文确保长程连贯性；2）局部时序细化，包含动态帧率自适应（基于时间映射RoPE嵌入实现精确定位）、光流损失函数增强运动连续性、运动速度控制在高频动作时保留高保真细节。

Result: 生成的舞蹈视频超过1分钟（720p/30fps），具有优越的时序稳定性；在5种不同舞蹈风格上均表现出鲁棒性，支持音频和文本双条件控制，达到了长形式舞蹈视频合成的最新水平。

Conclusion: 该分层框架成功突破了传统时长限制，能够生成长时长、高清且节奏连贯的音乐到舞蹈视频，并在多风格场景下展现出色泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Generating long-duration, high-definition, and rhythmically synchronized dance videos directly from music remains a significant challenge, primarily due to the temporal constraints of current diffusion models, which typically fail beyond 20 seconds. Existing approaches, whether they rely on intermediate 3D skeletons or on end-to-end video synthesis, suffer from temporal drift, identity inconsistency, and repetitive motion patterns when extended to longer horizons. To address these limitations, we propose a novel hierarchical framework for minute-scale coherent music-to-dance generation. Our method decouples the process into global keyframe planning and local temporal refinement, leveraging full-track musical context to ensure long-range coherence. Key innovations include dynamic frame rate adaptation via time-mapped RoPE embeddings for precise alignment, an optical-flow-based loss function to enhance motion continuity, and motion-speed control to preserve high-fidelity details during rapid movements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework surpasses the conventional duration barrier, generating stable, 720p/30fps videos exceeding one minute with superior temporal stability. Furthermore, the model exhibits robust versatility across five distinct dance genres, conditioned on both audio and textual prompts, establishing a new state-of-the-art in coherent, long-form dance video synthesis.

中文摘要翻译: 直接从音乐生成长时长、高清且节奏同步的舞蹈视频仍然是一个重大挑战，主要原因在于当前扩散模型的时间约束——通常无法支持超过20秒的生成。现有方法，无论是依赖中间3D骨架还是端到端视频合成，在扩展到更长时间范围时都会出现时序漂移、身份不一致和动作模式重复等问题。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种新颖的分层框架，用于分钟级连贯的音乐到舞蹈生成。该方法将生成过程解耦为全局关键帧规划和局部时序细化两个阶段，利用完整的音乐上下文来确保长程连贯性。关键创新包括：通过时间映射RoPE嵌入实现动态帧率自适应以达到精确定位、基于光流的损失函数增强运动连续性，以及运动速度控制以在快速运动时保留高保真细节。大量实验表明，我们的框架突破了传统的时长限制，能够生成稳定且超过一分钟的720p/30fps视频，具有优越的时序稳定性。此外，该模型在五种不同舞蹈风格上表现出强大的泛化能力，可同时基于音频和文本提示进行条件控制，在连贯的长形式舞蹈视频合成领域达到了新的最优水平。

</details>


### [63] [Promptable Concept Segmentation from Above: Evaluating SAM 3's Zero-Shot and One-Shot Capabilities in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09583)
*Mohammad Dabaja,Turgay Celik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了SAM 3在遥感图像零样本/单样本任务中的性能，发现视觉与文本提示存在跨模态干扰，并提出了一种无需训练的代理评估协议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SAM 3在专用领域（如遥感）的分布外泛化能力尚未被量化，尤其是面对自上而下的复杂几何结构时表现未知。

Method: 对SAM 3进行结构适配：将其解耦的二值存在头改造为独立的零样本分类器；系统隔离文本和视觉提示模态，在五种配置下诊断多模态解码器的对齐机制；提出无需训练的代理评估协议用于广义零样本任务。

Result: 视觉提示能有效对齐解码器到遥感几何结构，但文本提示注入地面语义偏差并干扰坐标回归；SAM 3避免了过拟合，在分割任务中取得高Harmonic Mean分数，但受限于亚像素分辨率和俯视语义盲区。

Conclusion: SAM 3具备良好的分布外泛化能力，但多模态解码器存在跨模态干扰和语义盲区，需要进行参数高效的地理空间微调。

Original Abstract: The deployment of large-scale foundation models, such as the Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3), promises a transition toward open-vocabulary, training-free computer vision. However, their capacity to generalize out-of-distribution to the complex, top-down geometric structures of Earth Observation imagery remains largely unquantified. Driven by SAM 3's performance disparities in highly specialized domains, we present a comprehensive, multi-task empirical evaluation across remote sensing scene classification, object detection, and instance segmentation under strict zero-shot and one-shot constraints. To achieve this, we introduce a structural adaptation of SAM 3 by repurposing its decoupled binary presence head into a standalone zero-shot classifier. Furthermore, by systematically isolating textual and visual prompt modalities across five configurations, we explicitly diagnose the alignment mechanics within the model's multimodal decoder. Our findings reveal severe cross-modal interference: while visual prompts successfully align the decoder to complex remote sensing geometry, textual prompts inject misaligned, ground-level semantic bias, actively degrading coordinate regression. To benchmark these capabilities without resource-intensive training, we formulate a novel training-free proxy evaluation protocol for Generalized Zero-Shot tasks (scene classification and instance segmentation). Ultimately, our results demonstrate that SAM 3 avoids the overfitting commonly seen in legacy domain-adapted models, achieving high Harmonic Mean scores in segmentation tasks. However, it remains fundamentally constrained by sub-pixel resolution limits and overhead semantic blind spots, charting a definitive mandate for parameter-efficient geospatial fine-tuning of its multimodal decoder.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模基础模型（如Segment Anything Model 3, SAM 3）的部署有望推动计算机视觉向开放词汇、无需训练的方向发展。然而，它们对地球观测图像中复杂、自上而下的几何结构的分布外泛化能力尚未得到量化。受SAM 3在高度专业化领域性能差异的驱动，我们在严格零样本和单样本约束下，对遥感场景分类、目标检测和实例分割进行了全面的多任务实证评估。为此，我们引入了一种SAM 3的结构适配方法，将其解耦的二值存在头重新用作独立的零样本分类器。此外，通过系统隔离五种配置下的文本和视觉提示模态，我们明确诊断了模型多模态解码器内的对齐机制。我们的发现揭示了严重的跨模态干扰：视觉提示能成功将解码器对齐到复杂的遥感几何结构，而文本提示则注入了不对齐的地面级语义偏差，主动降低了坐标回归性能。为了在不进行资源密集型训练的情况下对这些能力进行基准测试，我们提出了一种新颖的无需训练的代理评估协议，用于广义零样本任务（场景分类和实例分割）。最终，我们的结果表明，SAM 3避免了传统领域适应模型中常见的过拟合问题，在分割任务中取得了高Harmonic Mean分数。然而，它仍然受到亚像素分辨率限制和俯视语义盲区的基本约束，这为其多模态解码器的参数高效地理空间微调指明了明确的方向。

</details>


### [64] [4DR360: State Reasoning for Joint 3D Detection and Occupancy Prediction in 4D Radar-Camera Full-Scene Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09629)
*Xiaokai Bai,Lianqing Zheng,Runwei Guan,Songkai Wang,Siyuan Cao,Hui-liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种4D雷达-相机融合框架ManTruck，用于360°全场景感知，将语义占据建模为持续的状态而非终端输出，通过跨模态状态推理实现粗到细的特征聚合，并在统一的多任务评估框架下验证了检测与占据任务的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有雷达-相机方法主要优化检测任务，而双任务系统通常以有限的交互方式分别解码边界框和占据网格，缺乏有效的多任务协同。为填补这一空白并推进基于雷达的多任务学习，本文提出将语义占据建模为持续的场景状态，以实现更充分的检测与占据联合学习。

Method: 提出跨模态状态推理范式，将占据状态建模并在各阶段传播以实现粗到细的特征聚合。具体包含两个核心模块：状态引导的BEV增强模块（SBE）用于增强帧内BEV表示；多普勒引导的时间融合模块（DTF）用于在更长的时间范围内保持状态证据。

Result: 在统一的雷达-相机多任务评估框架下进行了准确性、鲁棒性、消融实验和效率分析，覆盖了检测与占据两个任务。

Conclusion: ManTruck框架有效实现了4D雷达-相机融合下的360°全场景感知，将语义占据作为持续状态进行建模和传播，提升了多任务感知的协同效果。

Original Abstract: Reliable autonomous driving requires full-scene perception that couples foreground objects with dense semantic layout. Recently, 4D millimeter-wave radar has emerged as a robust and affordable sensor, yet its sparse returns make radar-camera fusion necessary for comprehensive scene understanding. Existing radar-camera methods mainly optimize detection, while dual-task systems usually decode boxes and occupancy with limited interaction. To address this gap and advance radar-based multi-task learning, we propose \method, a 4D radar-camera framework for 360$^\circ$ full-scene perception, which models semantic occupancy as a persistent scene state rather than a terminal output. \method{} follows a cross-modal state reasoning paradigm, where the occupancy state is modeled and propagated through stages for coarse-to-fine feature aggregation. Specifically, State-guided BEV Enhancement (SBE) strengthens intra-frame BEV representation, while Doppler-guided Temporal Fusion (DTF) preserves state evidence over longer temporal horizons. Beyond the model, we further extend ManTruckScenes with satellite-map-based generated occupancy labels and pair it with OmniHD-Scenes in a unified cross-dataset detection-and-occupancy protocol. The resulting experiments cover accuracy, robustness, ablation, and efficiency under one radar-camera multi-task evaluation framework. Code and labels will be released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 可靠的自动驾驶需要耦合前景目标与密集语义布局的全场景感知。近期，4D毫米波雷达作为一种鲁棒且经济的传感器出现，但其稀疏的回波使得雷达-相机融合成为实现全面场景理解的必要条件。现有的雷达-相机方法主要优化检测任务，而双任务系统通常以有限的交互方式分别解码边界框和占据网格。为填补这一空白并推进基于雷达的多任务学习，本文提出\method，一个用于360°全场景感知的4D雷达-相机框架，该框架将语义占据建模为持久的场景状态而非终端输出。\method{}遵循跨模态状态推理范式，其中占据状态被建模并在各阶段中传播，以实现从粗到细的特征聚合。具体而言，状态引导的BEV增强模块（SBE）增强了帧内BEV表示，而多普勒引导的时间融合模块（DTF）在更长的时间范围内保持状态证据。除模型本身外，我们还利用基于卫星地图生成的占据标签扩展了ManTruckScenes，并将其与OmniHD-Scenes配对，形成统一的跨数据集检测与占据协议。最终实验在统一的雷达-相机多任务评估框架下涵盖了准确性、鲁棒性、消融研究和效率分析。代码和标签将在录用后开源。

</details>


### [65] [The Effects of Synthetic Data and Label Distribution on Canola Branch Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09630)
*Amirsalar Darvishpour,Mikolaj Cieslak,Adam Runions*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统研究了合成植物图像数据在自动表型分析中的有效性，发现合成与真实图像比例约1:7时性能最佳，标签分布越接近真实分布效果越好，过多合成数据反而有害。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 收集带标注的植物图像成本高昂且耗时，而植物生长模型可以生成无限量的合成图像，但合成数据能否提升性能取决于合成与真实图像的比例以及合成数据集的标签分布，目前缺乏对这两个因素的系统量化研究。

Method: 使用经过校准的L系统植物模型生成合成油菜图像，在分支计数任务上训练ResNet-18模型，独立变化合成与真实图像比例（1:5到1:22）以及合成数据标签分布（均匀分布、向真实分布插值、高斯平滑等），评估不同设置下的平均绝对误差。

Result: 最佳合成/真实比例1:7使平均绝对差降低7.6%；均匀标签分布效果最差（约1.70），向真实分布插值90%可降至0.927，高斯平滑真实标签分布取得最佳结果0.912（比纯真实数据提升14.7%）；每标签最少10张合成图像可适度提升，但每标签100张则过度矫正反而降低性能。

Conclusion: 合理比例的合成数据可以显著提升植物表型分析性能，但合成数据的标签分布必须尽可能匹配真实分布，过量合成数据会损害模型性能。

Original Abstract: Collecting annotated plant images for automated phenotyping is often slow and expensive. Plant models simulating growth and development can generate unlimited synthetic images with exact labels. However, previous work has established that whether incorporating synthetic data improves performance depends on the ratio of synthetic to real images and the label distribution of the synthetic dataset. To systematically quantify both factors, we train ResNet-18 models on a canola branch-counting task using a calibrated L-system plant model. We vary each factor independently. Synthetic-to-real ratios of 1:5 to 1:22 broadly improve performance; the best ratio (1:7) reduces mean absolute difference by 7.6% over real-only training. For label distribution, a uniform synthetic distribution is strongly suboptimal (abs. diff. of approximately 1.70); interpolating 90% toward the real distribution yields abs. diff. 0.927, whereas Gaussian smoothing of the real label distribution yields the best overall result (abs. diff. 0.912, a 14.7% improvement over real-only). A minimum of 10 synthetic images per label offers a simpler alternative with modest gains, while 100 per label over-corrects and hurts performance.

中文摘要翻译: 收集用于自动表型分析的标注植物图像通常既缓慢又昂贵。模拟生长和发育的植物模型能够生成无限量的合成图像及其精确标签。然而，先前研究表明，是否纳入合成数据能够提升性能取决于合成图像与真实图像的比例以及合成数据集的标签分布。为了系统量化这两个因素，我们使用经过校准的L系统植物模型，在油菜分支计数任务上训练ResNet-18模型。我们独立变化每个因素。合成与真实图像比例在1:5到1:22之间普遍能提升性能；最佳比例（1:7）比纯真实数据训练的平均绝对差降低了7.6%。对于标签分布而言，均匀的合成分布效果极差（绝对差约为1.70）；向真实分布插值90%得到绝对差0.927，而对真实标签分布进行高斯平滑则取得了最佳整体结果（绝对差0.912，比纯真实数据提升14.7%）。每标签至少10张合成图像提供了一种更简单的替代方案，能带来适度提升，而每标签100张则过度矫正并损害性能。

</details>


### [66] [Revisiting Euler-Angle Regression with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09650)
*Yangting Sun,Zijun Cui,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合范围感知欧拉角建模与KAN网络的新框架，通过利用欧拉角的有界范围特性和KAN的加性函数形式，显著提升了旋转回归任务的精度、收敛速度和效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 欧拉角回归中存在的间断性和奇异性常导致训练不稳定，而现有方法忽视了旋转表示、回归架构与领域约束之间的交互作用。本文旨在重新审视欧拉角回归，揭示其有效性取决于上述三者的协同设计。

Method: 提出一个结合范围感知欧拉角建模（range-aware Euler modeling）与Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（KAN）的框架。KAN用边缘上的可学习单变量函数替代固定节点激活函数，其加性函数形式与有界欧拉角范围导致的近加性回归结构相匹配。同时提供了理论分析证明该匹配关系的合理性。

Result: 在受控旋转回归、目标姿态估计、机器人及人体逆运动学等多个任务上的实验表明，该方法在精度、收敛速度和效率上均取得一致改进。理论分析和实验共同验证了有界欧拉范围与KAN加性结构之间的正向匹配趋势。

Conclusion: 本文证明欧拉角回归的有效性关键在于旋转表示、回归架构与领域约束的协同设计，所提出的范围感知欧拉建模+KAN框架为旋转回归任务提供了一个高效且准确的解决方案。

Original Abstract: In many real-world systems, including articulated robots and biomechanical models, rotations are defined in joint space and naturally parameterized by Euler angles with bounded ranges. Yet regressing Euler angles remains challenging, as their discontinuities and singularities often destabilize training. In this work, we revisit Euler-angle regression and show that its effectiveness depends critically on the interaction between rotation representation, regression architecture, and domain constraints. We introduce a new framework that combines range-aware Euler modeling with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN), which replace fixed node-wise activations with learnable univariate functions on edges. We further provide theoretical analysis indicating that bounded Euler ranges motivate a near-additive structure in the regression function, which favors the additive functional form of KAN, and we confirm this trend empirically. Extensive experiments on controlled rotation regression, object pose estimation, and robotic and human inverse kinematics demonstrate consistent improvements in accuracy, convergence, and efficiency. The code will be publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 在许多现实系统中，包括关节机器人和生物力学模型，旋转是在关节空间中定义的，并且自然由具有有界范围的欧拉角参数化。然而，回归欧拉角仍然具有挑战性，因为其间断性和奇异性常常破坏训练的稳定性。在这项工作中，我们重新审视了欧拉角回归，并表明其有效性关键取决于旋转表示、回归架构和领域约束之间的交互作用。我们引入了一个新框架，将范围感知欧拉角建模与Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）相结合，后者用边缘上的可学习单变量函数替代固定的节点激活函数。我们进一步提供了理论分析，表明有界欧拉范围促使回归函数呈现出近加性结构，这有利于KAN的加性函数形式，并通过实验证实了这一趋势。在受控旋转回归、目标姿态估计以及机器人和人体逆运动学上的大量实验表明，该方法在精度、收敛速度和效率方面均取得一致的改进。代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [67] [Evolution of Accuracy and Visual-Cognitive Errors in a Decade of Vision-Language AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09654)
*Shravan Murlidaran,Miguel P. Eckstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过构建复杂社会行为(CSB)数据集，系统评估了过去十年(2017-2025)视觉语言模型(VLM)在复杂社会交互场景描述上的进展，发现多模态大语言模型(MLLM)已基本消除了与人类的准确率差距，但空间依赖错误仍存在。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM评估多使用简单场景(如MS-COCO)，缺乏对复杂社会交互/行为的评测，且未深入分析模型错误类型。作者希望填补这一空白，系统评估VLM在复杂社会场景下的进步。

Method: 构建了包含100张复杂社会交互图像的CSB数据集；收集了4个预MLLM模型和5个MLLM模型以及20名人类描述者的场景描述；以黄金标准为基准评估准确率；分析五种视觉-认知错误类型：目标检测、识别、幻觉、场景理解和空间依赖。

Result: CSB数据集上的改进比MS-COCO更显著；预MLLM准确率远低于最差人类描述，而MLLM达到了与最优人类描述相近的准确率；MLLM消除了CSB与MS-COCO之间的准确率差距；MLLM几乎消除了所有错误类型，但空间依赖错误偶尔存在；检测、识别和幻觉错误对场景描述准确率影响最大。

Conclusion: 过去十年间VLM在复杂社会行为场景描述上取得了显著进步，MLLM已基本追赶上了人类水平，但仍需关注空间依赖等残留错误。

Original Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) have made remarkable progress in visual reasoning during the last decade. Most evaluations have used simple scenes (MS-COCO) that do not showcase complex human interactions or behaviors, only a handful of non-curated human descriptions as a benchmark, and have not focused on understanding the model's error types. Here, we introduce the Complex Social Behavior (CSB) dataset, containing 100 images depicting complex social interactions/behaviors. We analyze the progression of scene descriptions over a decade (2017-2025) of VLMs (four pre-Multimodal Large Language Models, MLLMs, and five MLLMs). We evaluate the accuracy of the models and 20 human descriptions relative to a gold standard on the CSB dataset and on a sample from MS-COCO. We analyzed five visual-cognitive error types: object detection, recognition, hallucination, scene understanding, and spatial dependence. The CSB dataset showed a more pronounced improvement than MS-COCO in scene description accuracy, with pre-MLLMs achieving much lower accuracy than the bottom-ranked human descriptions and MLLMs attaining accuracies similar to the top-ranked human descriptions. We show that MLLMs have eliminated the gap in scene description accuracy between simpler MS-COCO scenes and scenes depicting complex behaviors (CSB). MLLMs have almost eliminated all error types in our tested datasets, except for occasionally relying on different image regions for scene descriptions than humans do (spatial dependence error). We also show that detection, recognition, and hallucination errors have the highest impact on scene description accuracy. Together, our findings provide a more thorough evaluation of how visual language models have advanced over the last decade.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型(VLM)在过去十年中在视觉推理方面取得了显著进展。大多数评估使用简单场景(MS-COCO)，这些场景并未展示复杂的人类交互或行为，仅以少量非精心策划的人类描述作为基准，并且没有关注理解模型的错误类型。在此，我们引入了复杂社会行为(CSB)数据集，包含100张描绘复杂社会交互/行为的图像。我们分析了视觉语言模型(四个前多模态大语言模型(pre-MLLM)和五个多模态大语言模型(MLLM))在过去十年(2017-2025)间场景描述的进展。我们评估了模型和20名人类描述者在CSB数据集以及MS-COCO样本上相对于黄金标准的准确率。我们分析了五种视觉-认知错误类型：目标检测、识别、幻觉、场景理解和空间依赖。CSB数据集在场景描述准确率上显示出比MS-COCO更显著的改进，其中前多模态大语言模型的准确率远低于排名靠后的人类描述，而多模态大语言模型达到了与排名靠前的人类描述相似的准确率。我们发现多模态大语言模型已经消除了简单MS-COCO场景与描绘复杂行为(CSB)场景之间在场景描述准确率上的差距。多模态大语言模型几乎消除了我们测试数据集中所有类型的错误，除了偶尔会依赖与人类不同的图像区域进行场景描述(空间依赖错误)。我们还发现，检测、识别和幻觉错误对场景描述准确率的影响最大。综上，我们的研究结果提供了对视觉语言模型在过去十年间如何进步的更全面评估。

</details>


### [68] [OpenLongTail: Generative Scaling of Long-Tail Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09655)
*Lulin Liu,Nuo Chen,Yan Wang,Bangya Liu,Wenyan Cong,Hezhen Hu,Boris Ivanovic,Hao Wang,Ziyao Zeng,Xinyu Gong,Yang Zhou,Zixiang Xiong,Dilin Wang,Zhangyang Wang,Weisong Shi,Ruohan Zhang,Marco Pavone,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OpenLongTail，一个开源生成式数据引擎，通过从异构长尾视频数据中合成多视角训练数据，提升自动驾驶策略在长尾事件下的闭环驾驶鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长尾事件数据稀缺是自动驾驶策略扩展的根本瓶颈，真实世界采集的异构长尾视频缺乏完整视图覆盖（如缺少多视角位姿或仅来自单目摄像头），导致这些宝贵数据无法直接用于训练策略模型。

Method: 设计了一个位姿感知的外推式视图合成管线，将异构数据源转化为视角对齐、时序连贯的多视角数据；通过注入Plücker射线几何来增强新生成视图的跨视角一致性和时序对齐。

Result: 合成异构长尾数据后，闭环驾驶在处理长尾事件时的鲁棒性显著提升；在外推视图合成和位姿指标上验证了OpenLongTail在视觉保真度、跨视图一致性和自我轨迹恢复方面的有效性。

Conclusion: OpenLongTail能够有效利用异构长尾视频数据，通过生成多视角训练数据来提升自动驾驶策略在长尾事件下的泛化能力，为长尾泛化提供了可扩展的数据方案。

Original Abstract: Scaling robust driving policies is fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of edge cases in curated datasets. While the real world continuously captures these critical events, such long-tail events remain underutilized when collected from heterogeneous sources. Specifically, diverse but valuable in-the-wild long-tail videos lack the full view coverage required for training policy models, often missing multi-view poses or originating solely from monocular dash cameras. This modality gap prevents these ubiquitous observations from being converted into scalable training data for long-tail generalization. We introduce OpenLongTail, an open-source generative data engine for scaling autonomous driving policies under long-tail events. To transform heterogeneous data sources into view-aligned and temporally coherent multi-view assets that are useful for policy learning, we develop a pose-informed extrapolative view synthesis pipeline that generates the missing views. We further enhance cross-view consistency and the temporal alignment for the newly generated views by injecting Plücker ray geometry into the scalable generation engine. By synthesizing heterogeneous long-tail data, we observe a significant improvement in closed-loop driving robustness in handling long-tail events. By measuring the extrapolative view synthesis and pose metrics, we validate the effectiveness of OpenLongTail in visual fidelity, cross-view consistency, and ego-trajectory recovery.

中文摘要翻译: 扩展鲁棒的驾驶策略从根本上受限于 curated 数据集中边缘案例的稀缺性。虽然现实世界持续捕获这些关键事件，但此类长尾事件在从异构来源收集时仍未得到充分利用。具体而言，多样化但有价值的野外长尾视频缺乏训练策略模型所需的完整视图覆盖，通常缺少多视图位姿或仅源自单目行车记录仪。这种模态差距阻止了这些普遍存在的观测数据转化为用于长尾泛化的可扩展训练数据。我们提出了 OpenLongTail，一个用于在长尾事件下扩展自动驾驶策略的开源生成式数据引擎。为了将异构数据源转化为对策略学习有用的、视角对齐且时序连贯的多视图资产，我们开发了一个位姿感知的外推式视图合成管线，用于生成缺失的视图。我们通过将Plücker射线几何注入可扩展生成引擎，进一步增强了新生成视图的跨视图一致性和时序对齐。通过合成异构长尾数据，我们观察到在处理长尾事件时闭环驾驶鲁棒性显著提升。通过测量外推视图合成和位姿指标，我们验证了OpenLongTail在视觉保真度、跨视图一致性和自我轨迹恢复方面的有效性。

</details>


### [69] [Scalable Visual Pretraining for Language Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09657)
*Yiming Zhang,Zhonghan Zhao,Wenwei Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Tianyang Lin,Yunhua Zhou,Demin Song,Kuikun Liu,Haochen Ye,Haian Huang,Yuzhe Gu,Haijun Lv,Qipeng Guo,Bin Liu,Gaoang Wang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了语言模型必须仅基于文本训练的默认假设，提出视觉预训练（Visual Pretraining）是一种可扩展的基础模型智能学习方法。实验表明，在相同语料上，视觉预训练在多个骨干网络和基准测试中 consistently 优于纯文本预训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前的大规模基础模型预训练主要基于纯文本语料，但许多知识蕴含在视觉表示中（如图表、公式、页面布局），这些视觉信息无法被纯文本完整捕捉。现有预训练方法通过将视觉丰富的文档转换为纯文本，丢弃了这些视觉线索。

Method: 系统性地研究了无监督视觉预训练范式，直接利用视觉文档（即保留原始视觉形式的文档）进行预训练，而不进行文本提取。

Result: 在多个骨干网络和基准测试中，基于相同语料的视觉预训练 consistently 优于纯文本预训练。

Conclusion: 视觉预训练是一种可扩展的基础模型智能学习方法，为高效学习可扩展的语言智能提供了新途径。

Original Abstract: The rapid progress of large foundation models has been driven predominantly by pretraining on large-scale text corpora. However, many forms of knowledge are conveyed through visual representations, where figures, typeset equations, and page layouts carry rich information that cannot be faithfully or completely captured by text alone. Yet current pretraining approaches discard these visual cues by converting visually rich sources, such as documents and web pages, into plain text for learning language intelligence. This paper challenges the default assumption that language models must be trained on text-only representations and shows that Visual Pretraining is a scalable learner for foundation model intelligence. To this end, we conduct a systematic study of unsupervised visual pretraining paradigms that directly leverage visual documents without text extraction. Across multiple backbones and benchmarks, visual pretraining on the same underlying corpora consistently outperforms text-only pretraining, offering an efficient pathway to scalable language intelligence.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模基础模型的快速进步主要依赖于在大规模文本语料上进行预训练。然而，许多知识形式是通过视觉表示传递的，其中图表、排版公式和页面布局承载着丰富的信息，这些信息无法被纯文本忠实或完整地捕捉。然而，当前的预训练方法通过将视觉丰富的来源（如文档和网页）转换为纯文本以学习语言智能，从而丢弃了这些视觉线索。本文挑战了语言模型必须仅基于文本表示进行训练的默认假设，并展示了视觉预训练是基础模型智能的一种可扩展学习方法。为此，我们系统性地研究了无监督视觉预训练范式，该范式直接利用视觉文档而无需文本提取。在多个骨干网络和基准测试中，基于相同语料的视觉预训练 consistently 优于纯文本预训练，为高效学习可扩展的语言智能提供了一条途径。

</details>


### [70] [PanoWorld: Real-World Panoramic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09661)
*Haoyuan Li,Dizhe Zhang,Yuemei Zhou,Xiangkai Zhang,Haoran Feng,Xiaofan Lin,Wenjie Jiang,Bo Du,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PanoWorld，一种利用全景表示旋转等变性质实现长程记忆的 panoramic 世界模型，并通过DPRC和GMA机制简化相机轨迹建模，在自制数据集World360上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有全景世界模型难以处理长程记忆问题，且缺乏在大规模空间变化和多样光照条件下评估物理一致性的数据集。

Method: 利用全景表示的旋转等变性，将旋转视为隐式几何变换；提出PanoWorld，通过固定航向将相机轨迹简化为平移；引入密集全景射线条件（DPRC）和几何感知记忆增强（GMA）分别处理当前动作建模和长程记忆；设计三阶段训练流水线逐步优化各组件；构建World360数据集包含真实无人机全景视频和AirSim360模拟数据。

Result: 在World360数据集上的大量实验表明，PanoWorld以大幅优势超越对比方法。

Conclusion: PanoWorld有效利用了全景表示的旋转等变性来解决长程记忆问题，在物理一致性评估上表现优异，且模型、代码和数据集将开源。

Original Abstract: In this work, we aim to address the challenge of long-range memory in panoramic world models by exploiting the rotation-equivariant property of omnidirectional representations, where rotation can be treated as an implicit geometric transformation.Building on this insight, we propose PanoWorld, which simplifies camera trajectories into translations via fixed headings for both current-action modeling and long-range memory through Dense Panoramic Ray-Conditioning (DPRC) and Geometry-aware Memory Augmentation (GMA).Then, a three-stage training pipeline is introduced to progressively optimize each component. To better evaluate physical consistency under large-scale spatial variations and diverse illumination conditions, where existing datasets are relatively stable, we construct World360, a large-scale dataset consisting of both real-world video clips collected via panoramic unmanned aerial vehicles and high-quality simulated clips generated by AirSim360.Extensive experiments on World360 demonstrate the effectiveness of PanoWorld, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin.Our models, training code, and dataset will be publicly available. More information can be found on our project page: https://lihaoy-ux.github.io/panoworld-page/.

中文摘要翻译: 在这项工作中，我们旨在通过利用全景表示中旋转可作为隐式几何变换的旋转等变性，来解决全景世界模型中的长程记忆挑战。基于这一洞见，我们提出了PanoWorld，通过固定航向将相机轨迹简化为平移，并采用密集全景射线条件（DPRC）和几何感知记忆增强（GMA）分别进行当前动作建模和长程记忆。随后，引入三阶段训练流水线逐步优化各组件。为了在大规模空间变化和多样光照条件下更好地评估物理一致性（现有数据集在此方面相对稳定），我们构建了World360——一个大规模数据集，包含通过全景无人机采集的真实世界视频片段以及由AirSim360生成的高质量模拟片段。在World360上的大量实验证明了PanoWorld的有效性，以大幅优势超越替代方法。我们的模型、训练代码和数据集将公开提供。更多信息请访问我们的项目页面：https://lihaoy-ux.github.io/panoworld-page/。

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [Spatial Neighboring Scattering Transform: A Cross-Channel Amplitude Coupling Measure for EEG Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08855)
*Md. Taksimul Ahsan Tawhid,Nasif Ahmed Rafe,Alif Tahmid Priyom,K. M. Mustafizur Rahman*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出空间邻域散射变换（SNST），将小波散射变换扩展至多通道脑电设置，能够联合捕获通道间的幅度包络耦合及其跨频率调制。在运动想象数据集上验证发现，SNST识别出显著且跨被试一致的幅度耦合，且该耦合受慢节律周期性门控，呈现出与相位同步测度完全不同的连接信号。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有脑功能连接测度主要基于相位同步，易受容积传导伪影影响且忽略幅度域耦合，因此需要一种能够系统捕获幅度包络耦合及其跨频率调制结构的连接描述符。

Method: 将小波散射变换扩展至多通道脑电设置，提出空间邻域散射变换（SNST），通过一阶描述子捕获通道间幅度包络耦合，二阶描述子捕获该耦合的跨频率幅度调制结构。

Result: 在BCI Competition IV-2a运动想象数据集上，一阶描述子识别出中央-顶叶电极邻域内显著且跨被试一致的幅度耦合；二阶描述子揭示该耦合受慢节律周期性门控。与相位滞后指数和加权相位滞后指数对比，SNST发现的是几乎完全不同的连接信号（零重叠）。

Conclusion: SNST是一种基于跨通道散射的连接描述符，能够系统地恢复幅度包络耦合和跨频率耦合结构，适用于任何关注幅度域区域间依赖性的多通道脑电分析。

Original Abstract: The functional organization of the brain relies on coordinated activity across spatially distributed regions, making the analysis of inter-regional dependencies fundamental. Existing connectivity measures address this predominantly through phase synchronization, which is vulnerable to volume conduction artifacts and discards amplitude-domain coupling. This study introduces the Spatial Neighboring Scattering Transform, which extends the wavelet scattering transform to the multichannel setting, yielding two descriptors that jointly capture amplitude-envelope coupling between channels and its modulation across frequency scales. SNST was evaluated on the BCI Competition IV-2a motor imagery dataset using a bias-corrected, false-discovery-rate-controlled statistical pipeline, with the validation criterion defined as spatial consistency of significant coupling across subjects. The first-order descriptor identified statistically significant amplitude coupling within a central-parietal electrode neighborhood, reproduced consistently across all subjects and both imagery conditions. The second-order descriptor revealed that this coupling is periodically gated by slow rhythms, indicating a cross-frequency amplitude-modulation structure absent from single-frequency connectivity measures. Phase lag index and weighted phase lag index, computed under an identical correction procedure and verified robust to volume conduction, identified negligible significant coupling with zero overlap with SNST findings, demonstrating that amplitude envelope coupling constitutes a largely distinct connectivity signal. These results establish SNST as a cross-channel scattering-based connectivity descriptor that recovers amplitude-envelope and cross-frequency coupling structure systematically, applicable to any multichannel EEG analysis where amplitude-domain inter-regional dependence is of interest.

中文摘要翻译: 大脑的功能组织依赖于跨空间分布区域的协调活动，这使得区域间依赖性分析成为基础。现有的连接性测度主要通过相位同步来应对这一问题，而相位同步易受容积传导伪影影响，且丢弃了幅度域耦合。本研究引入了空间邻域散射变换（SNST），它将小波散射变换扩展至多通道设置，产生两个描述子，共同捕获通道间的幅度包络耦合及其跨频率尺度的调制。我们在BCI Competition IV-2a运动想象数据集上，采用偏差校正且控制错误发现率的统计流程评估SNST，验证准则定义为跨被试显著耦合的空间一致性。一阶描述子识别出中央-顶叶电极邻域内统计显著的幅度耦合，该耦合在所有被试及两种想象条件下均一致复现。二阶描述子揭示该耦合受慢节律周期性门控，表明存在单一频率连接性测度所无法捕获的跨频率幅度调制结构。在相同的校正流程下计算、经验证对容积传导稳健的相位滞后指数和加权相位滞后指数，识别出的显著耦合极少，且与SNST结果零重叠，这表明幅度包络耦合构成了一个基本独立的连接信号。这些结果确立了SNST作为一种基于跨通道散射的连接描述符，能够系统地恢复幅度包络耦合和跨频率耦合结构，适用于任何关注幅度域区域间依赖性的多通道脑电分析。

</details>


### [72] [Structural Brain Predictors of Visual Attention Gradient Modulated by Trait Anxiety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09278)
*Suhail Rafiq Mir,Dolcy Dhar,Ishita Singh,Prakrati Gupta,Varsha Singh,Mrinmoy Chakrabarty*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大脑结构形态学（小脑小叶VI灰质体积、左中央前回及旁中央小叶皮层厚度）如何预测特质焦虑对空间注意力梯度灵活性的调节作用，并利用机器学习模型验证了这些脑结构特征对注意力-焦虑关联模式的预测能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 空间注意力在视野中的动态分配（即视空间注意力梯度）对知觉广度至关重要，而特质焦虑可能干扰这种灵活分配。先前研究较少从脑结构个体差异角度预测特质焦虑如何调节空间注意力的部署，因此本研究旨在探索大脑结构形态学能否作为预测因素。

Method: 60名参与者完成包含短暂面部情绪线索的视空间注意力梯度任务；通过结构神经影像分析测量双侧小脑小叶VI灰质体积、左中央前回及旁中央小叶皮层厚度；采用机器学习模型预测个体注意力-焦虑关联模式。

Result: 离散的情绪线索未显著调节注意力梯度；双侧小脑小叶VI灰质体积更大、左中央前回和旁中央小叶皮层厚度更厚者，其空间注意力梯度幅度与特质焦虑之间的交互作用更弱；机器学习模型成功从这些神经解剖特征预测了个体注意力-焦虑特征。

Conclusion: 小脑和感觉运动区域的结构完整性更高，与低特质焦虑个体更灵活的空间注意力部署相关。这些区域超越了传统运动功能，贡献于视觉空间注意力和认知-情感交互的个体差异，且脑结构标记具有良好的预测效用。

Original Abstract: Dynamic allocation of attention across the visual field, quantified as a visuospatial attention gradient, is essential for maintaining perceptual breadth. Disruptions to this flexibility may contribute to altered spatial attentional bias and may be influenced by trait anxiety. We investigated whether individual differences in structural brain morphology predict spatial attentional deployment as a function of trait anxiety. Sixty participants, recruited based on an a priori sample size calculation, completed a visuospatial attention gradient task incorporating brief partial facial emotion cues. Although discrete emotional cues did not significantly modulate attention gradients, structural neuroimaging analyses revealed that greater grey matter volume in bilateral cerebellar lobule VI and increased cortical thickness in the left precentral gyrus and paracentral lobule were associated with reduced interaction between the magnitude of the spatial attention gradient (averaged across emotions) and trait anxiety. Machine-learning models further predicted individual attention-anxiety profiles from these neuroanatomical features. These findings suggest that greater structural integrity in cerebellar and sensorimotor regions is associated with more flexible spatial attentional deployment in individuals with lower trait anxiety. Together, the results highlight the contribution of cerebellar and sensorimotor regions, beyond their traditional motor functions, to individual differences in visual spatial attention and cognitive-affective interactions, while demonstrating the predictive utility of structural brain markers.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉场中注意力的动态分配（量化为视空间注意力梯度）对维持知觉广度至关重要。这种灵活性的破坏可能导致空间注意力偏倚的改变，并可能受到特质焦虑的影响。本研究探讨了大脑结构形态学的个体差异能否预测特质焦虑对空间注意力部署的调节作用。60名参与者（基于先验样本量计算招募）完成了一项结合短暂面部情绪线索的视空间注意力梯度任务。尽管离散的情绪线索未能显著调节注意力梯度，但结构神经影像分析显示，双侧小脑小叶VI灰质体积更大、左中央前回和旁中央小叶皮层厚度更厚的个体，其空间注意力梯度幅度（跨情绪平均）与特质焦虑之间的交互作用更弱。机器学习模型进一步从这些神经解剖特征预测了个体的注意力-焦虑关联模式。这些发现表明，小脑和感觉运动区域的结构完整性更高，与低特质焦虑个体更灵活的空间注意力部署相关。综上，结果强调了小脑和感觉运动区域（超越其传统运动功能）对视觉空间注意力和认知-情感交互个体差异的贡献，同时展示了脑结构标记的预测效用。

</details>


### [73] [PHINN-EEG: Topological Time-Series Analysis of Dream-State EEG -- Dynamic Betti Curves for Dream Content Classification and Topology-Conditioned Neural Signal Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09662)
*Ren Takahashi,Emre Yusuf,Jayabrata Bhaduri*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出PHINN-EEG，首个用于梦境分析的同调神经网络框架，通过持久同调和拓扑条件流匹配将EEG梦境检测AUC从0.70提升至0.82-0.90，并从频谱能量分析转向相空间几何分析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于EEG的梦境检测仅利用功率谱密度(PSD)和统计矩特征，AUC仅约0.70，性能受限。作者试图引入拓扑数据分析，捕获神经活动的几何结构而非仅能量信息，以提升梦境检测性能。

Method: 采用滑动窗口Takens延迟嵌入和Vietoris-Rips过滤对多通道觉醒前EEG时段提取动态Betti曲线作为拓扑不变量；结合拓扑条件流匹配(Topology-Conditioned Flow Matching)进行梦境状态检测；引入拓扑条件整流流模型用于梦境状态EEG合成，并以频谱条件流模型作为消融基线。

Result: 在DREAM数据库1,462次觉醒子集上，目标AUC达0.82-0.90，显著超越现有PSD和catch22基准（AUC≈0.70）。同时提出候选Betti过渡原型，将拓扑特征与现象学梦境报告类别关联。

Conclusion: 这项工作代表了从频谱能量分析到相空间几何分析的范式转变，若经实证验证，将为神经罕见事件检测带来变革性影响，并对未来可穿戴BCI梦境监测有潜在应用价值。

Original Abstract: Current electroencephalography (EEG)-based dream detection relies on power spectral density (PSD) and statistical moment features, achieving a state-of-the-art area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of approximately 0.70 on the DREAM database (Wong et al., 2025, Nature Communications). We introduce PHINN-EEG (Persistent Homology Inspired Neural Network for EEG), the first topological time-series framework for dream mentation analysis. Using sliding-window Takens delay embeddings and Vietoris-Rips filtrations on multichannel pre-awakening EEG epochs, we extract Dynamic Betti Curves that characterize the geometric architecture of neural activity, not merely its energy. These topological invariants, combined with topology-conditioned flow matching, are analytically projected to outperform existing PSD and catch22 benchmarks, targeting AUC = 0.82-0.90 on the 1,462-awakening open-access subset of the DREAM database (drawn from a full registry of 3,191 total awakenings from 263 participants across 20 independent laboratories). We further introduce a topology-conditioned rectified flow model for dream-state EEG synthesis-with a spectral-conditioned flow model of comparable feature dimensionality as an additional ablation baseline to isolate the value of topological conditioning specifically-and propose a set of candidate Betti transition archetypes linking topology to phenomenological dream report categories, presented as an exploratory hypothesis space pending empirical validation. If validated, this work represents a paradigm shift from spectral energy to phase-space geometry in neural rare-event detection, with potential future implications for wearable BCI dream monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 当前基于脑电图（EEG）的梦境检测依赖于功率谱密度（PSD）和统计矩特征，在DREAM数据库上（Wong等人，2025，《自然·通讯》）的受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUC）约达0.70。我们提出PHINN-EEG（持久同调启发式神经网络），这是首个用于梦境心理分析的拓扑时间序列框架。通过对多通道觉醒前EEG时段采用滑动窗口Takens延迟嵌入和Vietoris-Rips过滤，我们提取了动态Betti曲线，这些曲线刻画了神经活动的几何结构而不仅仅是其能量。将这些拓扑不变量与拓扑条件流匹配相结合，经分析预测，其在DREAM数据库1,462次觉醒的开放访问子集（来自20个独立实验室、263名参与者共3,191次觉醒的完整注册数据）上的性能将超越现有PSD和catch22基准，目标AUC为0.82-0.90。我们进一步引入了一个拓扑条件整流流模型用于梦境状态EEG合成——以同等特征维度的频谱条件流模型作为额外的消融基线，以单独隔离拓扑条件的价值——并提出了一组候选Betti过渡原型，将拓扑结构与现象学梦境报告类别联系起来，作为待实证验证的探索性假设空间。若经验证，这项工作代表了从频谱能量到相空间几何在神经罕见事件检测中的范式转变，对未来可穿戴BCI梦境监测具有潜在应用意义。

</details>

### [74] [Beyond Metadata: CAPRA for Hidden Subgroup Analysis under Missing Metadata in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09102)
*Yawen Li,Yan Li,Zhe Xue,Yingxia Shao,Meiyu Liang,Guanhua Ye*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: CAPRA提出了一种校准代理轴框架，在元数据缺失的情况下，通过预测图像语义轴并校准后验概率，实现对隐藏子群的发现、部署时失败分析以及下游鲁棒学习复用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学影像模型部署时往往缺少人口统计、采集和质量等元数据，导致子群审计无法进行，严重失败模式被强聚合性能掩盖，且许多鲁棒学习方法失去依赖的群体结构。

Method: CAPRA首先预测图像导出的语义轴，然后通过患者级交叉拟合在少量带元数据标签的样本上校准轴后验概率，最后将这些后验组织成校准子群接口，支持部署时失败分析和下游鲁棒学习，无需部署时提供子群标签。

Result: 在眼底、皮肤镜和胸部X光影像上，CAPRA能发现被纯元数据切片遗漏的差异模式，在数据集偏移下保持信息量，产生的子群划分比纯图像或潜在切片基线更贴近显式失败轴；下游鲁棒学习复用收益具有领域依赖性。

Conclusion: CAPRA将元数据缺失下的隐藏子群分析转化为一个校准、可解释且可复用的子群接口，适用于部署时分析和鲁棒迁移。

Original Abstract: Medical imaging models are often deployed without the demographic, acquisition, and quality metadata needed for subgroup auditing. Once those metadata disappear, clinically critical failure modes can be masked by strong aggregate performance, and many robust-learning methods lose the group structure they rely on. We present CAPRA, a calibrated proxy-axis framework for hidden subgroup analysis under missing metadata. CAPRA predicts image-derived semantic axes, calibrates axis posteriors on a small metadata-labeled split via patient-level cross-fitting, and organizes those posteriors into a calibrated subgroup interface that supports both deployment-time failure analysis and downstream robust learning without requiring subgroup labels at deployment. Across fundus, dermoscopy, and chest radiography, CAPRA reveals disparity patterns missed by metadata-only slicing, remains informative under dataset shift, and produces subgroup partitions that align more closely with explicit failure axes than image-only or latent-slice baselines. The same interface can also be reused by downstream robust learners, although those gains are domain-dependent. Overall, CAPRA turns hidden subgroup analysis under missing metadata into a calibrated, interpretable, and reusable subgroup interface for deployment-time analysis and robust transfer.

中文摘要翻译: 医学影像模型在部署时通常缺少用于子群审计的人口统计、采集和质量元数据。一旦这些元数据消失，临床关键失败模式可能被强聚合性能所掩盖，许多鲁棒学习方法也会失去它们所依赖的群体结构。我们提出CAPRA，一个用于元数据缺失下隐藏子群分析的校准代理轴框架。CAPRA预测图像导出的语义轴，通过患者级交叉拟合在少量带元数据标签的样本上校准轴后验概率，并将这些后验组织成一个校准子群接口，该接口既支持部署时的失败分析，也支持下游鲁棒学习，而无需在部署时提供子群标签。在眼底、皮肤镜和胸部X光影像上，CAPRA能发现纯元数据切片所遗漏的差异模式，在数据集偏移下仍保持信息量，并且产生的子群划分比纯图像或潜在切片基线更贴近显式失败轴。同一接口也可被下游鲁棒学习器复用，尽管这些收益具有领域依赖性。总体而言，CAPRA将元数据缺失下的隐藏子群分析转化为一个校准、可解释且可复用的子群接口，用于部署时分析和鲁棒迁移。

</details>


### [75] [Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Solar PV Panel Fault Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09205)
*Seyyedhamid Azimidokht,Mehdi Monemi,Abdelhak Kharbouch,Farid Hamzehaghdam,Mehdi Rasti,Jamshid Aghaei,Emil Kurvinen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出JEFFNet，一种结合JEPA自监督表示学习与EfficientNetV2-S监督卷积特征提取的多分支架构，用于热红外光伏故障分类。在PVF-10和ISM两个公开数据集上，该方法在多分类和二分类任务中均取得优异性能，同时参数量较GEPFNet减少47.2%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 光伏系统快速扩张使得人工巡检不可行，亟需可靠且可扩展的故障分类方法。热红外成像虽能非接触识别光伏故障，但存在类别不平衡、纹理信息有限及热差异细微等问题，导致准确分类仍具挑战。

Method: 提出JEFFNet多分支架构：一个分支使用JEPA预训练的Vision Transformer提取语义表示，另一分支使用EfficientNetV2-S提取监督卷积特征，通过融合两者实现互补特征学习。

Result: 在PVF-10上，10类任务F1-score 93.21%、准确率94.33%；二分类任务F1-score 97.53%、准确率96.41%。在ISM上，12类任务F1-score 72.60%、准确率83.88%；二分类任务F1-score 94.69%、准确率94.78%。参数量仅108.6M，较GEPFNet的205.91M减少47.2%。

Conclusion: 自监督语义特征与监督卷积特征的融合为热红外光伏故障分类提供了一种有效且参数高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: The rapid expansion of solar photovoltaic (PV) systems has increased the need for reliable and scalable fault classification, as manual inspection is impractical at scale. Thermal infrared (IR) imaging provides a non-contact solution for identifying PV faults; however, accurate classification remains challenging due to class imbalance, limited texture information, and subtle thermal differences. In this work, we investigate the applicability of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) for thermal IR PV fault classification across various scenarios and propose JEFFNet (JEPA-EFFicientNet), a multibranch architecture that combines JEPA-based self-supervised representation learning with EfficientNetV2-S-based supervised convolutional feature extraction. JEFFNet fuses semantic representations from a JEPA-pretrained Vision Transformer with convolutional features from EfficientNetV2-S, enabling complementary feature learning. JEFFNet is evaluated on two public thermal IR datasets, PVF-10 and InfraredSolarModules (ISM), for both multiclass and derived binary (healthy/faulty) classification. On PVF-10, JEFFNet achieves an F1-score of $93.21$ and an accuracy of $94.33$ in the 10-class task, and an F1-score of $97.53$ and an accuracy of $96.41$ in the derived 2-class task. On ISM, JEFFNet achieves an F1-score of $72.60$ and an accuracy of $83.88$ in the 12-class task, and an F1-score of $94.69$ and an accuracy of $94.78$ in the derived 2-class task. JEFFNet also uses only 108.6M parameters versus 205.91M for GEPFNet, a 47.2\% reduction. These results demonstrate that combining self-supervised semantic and supervised convolutional features provides an effective, parameter-efficient solution for thermal IR PV fault classification. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Azimi2kht/JEFFNet

中文摘要翻译: 太阳能光伏（PV）系统的快速扩张增加了对可靠且可扩展的故障分类的需求，因为人工巡检在大规模情况下不可行。热红外（IR）成像提供了一种非接触式解决方案来识别光伏故障；然而，由于类别不平衡、有限的纹理信息以及细微的热差异，准确分类仍然具有挑战性。在本文中，我们研究了联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在各种场景下用于热红外光伏故障分类的适用性，并提出了JEFFNet（JEPA-EFFicientNet），一种多分支架构，它结合了基于JEPA的自监督表示学习与基于EfficientNetV2-S的监督卷积特征提取。JEFFNet将JEPA预训练的Vision Transformer的语义表示与EfficientNetV2-S的卷积特征进行融合，实现了互补特征学习。JEFFNet在两个公开的热红外数据集PVF-10和InfraredSolarModules（ISM）上进行了多分类和派生二分类（健康/故障）评估。在PVF-10上，JEFFNet在10类任务中实现了93.21的F1分数和94.33的准确率，在派生二分类任务中实现了97.53的F1分数和96.41的准确率。在ISM上，JEFFNet在12类任务中实现了72.60的F1分数和83.88的准确率，在派生二分类任务中实现了94.69的F1分数和94.78的准确率。JEFFNet仅使用108.6M参数，而GEPFNet为205.91M，减少了47.2%。这些结果表明，结合自监督语义特征和监督卷积特征为热红外光伏故障分类提供了一种有效且参数高效的解决方案。源代码公开于https://github.com/Azimi2kht/JEFFNet。

</details>

### [76] [Matched Generators for the Karhunen--Loève Transform: A Double-Commutator Eigenvalue Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08788)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究KLT与群对称性的逆问题：给定协方差函数，寻找与其近似交换的生成元，通过双换位子特征值问题最小化交换性损失，从而恢复或发现隐藏的经典变换，并给出了对称性近似时的编码代价阈值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: KLT对协方差矩阵对角化时，其本征函数在对称性条件下退化为经典变换（如傅里叶、余弦等）。本文研究其逆问题：给定协方差，如何找到与其（近似）交换的生成元，从而发现隐藏在数据中的变换结构。

Method: 定义交换性损失δ(A,R)=‖[R,A]‖_F/(‖R‖_F‖A‖_F)，将其最小化转化为双换位子特征值问题ad_R²(A*)=λA*，这是一个与数据维度无关的Hermitian广义特征值问题。通过变分刻画将交换生成元的存在性转化为谱条件，并利用三对角换位子唯一性结果恢复经典变换。

Result: 框架能恢复经典变换（如椭球波函数、余弦、离散正交多项式基）及其矩阵扩展，产生介于经典族之间的连续变换谱。对称性近似时，对称自适应分块变换的编码代价等于扇区间的多信息量，给出了固定变换与数据驱动变换之间的精确阈值。还给出了置换结构的图自同构刻画、非阿贝尔对称性的顺序缩并以及估计误差下的稳定性界。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架统一了经典变换的发现与恢复问题，为数据驱动与固定变换之间的权衡提供了理论界限，并在双范式协方差的KLT合成中展示了无需形成混合协方差即可达到全数据变换压缩性能的应用。

Original Abstract: The Karhunen--Loève transform (KLT) diagonalizes the covariance of a second-order process and is optimal for mean-square truncation. Which classical transform it reduces to is governed by the symmetry commutant of the covariance: when the kernel commutes with a group action, the KLT eigenfunctions are the irreducible representation functions of that group, recovering the Fourier, cosine, Mellin, and spherical-harmonic systems. We study the inverse question. Given a covariance $R$ and a finite-dimensional space of candidate generators, the generator nearest to commuting with $R$, the minimizer of $δ(A,R)=\|[R,A]\|_F/(\|R\|_F\|A\|_F)$, is the smallest-eigenvalue solution of a double-commutator eigenvalue problem $\mathrm{ad}_R^2(A^\ast)=λA^\ast$, a Hermitian generalized eigenvalue problem of size the number of generators, independent of dimension. The framework recovers hidden transforms as well as classical ones: a variational characterization turns the existence of a commuting generator into a spectral condition, and a tridiagonal commutant-uniqueness result yields the prolate spheroidal, cosine, and discrete orthogonal-polynomial bases as exact recoveries, with matrix-valued extensions, and produces a continuum of transforms interpolating between and beyond the classical families. When symmetry is approximate, the coding penalty of the symmetry-adapted blockwise transform equals the multi-information among the sectors, an exact threshold between the fixed and data-driven transforms. We further give a graph-automorphism characterization of permutation structure, a sequential deflation for non-Abelian symmetry, and stability bounds under estimation error. As an application, the KLT of a two-paradigm covariance is synthesized from its two known generators, without forming the mixed covariance, reaching the full-data transform's compaction from few observations.

中文摘要翻译: Karhunen–Loève变换（KLT）对二阶过程的协方差进行对角化，并在均方截断意义下最优。它退化为哪种经典变换取决于协方差的对称交换子：当核函数与群作用交换时，KLT本征函数即为该群的不可约表示函数，从而恢复傅里叶、余弦、Mellin和球谐函数系统。本文研究逆问题：给定协方差R和一个有限维候选生成元空间，与R最接近交换的生成元——即最小化δ(A,R)=‖[R,A]‖_F/(‖R‖_F‖A‖_F)者——是双换位子特征值问题ad_R²(A*)=λA*的最小特征值解，这是一个大小为生成元个数、与维度无关的Hermitian广义特征值问题。该框架既能恢复经典变换也能发现隐藏变换：变分刻画将交换生成元的存在性转化为谱条件，三对角交换子唯一性结果将椭球波函数、余弦和离散正交多项式基作为精确恢复，并给出矩阵值扩展，同时产生介于经典族之间及之外的连续变换谱。当对称性近似时，对称自适应分块变换的编码代价等于扇区间的多信息量，这是固定变换与数据驱动变换之间的精确阈值。本文进一步给出了置换结构的图自同构刻画、非阿贝尔对称性的顺序缩并方法以及估计误差下的稳定性界。作为应用，双范式协方差的KLT可由其两个已知生成元合成，无需形成混合协方差，即可从少量观测达到全数据变换的压缩性能。

</details>


### [77] [A 2.4 GHz LC-VCO Fractional-N Phase Locked Loop Open-Source Design in 130-nm BiCMOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08852)
*Manimohan Thiriloganathan,Shenal Ranasinghe,Avishka Herath,Rajinthan Rameshkumar,Hansa Marasinghe,Anjana Viduranga,Gayangana Leelarathne,Kithmin Wickremasinghe*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文在开源CMOS设计生态中，利用OpenEMS电磁建模工作流设计了定制螺旋电感，成功实现了一款2.4 GHz分数-N频率综合器PLL，验证了开源工具链用于射频集成电路设计的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 开源CMOS生态中缺乏可靠的被动器件模型（尤其是片上螺旋电感），导致先前工作只能使用环形振荡器VCO，相位噪声性能较差。因此需要开发基于电感的LC-VCO以提升性能。

Method: 采用IHP SG13G2 130 nm BiCMOS开源工艺，通过OpenEMS开源电磁建模工作流设计定制螺旋电感，并基于该电感实现交叉耦合差分LC-VCO，最终构建完整的2.4 GHz type-II分数-N频率综合器PLL。

Result: 优化后的电感在2.45 GHz下电感值为4 nH，品质因数16.8；LC-VCO灵敏度约120 MHz/V；PLL在1 MHz偏移处相位噪声为-100.8 dBc/Hz；芯片面积0.619 mm²，功耗12.73 mW。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在完全开源的CMOS IC设计生态中进行射频集成电路设计的可行性与有效性。

Original Abstract: Radio frequency (RF) integrated circuit design using the open-source complementary Metal-Oxide semiconductor (CMOS) ecosystem, such as for phase-locked loops (PLLs), is limited by the absence of reliable passive device models, particularly on-chip spiral inductors. Consequently, prior work relies on ring-oscillator-based voltage-controlled oscillators (VCOs) with degraded phase noise performance. This work presents a 2.4 GHz type-II fractional-N PLL implemented in the IHP SG13G2 130 nm BiCMOS open-source technology. The proposed design employs a cross-coupled differential LC-VCO integrated with a custom-designed spiral inductor, developed using an open-source electromagnetic modelling workflow in OpenEMS. The optimized inductor achieves 4 nH inductance with a quality factor of 16.8 at 2.45 GHz. The LC-VCO sensitivity is approximately 120 MHz/V, while the PLL phase noise is -100.8 dBc/Hz at 1 MHz offset. The complete PLL is realized using a fully open-source electronic design automation (EDA) flow, occupying a total area of 930 um x 666 um (~0.619 mm2) and consuming 12.73 mW, demonstrating the feasibility of RF integrated circuit design in an open-source CMOS IC design ecosystem.

中文摘要翻译: 利用开源互补金属氧化物半导体（CMOS）生态系统进行射频（RF）集成电路设计（例如锁相环（PLL））受到缺乏可靠无源器件模型（尤其是片上螺旋电感）的限制。因此，先前的工作依赖于环形振荡器压控振荡器（VCO），其相位噪声性能较差。本文介绍了采用IHP SG13G2 130 nm BiCMOS开源工艺实现的2.4 GHz type-II分数-N频率综合器PLL。所提出的设计采用交叉耦合差分LC-VCO，集成了通过OpenEMS开源电磁建模工作流开发的定制螺旋电感。优化后的电感在2.45 GHz下实现4 nH电感量和16.8的品质因数。LC-VCO灵敏度约为120 MHz/V，PLL在1 MHz偏移处的相位噪声为-100.8 dBc/Hz。完整的PLL采用全开源电子设计自动化（EDA）流程实现，占用总面积为930 μm × 666 μm（约0.619 mm²），功耗为12.73 mW，证明了在开源CMOS IC设计生态中进行射频集成电路设计的可行性。

</details>


### [78] [Fast Pinching-Antenna Activation for AirComp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08873)
*Zhenqiao Cheng,Boqun Zhao,Chongjun Ouyang,Xingqi Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究用于空中计算（AirComp）的可重构天线系统（PASS），提出在有限预配置位置约束下，通过贪心搜索、束搜索和相干聚合搜索等方法优化离散激活问题，以最小化均方误差（MSE），数值结果表明所提方法相比传统天线阵列显著提升了AirComp精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可重构天线系统（PASS）用于空中计算时，实际部署中每个天线只能从有限个预配置位置中选择激活，由此产生的离散激活问题尚未得到有效解决，需要设计高效的选择算法以最小化空中计算的均方误差。

Method: 首先推导最优聚合向量，将最小MSE表示为逆Gram矩阵形式；然后导出秩一递归公式以精确评估每个候选位置带来的MSE缩减；据此开发贪心搜索和束搜索用于快速树搜索；进一步提出基于一阶MSE近似的相干聚合搜索，该搜索具有可分离的闭式选择规则，且在低信噪比下渐近最优。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的贪心搜索、束搜索和相干聚合搜索等方法相比传统天线阵列，显著提高了PASS系统的空中计算精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的离散激活优化方法有效解决了PASS在有限预配置位置约束下的AirComp精度问题，其中相干聚合搜索在低信噪比下具有渐近最优性，为可重构天线在空中计算中的实际部署提供了理论依据和高效算法。

Original Abstract: A pinching-antenna system (PASS) is considered for over-the-air computation (AirComp). Multiple dielectric waveguides are deployed at the base station, and one pinching antenna (PA) is activated on each waveguide. For practical implementation, each PA is restricted to a finite set of preconfigured locations. The resulting discrete activation problem is formulated to minimize the AirComp mean-squared error (MSE). After the optimal aggregation vector is derived, the minimum MSE is expressed through an inverse Gram matrix. A rank-one recursion is derived to evaluate the exact MSE reduction produced by each candidate. Greedy search and beam search are then developed for fast tree search. To further reduce complexity, coherent aggregation search is proposed from a first-order MSE approximation. It admits a separable closed-form selection rule and is asymptotically optimal in the low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime. Numerical results show that the proposed methods substantially improve the AirComp accuracy of PASS over conventional antenna arrays.

中文摘要翻译: 本文考虑将可重构天线系统（PASS）用于空中计算（AirComp）。基站在多个介质波导上部署天线，每个波导上激活一个可重构天线（PA）。在实际部署中，每个PA被限制在一组有限的预配置位置中。由此构成的离散激活问题被建模为最小化空中计算的均方误差（MSE）。在推导出最优聚合向量后，最小MSE通过逆Gram矩阵表示。进一步导出秩一递归公式，用于精确评估每个候选位置带来的MSE缩减。基于此，开发了贪心搜索和束搜索以实现快速树搜索。为降低复杂度，从一阶MSE近似出发提出了相干聚合搜索，该方法具有可分离的闭式选择规则，且在低信噪比（SNR）区域渐近最优。数值结果表明，所提出的方法相比传统天线阵列显著提升了PASS系统的空中计算精度。

</details>


### [79] [Every Sample Counts: Supervised Fine-Tuning of Language Models with Pointwise Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08968)
*Ignacio Hounie,Ignacio Boero,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的对齐框架，通过对每个样本施加独立约束并引入可学习的松弛机制，在保持下游性能的同时减少尾部约束违规。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的约束对齐方法仅在平均意义上施加约束，会导致不同输入或用户之间出现不期望的差异（即尾部约束违规严重），因此需要一种能在单样本级别强制执行约束的方法。

Method: 提出一种每样本约束对齐框架：1）在最小化平均损失的同时对每个样本独立施加约束；2）引入可学习的样本依赖松弛机制，以用户定义的松弛成本权衡约束的严格性与训练目标；3）采用定制化的增广拉格朗日方法解决对偶性和优化挑战。

Result: 在指令遵循（安全性）、函数调用（偏好）和重排序（长度）三类小语言模型微调任务上，该方法在保持模型性能的同时显著减少了尾部约束违规。

Conclusion: 所提出的每样本约束对齐框架能够有效缓解跨样本的约束差异问题，在保证整体性能的前提下提升约束公平性，具有广泛的适用性。

Original Abstract: Fine-tuning language models often requires enforcing constraints on individual inputs without compromising downstream performance. Existing constrained alignment methods impose constraints on average, which can induce undesirable disparities across inputs or users. We propose a novel alignment framework that addresses this gap by enforcing per-sample constraints while still minimizing an average loss. To mitigate the impact of overly restrictive constraints and outliers, we introduce a learned, sample-dependent relaxation that minimally adjusts the constraints, trading off a user-defined relaxation cost with the training objective. To address practical duality and optimization challenges, we develop an augmented Lagrangian approach tailored to this formulation. We demonstrate the flexibility of the framework by instantiating it under distinct small language-model fine-tuning tasks and constraints: safety in instruction following, preferences in function calling and length in re-ranking. Across these settings, our approach reduces tail constraint violations while largely preserving the model's performance.

中文摘要翻译: 微调语言模型通常需要在不妨碍下游性能的前提下对每个输入施加约束。现有的约束对齐方法仅在平均意义上施加约束，这可能导致不同输入或用户之间出现不期望的差异。我们提出了一种新颖的对齐框架，通过强制执行每样本约束同时最小化平均损失来解决这一问题。为了缓解过度严格的约束和异常值的影响，我们引入了一种可学习的、样本依赖的松弛机制，该机制以用户定义的松弛成本与训练目标进行权衡，对约束进行最小程度的调整。为了解决实际中的对偶性和优化挑战，我们开发了一种针对该公式定制的增广拉格朗日方法。我们通过在三种不同的小语言模型微调任务和约束下实例化该框架来展示其灵活性：指令遵循中的安全性、函数调用中的偏好以及重排序中的长度约束。在这些设置中，我们的方法在基本保持模型性能的同时减少了尾部约束违规。

</details>


### [80] [Inter-frame Channel Prediction for Zak-OTFS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09184)
*Muhammad Ubadah,Saif Khan Mohammed*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明Zak-OTFS调制可以实现帧间信道预测，利用有效DD域信道滤波器系数在时频上的确定性变化和子空间不变性，提出了一种确定性ESPIRIT方法，能够基于训练帧的信道估计预测未来数十帧的信道响应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Zak-OTFS调制虽能通过帧内信道估计应对高移动性场景中的多普勒扩展，但帧间信道预测是否可能仍是一个重要的开放问题。本文旨在回答这一问题，即能否基于某一帧的Zak-OTFS载波信道响应，准确预测另一帧（非同一帧）中的信道响应。

Method: 本文提出了一种确定性ESPIRIT型方法。该方法利用有效DD域信道滤波器系数在从当前帧到未来帧时频移动时的确定性变化规律，以及连续帧信道滤波器系数所张成的子空间在时频离散移位下的不变性，通过训练帧（当前/过去帧）中估计的信道抽头/系数来预测未来数十帧及频域上相隔数十帧的信道滤波器。

Result: 理论分析表明：1) 有效DD域信道滤波器系数在时频维度的帧间变化是确定性的；2) 时频上连续帧的信道滤波器系数子空间对离散时频移位具有不变性。基于这些性质，所提方法能够预测未来数十帧和频域上相隔数十帧的信道响应。

Conclusion: 本文证实了Zak-OTFS调制中帧间信道预测是可行的，为高移动性场景下的信道预测提供了理论依据和有效方法。

Original Abstract: Zak-Orthogonal Time Frequency Space (OTFS) modulation is known to be robust to Doppler spread in high mobility scenarios when compared to Orthogonal Frequency Division Multiplexing (OFDM). This is due to the fact that the channel response to a Zak-OTFS carrier within a frame can be accurately estimated from the channel response to another carrier within the same frame. However, an important open problem and question is whether inter-frame channel prediction is possible with Zak-OTFS, i.e., is it possible to accurately predict the channel response to a Zak-OTFS carrier in a frame based on knowledge of the channel response to some Zak-OTFS carrier in \emph{another} frame (i.e., not the same frame).
  In this paper we show that indeed inter-frame channel prediction is possible. We show that the effective DD domain channel filter coefficients vary in a deterministic manner as we move from current to future frames in time and frequency. We also show that the subspace spanned by channel filter coefficients of consecutive frames in time/frequency is invariant to discrete shifts in time and frequency. We exploit the deterministic variation and subspace invariance to propose a novel deterministic ESPIRIT-type method which uses the effective DD domain channel filter taps/coefficients estimated in training frames (i.e., current/past frames in time and frequency having both pilot and data carriers) to predict the effective DD domain channel filter for frames which are several tens of frames in future and several tens of frames away in frequency.

中文摘要翻译: 与正交频分复用(OFDM)相比，Zak正交时频空(OTFS)调制在高移动性场景下对多普勒扩展具有鲁棒性。这是因为帧内一个Zak-OTFS载波的信道响应可以通过同一帧内另一个载波的信道响应进行准确估计。然而，一个重要且开放的问题在于：Zak-OTFS是否能够实现帧间信道预测，即能否基于某一帧中Zak-OTFS载波的信道响应知识，准确预测另一帧（而非同一帧）中Zak-OTFS载波的信道响应。本文证明帧间信道预测确实是可行的。我们揭示了有效DD域信道滤波器系数在从当前帧向未来帧的时频移动中呈现确定性变化规律。我们还证明了时频上连续帧的信道滤波器系数所张成的子空间对时频离散移位具有不变性。利用这种确定性变化和子空间不变性，我们提出了一种新颖的确定性ESPIRIT型方法，该方法利用训练帧（即当前/过去帧，同时包含导频和数据载波）中估计的有效DD域信道滤波器抽头/系数，来预测未来数十帧以及频域上相隔数十帧的有效DD域信道滤波器。

</details>


### [81] [Multi-Domain Iterative Detection for Massive Connectivity in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09212)
*Xinhua Liu,Yueqing Wang,Keke Ying,Peihu Duan,Dapeng Li,Ziwei Wan,Zhongliang Zhao,Chabalala S. Chabalala,Andrey Ivanov,Zhen Gao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于低轨卫星互联网免授权随机接入的多域协同稀疏信号处理方案，包含迭代残差反馈多测量矢量近似消息传递算法（用于活跃用户检测和信道估计）以及联合空频多域扩频数据调制方案，显著提升了大规模连接场景下的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有免授权随机接入方案在大规模连接场景下性能较差，且当活跃用户终端空间距离近或数量超过卫星接收天线时，传统数据检测方法因秩亏或欠定问题而性能严重下降。

Method: 1) 提出迭代残差反馈多测量矢量近似消息传递算法，利用空频域和角延迟域的多域协同稀疏性交替进行活跃用户检测和信道估计，并引入残差反馈机制抑制误差累积；2) 设计联合空频多域扩频数据调制方案，利用空域和频域观测实现多域数据检测。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案在活跃用户检测精度、信道估计精度和误码率方面显著优于现有免授权方法，尤其在低有效导频长度和实际信噪比条件下优势明显。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多域协同稀疏处理框架有效解决了低轨卫星互联网大规模连接场景下免授权随机接入的性能瓶颈问题。

Original Abstract: Grant-Free (GF) random access is promising for low Earth orbit satellite Internet due to its reduced access latency. However, existing schemes suffer from poor performance in massive connectivity scenarios. To address this challenge, we firstly propose an iterative residual feedback multi-measurement vector approximate message passing algorithm. This algorithm leverages multi-domain synergistic sparsity in the spatial-frequency and angular-delay domains to alternately perform active user terminal detection (AUD) and channel estimation (CE). Additionally, a residual feedback mechanism is incorporated to suppress error accumulation, thereby enhancing AUD performance. Furthermore, conventional data detection (DD) methods significantly degrade when active user terminals are spatially close or outnumber the satellite's receive antennas, making the demodulation problem rank-deficient or underdetermined. To mitigate this, we design a data modulation scheme via joint spatial-frequency multi-domain spreading, which utilizes observations from both spatial and frequency domains to facilitate multi-domain DD. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly outperforms existing GF methods in terms of AUD accuracy, CE precision, and bit error rate, especially under conditions of low effective pilot length and practical signal-to-noise ratios.

中文摘要翻译: 免授权（GF）随机接入因其低接入延迟而在低轨卫星互联网中具有广阔前景。然而，现有方案在大规模连接场景下性能较差。为解决这一挑战，我们首先提出了一种迭代残差反馈多测量矢量近似消息传递算法。该算法利用空频域和角延迟域中的多域协同稀疏性，交替执行活跃用户终端检测（AUD）和信道估计（CE）。此外，引入残差反馈机制以抑制误差累积，从而提升AUD性能。进一步地，当活跃用户终端空间距离接近或数量超过卫星接收天线时，传统数据检测（DD）方法性能显著下降，导致解调问题出现秩亏或欠定。为缓解该问题，我们设计了一种通过联合空频多域扩频的数据调制方案，利用来自空域和频域的观测以促进多域DD。仿真结果表明，所提方案在AUD精度、CE精度和误码率方面显著优于现有GF方法，尤其在低有效导频长度和实际信噪比条件下表现突出。

</details>


### [82] [Tensor-based Random Access for Ambient IoT Contention Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09331)
*Alberto Rech,Alexis Decurninge,Ala Baccar,Sofiane Kharbech*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量的免授权随机接入方案，专门针对环境物联网（A-IoT）中低阶调制和简单收发机架构的硬件约束设计。通过将设备短载荷构造为秩-1张量并在多个接入时机发送副本，接收端利用张量分解和连续干扰消除实现联合活动检测、信道估计与解码，在保持发射机低复杂度的同时显著提升了吞吐量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 环境物联网（A-IoT）部署面临严格的硬件约束，包括低阶调制和简单的收发机架构，传统随机接入方案难以在此类限制下高效工作，因此需要一种适用于低复杂度发射机设计的免授权随机接入方案。

Method: 提出基于张量的免授权随机接入方案：每个设备将其短载荷构造为秩-1张量，并在多个接入时机发送副本；接收端通过张量分解与连续干扰消除（SIC）联合进行设备活动检测、信道估计和信号解码。

Result: 与传统方案相比，该方法在保持发射机低复杂度的同时，实现了显著的吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: 基于张量的免授权随机接入方案能够有效适配A-IoT的硬件约束，在保持发射端简单设计的前提下大幅提升系统吞吐量，为未来大规模A-IoT部署提供了可行的接入方案。

Original Abstract: Ambient Internet of Things (A-IoT) deployments impose stringent hardware constraints, including low-order modulations and simple transceiver architectures. We propose a tensor-based grant-free random access scheme for the initial access message (Msg1) tailored to these limitations. Each device transmits replicas of its short payload structured as a rank-1 tensor over multiple access occasions. The A-IoT receiver performs joint activity detection, channel estimation, and decoding via tensor decomposition and successive interference cancellation. Results show significant throughput gains over conventional schemes while preserving low-complexity transmitter designs.

中文摘要翻译: 环境物联网（Ambient Internet of Things, A-IoT）部署面临着严格的硬件约束，包括低阶调制和简单的收发机架构。本文提出了一种基于张量的免授权随机接入方案，专门针对初始接入消息（Msg1）进行设计，以适应上述限制。每个设备将其短载荷构造为秩-1张量，并在多个接入时机发送副本。A-IoT接收端通过张量分解和连续干扰消除，联合完成设备活动检测、信道估计与信号解码。实验结果表明，与现有方案相比，本方法在保持发射机低复杂度设计的同时，实现了显著的吞吐量增益。

</details>


### [83] [Site Geometry and Calibration Uncertainties in Digital Twin-enabled Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09334)
*Lorenzo Del Moro,Francesco Linsalata,Umberto Spagnolini,Maurizio Magarini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于数字孪生（DT）的快速光线追踪（RT）辅助信道估计的鲁棒性，重点分析了用户定位误差和电磁材料校准误差对信道协方差矩阵低秩本征结构的影响，推导了一阶扰动模型，发现定位误差是性能下降的主因，而电磁校准误差影响有限。数值结果表明在低信噪比城市环境下，DT方法相比基线方法可获得高达10dB的NMSE提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数字孪生技术能为环境感知通信提供站点特定的先验信息，但数字模型的不准确性（如用户定位误差和电磁材料校准误差）会影响基于光线追踪提取的多径特征所构建的信道协方差矩阵的低秩本征结构，从而影响信道估计性能。本文旨在系统分析这些模型失配的影响，并评估DT辅助信道估计的鲁棒性。

Method: 通过光线追踪提取多径特征，构造信道协方差矩阵的低秩本征结构用于信道估计。针对用户定位误差（导致站点表示的几何不一致）和电磁材料校准误差（影响路径增益），推导了一阶扰动模型，将几何扰动（影响角度和时延）与电磁扰动（影响路径增益）分离，并基于该扰动模型进行归一化均方误差（NMSE）分析。

Result: 分析表明，低秩估计本质上对电磁校准扰动具有鲁棒性，而定位误差通过改变信道本征结构主导性能退化。城市、郊区和乡村场景的数值结果证实，定位误差是主要的性能限制因素，电磁校准误差影响相对有限。在低信噪比城市环境下，DT方法相比基线方法可获得高达10dB的NMSE提升；在高信噪比且定位误差适中（<1m）时，性能与基线方法相当。

Conclusion: 数字孪生辅助的信道估计对电磁材料校准误差具有内在鲁棒性，但用户定位误差是性能退化的主导因素。尽管如此，在低信噪比条件下，DT方法仍能显著提升信道估计性能，证实了DT在环境感知通信中的应用价值。

Original Abstract: Fast ray tracing (RT) has stimulated the Digital Twin (DT) as an emerging technology for environment-aware communications. Since wireless propagation is governed by the interaction between site geometry and electromagnetic (EM) properties of the environment, DT-based approaches can provide site-specific prior information for channel estimation. In this work, we investigate the robustness of DT to aid the channel estimation, where multipath features extracted via RT are used to construct the low-rank (LR) eigenstructure of the channel covariance matrix. This LR structure is used in channel estimation. However, the digital representation of propagation model is inaccurate and thus it affects the LR. We explicitly analyze these model mismatches that arise from user positioning errors, which translate into geometric inconsistencies in the site representation, and EM material calibration errors. We derive a first-order perturbative model that separates geometric perturbations, affecting angles and delays, from EM perturbations, affecting path gains. Based on this perturbed model, we provide a normalized mean-square error (NMSE) analysis that reveals a fundamental difference between geometric and EM perturbations. In particular, we show that LR estimation is inherently robust to EM calibration perturbations, while positioning errors, dominate performance degradation by altering the channel eigenstructure. Numerical results confirm that, in urban, suburban and rural scenarios, positioning errors are the primary limiting factor, whereas EM calibration errors have a comparatively limited impact. Despite these mismatches, DT-empowered estimators provide up to 10dB NMSE improvement, over baseline methods, in the urban low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) settings, while achieving performance comparable to baseline estimators at high SNR for moderate (< 1 m) positioning errors.

中文摘要翻译: 快速光线追踪技术推动了数字孪生作为一种新兴技术用于环境感知通信。由于无线传播受场地几何形状与环境电磁特性之间相互作用的支配，基于数字孪生的方法可为信道估计提供站点特定的先验信息。本文研究了数字孪生辅助信道估计的鲁棒性，其中通过光线追踪提取的多径特征被用于构建信道协方差矩阵的低秩本征结构，该低秩结构被用于信道估计。然而，传播模型的数字表示存在不准确性，从而影响低秩结构。我们明确分析了由用户定位误差（转化为站点表示的几何不一致）和电磁材料校准误差引起的这些模型失配。我们推导了一阶扰动模型，将影响角度和时延的几何扰动与影响路径增益的电磁扰动分离开来。基于该扰动模型，我们进行了归一化均方误差分析，揭示了几何扰动与电磁扰动之间的根本差异。特别地，我们表明低秩估计本质上对电磁校准扰动具有鲁棒性，而定位误差通过改变信道本征结构主导了性能退化。数值结果证实，在城市、郊区和乡村场景中，定位误差是主要的限制因素，而电磁校准误差的影响相对有限。尽管存在这些失配，在低信噪比城市环境下，数字孪生赋能估计器相比基线方法可获得高达10dB的NMSE提升，而在高信噪比且定位误差适中（小于1米）时，其性能与基线方法相当。

</details>


### [84] [Drone-Based Antenna Measurement System with Optimized Positioning and ASPIRE-Based NF-FF Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09361)
*Simranjit Singh,Abha Nilesh Jadav,Aarish Dharmesh Patel,Jaswant,Jigar M. Pandya*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于无人机的天线测量系统，重点通过系统化无人机组件选型提升定位精度和飞行续航，并采用自适应稀疏逆辐射估计（ASPIRE）算法处理近场数据，在6.7125 GHz下实现残差1.94%、波束宽度误差0.4度，仅用24%的RWG网格活跃支撑，显著提升了远场方向图重建精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于无人机的天线测量系统虽灵活且成本低，但其精度严重依赖无人机精确定位和有效飞行时间利用，而这两者又受无人机组件（包括机身、飞控、推进系统、定位模块和机载仪器）选型的强烈影响，因此需要系统研究如何通过优化组件选型来提升测量性能。

Method: 首先通过系统化无人机组件选型优化定位精度和飞行续航；然后采用自适应稀疏逆辐射估计（ASPIRE）算法处理近场测量数据，该算法利用稀疏信号恢复技术补偿定位误差，从不规则采样的近场数据中重建远场方向图，实现精确的近远场变换。

Result: 在6.7125 GHz频率下，ASPIRE算法相对于传统设施测量达到1.94%的残差和0.4度的波束宽度误差，且仅使用17,298单元RWG网格中24%的活跃支撑。

Conclusion: 优化无人机组件选型与ASPIRE近远场变换相结合，显著提高了基于无人机的天线测量精度，生成的远场方向图与传统天线测试场测量结果高度一致。

Original Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV)-based antenna measurement systems provide a flexible and cost-effective alternative to conventional antenna test ranges for characterizing large and installed antennas. However, their accuracy depends on precise UAV positioning and efficient flight-time utilization, both of which are strongly influenced by the selection of drone assemblies, including the airframe, flight controller, propulsion system, positioning modules, and onboard instrumentation. This paper presents a comprehensive study of UAV-based antenna measurements with emphasis on improving positioning accuracy and optimizing flight endurance through systematic drone assembly selection. The acquired near-field measurement data are susceptible to positioning errors, amplitude and phase inconsistencies, and irregular sampling, which degrade the reconstructed far-field pattern. To address these challenges, the recorded near-field data are processed using the Adaptive Sparse Inverse Radiation Estimation (ASPIRE) algorithm. ASPIRE compensates for positioning inaccuracies and reconstructs the far-field pattern from irregularly sampled near-field data using sparse signal recovery, enabling accurate Near-Field to Far-Field (NF-FF) transformation. At 6.7125 GHz, ASPIRE achieves a residual of 1.94% and a beamwidth error of 0.4 degrees relative to a conventional facility measurement while using only 24% of the 17,298-element RWG mesh as active support. The results demonstrate that the combination of optimized drone assembly selection and ASPIRE-based NF-FF transformation significantly improves the accuracy of UAV-based antenna measurements and produces far-field patterns that closely agree with conventional antenna test range measurements.

中文摘要翻译: 基于无人机的天线测量系统为表征大型和已安装天线提供了一种灵活且经济高效的传统天线测试场替代方案。然而，其精度取决于无人机精确定位和高效的飞行时间利用，这两者都受到无人机组件（包括机身、飞控、推进系统、定位模块和机载仪器）选型的强烈影响。本文对基于无人机的天线测量进行了全面研究，重点是通过系统化无人机组件选型来提高定位精度和优化飞行续航。采集到的近场测量数据易受定位误差、幅度和相位不一致以及不规则采样的影响，这些都会降低重建的远场方向图质量。为解决这些挑战，记录到的近场数据采用自适应稀疏逆辐射估计（ASPIRE）算法进行处理。ASPIRE利用稀疏信号恢复补偿定位不准确性，并从不规则采样的近场数据中重建远场方向图，从而实现精确的近远场（NF-FF）变换。在6.7125 GHz频率下，ASPIRE相对于传统设施测量达到了1.94%的残差和0.4度的波束宽度误差，同时仅使用17,298单元RWG网格中24%的活跃支撑。结果表明，优化无人机组件选型与基于ASPIRE的近远场变换相结合，显著提高了基于无人机的天线测量精度，生成的远场方向图与传统天线测试场测量结果高度一致。

</details>


### [85] [On LLR Calculations for Soft-decision Decoding in Next-generation IM-DD Systems with Laser RIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09433)
*Felipe Villenas,Yunus Can Gültekin,Alex Alvarado*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对强度调制直接检测（IM-DD）系统中相对强度噪声（RIN）的低复杂度对数似然比（LLR）近似方法，用于软判决（SD）译码，在级联KP4+Hamming前向纠错（FEC）方案中无BER性能损失，且优于基于失配AWGN的LLR。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在IM-DD系统中，相对强度噪声（RIN）会显著影响译码性能，而传统的基于失配加性高斯白噪声（AWGN）假设的LLR计算方法在该场景下性能不佳，因此需要一种既能降低复杂度又能保持良好译码性能的LLR近似方法。

Method: 提出了一种低复杂度的LLR近似方法，专门针对IM-DD系统中的相对强度噪声进行优化设计，用于软判决译码。

Result: 该近似方法在级联KP4+Hamming前向纠错方案中实现了与理想LLR相同的误码率（BER）性能，且优于基于失配AWGN假设的LLR方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的低复杂度LLR近似方法能够在存在相对强度噪声的IM-DD系统中有效工作，在降低计算复杂度的同时不引入BER性能损失，优于传统的失配AWGN方法。

Original Abstract: We propose a low-complexity LLR approximation for SD decoding in IM-DD systems with relative intensity noise. Our approximation results in no BER performance penalty for a concatenated KP4+Hamming FEC scheme, and outperforms mismatched AWGN-based LLRs.

中文摘要翻译: 我们针对存在相对强度噪声的强度调制直接检测（IM-DD）系统中的软判决（SD）译码，提出了一种低复杂度的对数似然比（LLR）近似方法。该近似方法在级联KP4+Hamming前向纠错（FEC）方案中不会造成误码率（BER）性能损失，并且优于基于失配加性高斯白噪声（AWGN）的LLR方法。

</details>


### [86] [OAM-Enabled Holographic MIMO Communications with Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09479)
*Giulia Torcolacci,Davide Dardari*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 论文研究了基于堆叠智能超表面（SIM）在辐射近场中实现轨道角动量（OAM）全息多输入多输出（HMIMO）链路。通过多层可编程超表面实现模拟电磁域波处理，分析了OAM模式生成与接收，提出了相关性驱动的优化算法，数值评估表明合理设计的SIM架构能提供鲁棒的近场空间复用和近乎均衡的每模容量，且无需连续相位重优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索利用堆叠智能超表面（SIM）在辐射近场中实现基于轨道角动量（OAM）的全息多输入多输出（HMIMO）通信，以解决传统HMIMO架构中复杂度高、能耗大的问题，并实现灵活、低开销的波前合成与空间复用。

Method: 采用多层可编程超表面（SIM）在链路两端实现模拟电磁域波处理；分析SIM收发器的OAM模式生成与接收能力；量化离散HMIMO架构中近正交模式的合成能力；提出相关性驱动的优化算法以最大化OAM波束的重建精度。

Result: 天线孔径与支持的模式阶数存在基本解耦关系，而SIM层深度主要控制串扰抑制；合理尺寸的SIM架构能提供鲁棒的近场空间复用、近乎均衡的每模容量，并在不同链路距离下保持性能平稳退化，无需连续相位重优化。

Conclusion: 合理设计的SIM架构能够支持可扩展、低开销的HMIMO通信，为近场OAM空间复用提供了一种低复杂度、高能效的可行方案。

Original Abstract: This study investigates orbital angular momentum (OAM)-based holographic multiple-input multiple-output (HMIMO) links enabled by stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIM) in the radiative near-field. By using multilayer programmable metasurfaces at both link ends, SIMs enable analog electromagnetic domain wave processing for low-complexity and energy-efficient flexible wavefront synthesis. We analyzed OAM mode generation and reception with SIM-based transceivers and quantified their ability to synthesize near-orthogonal modes in practical discrete HMIMO architectures. We further developed a correlation-driven optimization algorithm that maximizes reconstruction accuracy of OAM beams. Numerical evaluations revealed a fundamental decoupling between the required antenna aperture, which limits the supported mode orders, and the SIM layer depth, which governs crosstalk suppression. The results confirm that properly dimensioned SIM architectures provide robust near-field spatial multiplexing, nearly balanced per-mode capacities, and graceful degradation across link distances without requiring continuous phase re-optimization, thereby supporting scalable and low-overhead HMIMO communications.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究研究了由堆叠智能超表面（SIM）在辐射近场中实现的基于轨道角动量（OAM）的全息多输入多输出（HMIMO）链路。通过使用链路两端的多层可编程超表面，SIM能够实现模拟电磁域波处理，从而进行低复杂度、高能效的灵活波前合成。我们分析了基于SIM收发器的OAM模式生成与接收，并量化了它们在实用离散HMIMO架构中合成近正交模式的能力。我们进一步开发了一种相关性驱动的优化算法，该算法能最大化OAM波束的重建精度。数值评估揭示了所需天线孔径（其限制支持的模式阶数）与SIM层深度（其控制串扰抑制）之间存在基本解耦关系。结果证实，合理尺寸的SIM架构能够提供鲁棒的近场空间复用、近乎均衡的每模容量，并在不同链路距离下实现性能的平稳退化，而无需连续相位重优化，从而支持可扩展、低开销的HMIMO通信。

</details>


### [87] [Fused Constrained Policy Reuse Optimization for Wireless Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09498)
*An Liu,Zheyuan Zhou,Kexuan Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出Fused-CPRO方法，通过融合可学习目标策略、源策略和领域知识策略，在约束马尔可夫决策过程下联合优化，解决无线资源分配中在线探索成本高、收敛慢的问题，并证明了几乎必然收敛到KKT点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度强化学习在无线资源分配中虽具无模型自适应优势，但在线探索成本高——随机初始化的策略在收集足够数据前可能违反长期约束。未来无线系统需应对动态流量、波动信道和严苛能效要求，亟需能快速学习、与环境交互最少化的算法以降低能耗和信令开销。

Method: Fused-CPRO将分配策略构建为可学习目标策略、相关场景源策略和专家规则领域知识策略的混合体，在约束马尔可夫决策过程下联合优化目标策略和复用概率。采用约束随机逐次凸逼近处理非凸目标和约束，并通过混合离线和在线数据训练的评论家提高样本效率。

Result: 在时延约束MU-MIMO功率控制和CRB约束MIMO-ISAC波束赋形仿真中，Fused-CPRO比代表性基线方法具有更优的实证性能且收敛速度显著更快。

Conclusion: 融合异构先验知识（源策略和领域知识策略）能加速收敛并增强鲁棒性，所提方法理论上保证几乎必然收敛到KKT点，实验验证了其在复杂无线资源分配任务中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has been widely adopted for wireless resource allocation due to its model-free adaptability. However, online exploration is costly, as randomly initialized policies may violate long-term constraints before sufficient data are collected. Future wireless systems must cope with increasingly dynamic traffic, fluctuating channel conditions, and stringent energy efficiency requirements, demanding algorithms that can learn quickly with minimal environment interactions to reduce both energy consumption and signaling overhead. We develop Fused-CPRO, a knowledge-fused constrained policy reuse optimization method addressing these challenges. Fused-CPRO constructs the allocation policy as a mixture of a learnable target policy, source policies from related scenarios, and domain-knowledge (DK) policies from expert rules, jointly optimizing the target policy and reuse probabilities under a constrained Markov decision process (CMDP). This fusion of heterogeneous priors accelerates convergence and enhances robustness. Constrained stochastic successive convex approximation (CSSCA) handles non-convex objectives and constraints, while a critic trained from mixed offline-online data improves sample efficiency by reusing pre-collected experience. We prove almost-sure convergence to a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) point. Simulations on delay-constrained multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MU-MIMO) power control and Cramer-Rao bound (CRB)-constrained multiple-input multiple-output integrated sensing and communication (MIMO-ISAC) beamforming demonstrate that Fused-CPRO improves empirical performance and converges substantially faster than representative baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 深度强化学习因其无模型自适应性而被广泛用于无线资源分配。然而，在线探索成本高昂，因为随机初始化的策略在收集足够数据之前可能违反长期约束。未来的无线系统必须应对日益动态的流量、波动的信道条件和严苛的能效要求，这要求算法能够以最少的环境交互快速学习，以降低能耗和信令开销。我们提出了Fused-CPRO——一种知识融合的约束策略复用优化方法，以应对这些挑战。Fused-CPRO将分配策略构建为可学习目标策略、来自相关场景的源策略以及来自专家规则的领域知识策略的混合，在约束马尔可夫决策过程下联合优化目标策略和复用概率。这种异构先验的融合加速了收敛并增强了鲁棒性。约束随机逐次凸逼近方法处理非凸目标和约束，而由混合离线和在线数据训练的评论家通过复用预收集的经验提高了样本效率。我们证明了该方法几乎必然收敛到卡鲁什-库恩-塔克点。在时延约束多用户多输入多输出功率控制和克拉美罗界约束多输入多输出联合感知与通信波束赋形上的仿真表明，Fused-CPRO比代表性基线方法具有更优的实证性能且收敛速度显著更快。

</details>


### [88] [Symbol-Level Precoding for Continuous-Aperture ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09610)
*Hongli Liu,Qiang Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对连续孔径阵列（CAPA）使能的通感一体化（ISAC）下行系统，提出了一种符号级预编码与接收极化合并框架，通过将无限维非凸优化问题精确降维为有限维系数优化，并设计惩罚投影梯度算法，实现了比传统傅里叶基CAPA和离散阵列更高的感知效用和通信可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 连续孔径阵列（CAPA）为通感一体化（ISAC）提供了丰富的电磁自由度，但优化连续电流分布面临着无限维优化难题。现有方法难以直接高效地求解此类问题，因此需要一种结构保持的降维方法来实现CAPA-ISAC系统的高性能联合优化。

Method: 首先建立符号级预编码与接收极化合并的CAPA-ISAC下行系统模型；将发射电流分布优化建模为最大化加权目标照射功率同时满足通信用户建设性干扰约束的无限维非凸问题；证明最优电流分布位于通信与感知电磁响应张成的有限维子空间中，从而将问题精确转化为有限维系数优化问题；最后设计惩罚投影梯度算法联合优化电流系数与极化合并器。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架在感知效用和通信可靠性方面均优于传统傅里叶基CAPA和空间离散阵列基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了CAPA-ISAC系统中最优电流分布的可降维性，并提出了有效的联合优化算法，为连续孔径阵列在通感一体化中的应用提供了理论依据和实用方案。

Original Abstract: Continuous-aperture arrays (CAPAs) offer rich electromagnetic degrees of freedom for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC), but optimizing continuous current distributions leads to challenging infinite-dimensional problems. This paper investigates a CAPA-enabled downlink ISAC system with symbol-level precoding and receive polarization combining. The transmit current is optimized to maximize weighted target-illumination power while enforcing constructive-interference constraints for communication users. To address the resulting infinite-dimensional nonconvex problem, we establish that the optimal current distribution lies in a finite-dimensional subspace spanned by the communication and sensing electromagnetic responses. This result yields an exact, structure-preserving reformulation in terms of finite-dimensional coefficients. A penalty projected-gradient algorithm is then developed to jointly optimize the current coefficients and polarization combiners. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves higher sensing utility and improved communication reliability than conventional Fourier-basis CAPA and spatially discrete array baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 连续孔径阵列（CAPA）为通感一体化（ISAC）提供了丰富的电磁自由度，但优化连续电流分布会带来具有挑战性的无限维问题。本文研究了一种基于符号级预编码和接收极化合并的CAPA使能下行ISAC系统。发射电流被优化以最大化加权目标照射功率，同时为通信用户强制执行建设性干扰约束。为解决由此产生的无限维非凸问题，我们证明最优电流分布位于由通信和感知电磁响应张成的有限维子空间中。这一结果导出了一个关于有限维系数的精确的、结构保持的重构形式。进而开发了一种惩罚投影梯度算法来联合优化电流系数和极化合并器。仿真结果表明，与传统的傅里叶基CAPA和空间离散阵列基线相比，所提出的框架实现了更高的感知效用和更优的通信可靠性。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [A Unified Approach to Interpreting Knowledge Distillation for Large Language Models via Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08776)
*Qingzhuo Wang,Ruiyang Qin,Zhenxin Qin,Wen Shen,Zhihua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架，通过交互分解揭示知识蒸馏的通用机制是交互稀疏化，并据此提出复杂交互惩罚（CIP）损失函数以提升蒸馏性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管知识蒸馏（KD）在大语言模型中取得了成功，但其有效性的底层机制尚不明确，作者希望探索不同KD方法的共同机制。

Method: 将LLM的输出分数分解为多个交互项之和（每个交互表示一组输入变量间的非线性关系），发现KD的通用机制是交互稀疏化；基于此提出复杂交互惩罚（CIP）损失函数，在蒸馏过程中显式强制复杂交互的稀疏性。

Result: 不同KD方法的共性机制是交互稀疏化（学生模型保留更少交互进行推理）；性能差异源于处理复杂交互的能力；引入CIP后，在域内和分布外基准上一致提升了多种KD方法的性能。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏的核心机制是交互稀疏化，复杂交互的稀疏程度决定了蒸馏性能；CIP作为即插即用的损失函数能有效提升各种KD方法的表现。

Original Abstract: Despite the success of knowledge distillation (KD) in Large Language Models (LLMs), the underlying mechanism behind its efficacy remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a unified approach to explore the common mechanism of various KD methods using interactions. Specifically, we decompose the output score of the LLM into the sum of numerous interactions. Each interaction represents a nonlinear relationship involving a set of input variables (e.g., words).
